This report originally recorded four source-backed candidates; a cross-PR reconciliation in group 48 discovered one overlapping finding in parallel PR #4054. No runtime changes. `ai/langgraph/scripts/run_agent_gate.ps1` was **not run** and remains mandatory for subsequent auth/DB/realtime or frontend-backend contract repair. ## Group 47 ledger

| Candidate | Priority | Scope | Defect mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NOTIFYPAGEFLAG-BE-001` | P2 conditional | backend cursor page metadata | On a final full page, `has_more` remains true because `total` ignores cursor |
| `NOTIFYMARKSCOPE-FE-001` | P2 conditional | optimistic mark-all-as-read | Local update ignores department filter and includes null-role items |
| `NOTIFYSYNCBADGE-FE-001` | P2 conditional | replay/counter contract | WS `notification_sync_response` clears authoritative role counters to `{}` |
| `NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001` | P2 | inbox action | The "Прочитать" button also navigates via the Open handler |

> **Cross-report reconciliation (13 November):** NOTIFYPAGEFLAG-BE-001 is the same root cause as NOTIFYCURSORLAST-BE-001 in [parallel PR #4054, group 50](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4054). Keep its code evidence for provenance, but **do not add it again** to the combined distinct-defect count.

### NOTIFYPAGEFLAG-BE-001 — P2 conditional: incorrect cursor `has_more` on the last full page

**Source.** [`RestMixin.get_inbox`, lines 134–190](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L134-L190) reads `deliveries = list_deliveries(... cursor=cursor, limit=limit)`, then independently reads `total = count_deliveries(...)` **without a cursor** and sets `has_more = total > len(items)` when `len(items) == limit`. [`NotificationPlatformRepository.list_deliveries`, lines 140–152](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L140-L152) applies `sequence_id < cursor` only to the page query; `count_deliveries` has no cursor parameter.

**Consequence.** With 100 eligible deliveries and `limit=50`, the first page correctly says `has_more=true`; the second page returns the final 50 but still calculates `100 > 50` and advertises a third page, which returns empty. This occurs for non-first **full** final pages; partial final pages are handled differently. The public response contract for `GET /notifications/inbox`, `/history`, `/sync` and the WS sync response inherits this flag. It is **not** `NOTIFYSEQ-BE-001` (group 42 concurrent sequence collisions) nor `NOTIFYHISTORYPAGE-FE-001` (group 44 UI never requesting further pages).

**Synthetic RED test to implement.** Insert 50, 100 and 101 synthetic deliveries for one authorized user with unique ordered sequence IDs, then paginate `limit=50` through both REST and WS surfaces. For 100 assert page 2: 50 items, `has_more=false`, `next_cursor=null`; for 101 assert page 3: 1 item and terminal; for 50 assert page 1 terminal. Ensure filtered searches and role scopes do not introduce gaps/duplicates. Use cursor-aware lookahead (e.g. limit+1) or count of remaining rows, not the unfiltered global total.

### NOTIFYMARKSCOPE-FE-001 — P2 conditional: optimistic bulk read updates unrelated departments

**Source.** [`NotificationCenterContext.markAllAsRead`, lines 879–917](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L879-L917) accepts `role` and `departmentKey`. The optimistic `setInbox` maps all items and skips only when `role && item.role && item.role !== normalizeRole(role)`. It **never checks `item.departmentKey`** and lets `item.role === null` through. The REST request immediately below sends **both** `role` and `department_key`. The backend [`NotificationPlatformRepository.mark_all_read`, lines 311–344](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L311-L344) filters recipient, role **and department**. A success only triggers `refreshUnreadCounts` on the frontend, not an immediate per-item inbox reconciliation.

**Consequence.** If a caller invokes `markAllAsRead("doctor", "cardiology")`, a `dermatology` delivery with `role=doctor` is locally rendered read even when the backend correctly leaves it unread. Role-unset items are also treated as within scope. The current [NotificationInbox action](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L239-L251) sends only the role (no department), so the department-specific failure is **conditional on a scoped consumer**; avoid claiming an observed user incident. This is different from group 42 `NOTIFYROLLBACK-FE-001` (unsuccessful optimistic mutation); here the request **succeeds for a subset**.

**Synthetic RED test to implement.** Mount the provider with two doctor inbox rows from distinct department keys, plus a role-null row. Mock a successful backend mark-all for cardiology only. Verify exactly the matching row becomes read in local state, all other rows remain unchanged and server counts remain authoritative. Cover no-department, different-role, archived, and same-account cases.

### NOTIFYSYNCBADGE-FE-001 — P2 conditional: WS replay drops existing authoritative role counts

**Source.** [`RestMixin.get_inbox`, return keys near lines 175–191](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L175-L191) supplies `items`, `total`, `unread_count`, `next_cursor`, `has_more`, `cursor`, `role` and `status`, **not** `by_role`/`by_channel`/`by_severity`. [`notification_websocket.py`, sync response](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L189-L218) forwards that payload. [`NotificationWebSocketContext`, lines 226–245](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L226-L245) nevertheless builds a snapshot with `by_role: data.by_role || {}` (similarly for channel/severity) and calls `replaceNotifications`. [`NotificationCenterContext.replaceNotifications`, lines 612–622](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L612-L622) applies this incomplete snapshot with `applyUnreadSnapshot(..., true)`, **overwriting** previous REST group breakdown with empty maps. [`getUnreadCount(role)`, lines 932–949](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L932-L949) falls back to counting locally cached inbox rows when that role key is missing; [`HeaderNew` bell](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/layout/HeaderNew.tsx#L1-L1) uses role-specific counts.

**Consequence.** Example: server has 75 unread doctor deliveries and an accurate REST snapshot `{total:75,by_role:{doctor:75}}`; a reconnect replay returns the first 50 rows, `unread_count:75` but no `by_role`. The client replaces role counts with `{}`; the doctor badge can show only the 50 cached records even though server total is 75. This is an **incomplete replay payload + destructive merge** issue, separate from `NOTIFYACK-FE-001` (group 41 mutation ACKs and stale item state) and from `NOTIFYHISTORYPAGE-FE-001` (group 44 older rows unavailable via UI).

**Synthetic RED test to implement.** Seed 75 synthetic same-role unread server rows, a previous authoritative REST role map, and a WS replay with first 50 rows and no `by_role`. Assert that the role badge remains 75 and total remains 75; guard against account switches and older in-flight replies. Require an authoritative grouped snapshot or a safe follow-up refresh rather than manufacturing `{}` as a server-provided zero count.

### NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001 — P2: "Прочитать" also opens the destination page

**Source.** [`NotificationInbox.handleOpen`, lines 217–239](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L217-L239) first invokes `markAsSeen`/`markAsRead` and then resolves and navigates to the target using `navigateToNotificationTarget`. The separately rendered button **labelled `Прочитать`** [lines 488–512](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L488-L512) calls `void handleOpen(item)` too. The notification body button explicitly invokes this Open handler, but the "read" action has different user-facing semantics from "open".

**Consequence.** A user trying simply to mark a notification read is taken to its `deepLink` (or inferred route) and leaves the inbox if a target exists. This is not group 44 `NOTIFYOPENBLOCK-FE-001`: that issue covers an **intended Open** navigation blocked by a failed read mutation; this one covers **unwanted navigation on a read-only control**. The existing guarded navigation helper is still essential for actual Open actions and must not be bypassed.

**Synthetic RED test to implement.** Render one synthetic notification with a safe app-relative target. Click the "Прочитать" button: expect exactly one read mutation, **zero navigation attempts**, and the inbox remaining open. Click the notification body: expect the existing guarded Open flow (including lab dirty-leave behavior). Also test mutation error and absent link. Preserve accessibility and control labels.

## Continued source Code Review — group 48: WS cursor parsing, Redis listener robustness and connection lifecycle

**Review date:** 2026-11-13, Asia/Tashkent. **Main reference:** [eaab3b9365c0a1](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2). Reviewed current files: personal WS endpoint blob 5ff188ba187552c9db25ae7853379e6e9c7dd127, notification React provider 2f76184eb4843544a6332b3e67f07b4f24fbc0a8, shared Redis bridge dd97dfb0d4f82ea0ccb6dc5747b48fd1d01c8d94 and personal manager b27ce06c96e853fb967a237f5cefc483180d841f. Previous groups 40–47 and parallel #4054 groups 47–52 checked for duplication. **All four below are source-observed candidates; none is a confirmed production incident.**

| Finding | Priority | Mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYWSCURSOR-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Invalid cursor silently resets WS sync to the first page |
| NOTIFYREDISHOL-BE-001 | P2 conditional | One blocked notification socket stalls unrelated Redis channels on the same worker |
| NOTIFYREDISENVELOPE-BE-001 | P2 conditional | JSON array/scalar envelope crashes the Redis listener |
| NOTIFYROUTECHURN-FE-001 | P2 conditional | Protected-to-protected SPA navigation closes and recreates a healthy personal WebSocket |

### NOTIFYWSCURSOR-BE-001 — invalid WS cursor silently treated as first page

**Exact source:** [notification_websocket.py lines 168–181](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L168-L181) coerces the supplied sync cursor with Python int and **catches TypeError/ValueError by assigning None**, then [passes that value to get_inbox](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L201-L218). Thus an authenticated command with a malformed cursor such as "not-an-int" returns a fresh first page without validation error; Boolean true also converts to 1. The corresponding [REST sync API accepts a typed integer cursor](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L573-L654), with FastAPI validation for invalid strings.

**Scope and distinction:** This is a client-protocol corruption issue during pagination, not a recipient authorization bypass. Different from group-41 NOTIFYFRAME-BE-001 (non-object top-level frames / sync limit), group-42 NOTIFYSEQ-BE-001 (sequence collision) and the cursor end-of-pages report duplicated with #4054.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** authenticate a synthetic user; send a sync command with malformed string cursor and then Boolean cursor; expect a bounded error identifying the cursor and *no* unintended first-page query, while the socket remains usable. Follow with a valid integer cursor to prove recovery. Establish valid lower bound and whether null is allowed before implementation.

### NOTIFYREDISHOL-BE-001 — one slow user blocks the entire notification Redis subscriber loop

**Exact source:** [RedisPubSubBridge._listen_loop lines 208–246](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L208-L246) processes Redis messages in one async-for loop and **awaits each handler inline** before reading the next event. The [personal manager's per-user receiver](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L69-L87) awaits _send_json_local, which [awaits WebSocket.send_json without deadline](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L150-L167). A backpressured recipient A can therefore stall dispatch to **different recipient B** on the same worker's notification bridge, notwithstanding separate user channel names.

**Scope and distinction:** The impact is conditional on a blocked send, not proven under real load. Group-41 NOTIFYFANOUT-BE-001 concerns sequential *same-user* fanout and publisher ordering; this concerns the shared Redis listener for *different users*. Persistence is outbox-owned and is not claimed lost.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** fake Redis receives events for synthetic users A and B. Hold A's send unresolved; assert B's event is dispatched within a bounded time independently, without releasing A first. Verify same-recipient message ordering, safe timeout/cancellation, no unbounded detached task accumulation, and last-socket cleanup.

### NOTIFYREDISENVELOPE-BE-001 — valid non-object Redis JSON kills the listener

**Exact source:** [RedisPubSubBridge._listen_loop lines 221–245](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L221-L245) catches invalid json.loads syntax, then immediately calls envelope.get("source") and envelope.get("payload") **without checking that the parsed value is a dictionary**. A valid JSON array, null, number or string from a noncanonical producer raises an AttributeError and falls into the [outer crash/recovery block](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L244-L270), rather than dropping one bad frame.

**Scope and distinction:** Owned bridge publishers [create dictionary envelopes](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L53-L67); malformed input depends on a faulty/trusted external publisher or transport corruption, **not established attacker access**. The older NOTIFYFRAME-BE-001 concerns WebSocket *client* payloads in another owner.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** fake pubsub first yields a matching-channel JSON array, then a valid canonical envelope. Assert the malformed event is ignored, the listener stays alive (no recovery/reset), and the valid event reaches its handler. Repeat null and string; verify no notification payload enters error logs.

### NOTIFYROUTECHURN-FE-001 — protected page transitions unnecessarily disconnect notification WebSocket

**Exact source:** [NotificationWebSocketProvider route effect lines 396–437](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L396-L437) depends on location.pathname, even though its policy is based on whether a route is public. Cleanup calls disconnectRef.current and closeSocketSafely on every pathname change, including a move **between two protected staff pages with unchanged user/token**. [closeSocketSafely actively closes an OPEN socket](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L145-L163); the next effect [schedules a new connection](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L304-L328). This repeats authentication, unread-count DB reads and a fresh 50-item replay after ordinary navigation.

**Impact / distinction:** Extra websocket handshakes and a transient disconnect window, conditional on typical multi-page navigation; no real dropped notifications were measured. Distinct from group-40 permanent-denial retry and group-41 cross-principal isolation. Before changing, verify whether per-route rotation is deliberate and preserve logout/public-route/identity changes.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** with MemoryRouter and fake WebSocket, establish a socket on a synthetic protected doctor page, navigate to a different protected doctor page, assert no close, no second socket, no duplicate replay. Move to login and assert clean close; change account/token and assert mandatory security replacement. Include CONNECTING and stale onclose races.

## Continued source Code Review — group 49: Redis subscribe race, scoped unread totals, archived UI and redundant mutations

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13 (Asia/Tashkent). Latest observed main SHA: [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2) (unchanged since group 48). This group was checked against merged #4049 groups 40–44, merged #4052 groups 45–46, this report's groups 47–48, concurrent #4054 groups 47–52, and the historical DISPLAYRELAY-BE-003 first-SUBSCRIBE fix. **Four source-observed P2 candidates** are documented; none is a verified production incident.

| Candidate | Severity | Owner | Mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYREDISJOINRACE-BE-001 | P2 conditional / limited active-path evidence | RedisPubSubBridge.subscribe | Two different handlers join one channel during a failing first SUBSCRIBE; one gets false success without an actual subscription |
| NOTIFYUNREADSCOPE-BE-001 | P2 conditional | REST unread-count contract | Scoped total and unscoped by-role/channel/severity maps describe different populations |
| NOTIFYALLARCHIVE-FE-001 | P2 | NotificationInbox filter | Archived entry remains in "Все" immediately after successful optimistic archive, despite server "all" excluding it |
| NOTIFYSEENREAD2X-FE-001 | P2 | NotificationInbox Open workflow | Opening an unseen unread delivery causes two successful REST mutations, two commits and two broadcasts when one read mutation suffices |

### NOTIFYREDISJOINRACE-BE-001 — concurrent different handlers can leave an unregistered Redis channel

**Source:** [RedisPubSubBridge.subscribe, lines 117–164](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L117-L164). The bridge adds each handler to the mutable set before awaiting Redis SUBSCRIBE. Only the call with was_added=true and len(handlers)==1 actually subscribes. An overlapping registration of *a distinct handler* for the same logical channel sees len==2 and returns true without awaiting a Redis subscription. If the first in-flight redis-py SUBSCRIBE then raises, its failure handler removes only that first handler. The second handler remains registered locally, and its caller has already received true although no server-side SUBSCRIBE succeeded.

**Minimal interleaving:** A adds handler_A, starts and awaits SUBSCRIBE; B adds distinct handler_B on the same logical channel, observes set length 2, returns true; A's pending SUBSCRIBE raises, removes A; the bridge retains B without an active Redis subscription. The previously repaired DISPLAYRELAY-BE-003 scenario was **single-handler first-attempt failure followed by retry**; this is a **separate join-while-in-flight failure race** not resolved by the current was_added rollback. No specific active production caller registering two distinct handlers for a notification-user channel was confirmed: the personal manager caches one handler per user, so mark this **conditional/latent for multi-handler shared-bridge users**, not an observed notification outage. This is also distinct from group-48 NOTIFYREDISHOL-BE-001 (the shared listener blocked on an already-delivered handler).

**Synthetic RED test, not executed:** instantiate the real bridge with two distinct handler closures and a fake redis-py PubSub whose first subscribe blocks on an asyncio.Event then raises. Start A, start B while A is pending, release the failure, assert the bridge does **not** report success for B without real Redis subscription; either retry safely or roll back B. Then publish one synthetic remote frame and assert B receives it. Cover one-handler normal SUBSCRIBE, concurrent same-handler idempotency, unsubscribing A while B remains, and no leaked listener tasks.

### NOTIFYUNREADSCOPE-BE-001 — mixed scope in the server-owned unread-count snapshot

**Source:** [RestMixin.get_unread_counts, lines 200–226](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L200-L226) resolves a panel role and department filter and applies both **only to total**. The same dictionary obtains by_role, by_channel, and by_severity through repository methods with **recipient_id only**, omitting the role/department filters. The public [GET /notifications/unread-count](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L654-L675) explicitly accepts role and department_key. Even with no role argument, total defaults to the user's panel role while all three group maps span every role attached to the user.

**Example:** one authorized synthetic user has cardiology 2 unread and dermatology 3 unread, all stored in the same account. A request with role=doctor and department_key=cardiology can report total=2 but by_role.doctor=5 (and possibly by_channel.in_app_inbox=5). [Frontend refreshUnreadCounts](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L625-L645) replaces one shared snapshot, and [markAllAsRead](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L887-L908) explicitly refreshes with role/department scope. The resulting snapshot mixes a scoped total with global map fields, so consumers treating the object as one coherent population can show contradictory badge counts.

**Boundary / distinction:** This is not group-47 NOTIFYSYNCBADGE-FE-001 (WS replay *deletes* role maps), group-43 NOTIFYSCOPEFLIGHT-FE-001 (in-flight responses reused across scopes), or group-47 NOTIFYMARKSCOPE-FE-001 (optimistic item mutation ignores department). The possible product intent of global-by_role maps must be decided before altering schemas; the current response does not label the mixed scopes.

**Synthetic RED test, not executed:** seed one user with two departments and multiple roles/channels, call unread-count with and without department filter, assert the response either applies consistent scoping to all fields or clearly distinguishes global aggregates from scoped total using an explicit contract. Verify the frontend global bell, per-role bell, and mark-all-read scoped refresh remain correct. Do not change authorization or cross-principal isolation incidentally.

### NOTIFYALLARCHIVE-FE-001 — "Все" displays a locally archived notification until reload

**Source:** [NotificationInbox local status selector, lines 169–211](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L169-L211) checks unread, archived and seen, but for statusFilter=all ends in unconditional return true. [NotificationCenterContext.archiveNotification, lines 860–885](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L860-L885) sets isArchived:true in its existing local inbox and never removes the row after success. The canonical backend [NotificationPlatformRepository.list_deliveries, lines 110–118](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L110-L118) excludes archived deliveries for status=all. Thus the "Все" tab is inconsistent before vs after refreshing the same canonical view.

**Impact:** a user archives a delivery; it remains visible immediately in the "Все" list (archived icon/no archive button), only to disappear after reloading the canonical page, creating misleading archive feedback. Different from group-44 NOTIFYARCHIVEFETCH-FE-001, which covers *archived filter losing all archived history on a fresh reload*. That problem needs archive retrieval; this one needs consistent "all" presentation after a successful local mutation.

**Synthetic RED test, not executed:** render the inbox at status=all with one synthetic unread entry. Mock successful archive. Assert the entry disappears immediately from "Все" but remains available when choosing "Архив" during the current session. After a server refresh of all, it stays absent from "Все"; a separately scoped archived fetch must populate "Архив". Preserve accessible action labels and the existing read/open behavior.

### NOTIFYSEENREAD2X-FE-001 — Open performs two successful mutations when read already implies seen

**Source:** [NotificationInbox.handleOpen lines 217–235](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L217-L235) awaits markAsSeen for !isSeen and then awaits markAsRead for !isRead. [Repository.mark_delivery_read, lines 317–328](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L317-L328) explicitly populates seen_at when absent, so a read mutation **alone** satisfies both state transitions. The [service mark_seen and mark_read, lines 446–467](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L446-L467) each commits and separately broadcasts the same delivery through WS. Consequently an ordinary unseen/unread Open entails two HTTP trips, two mutations/commits and two realtime fanouts, not one.

**Impact / scope:** unnecessary latency and mutation/broadcast amplification under ordinary clicks; a temporary seen mutation failure also prevents the otherwise sufficient read request. This is distinct from group-44 NOTIFYOPENBLOCK-FE-001 (failure prevents *navigation*), group-47 NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001 (labelled read button *navigates*), and parallel #4054 NOTIFYMUTATIONTOAST-FE-001 (even one successful mutation emits a new-delivery toast). Do not double count those UI outcomes; this issue is the **redundant seen→read call sequence** and its two writes.

**Synthetic RED test, not executed:** mount the inbox with a synthetic unseen/unread item, mock the REST mutation service. Click the *Open body*: assert exactly **one read mutation, zero seen mutations**, both read+seen state true and a single state-change broadcast; retain guarded navigation. Click a *seen but unread* entry: one read. Click an *already read* entry: no mutation. If seen-only behavior is separately required, preserve it as an explicit distinct control and test it there.

## Continued source Code Review — group 50: specialty-role visibility, critical toast classification and unread recovery races

**Review date:** 2026-11-13 (Asia/Tashkent). **Main pin:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2), latest observed main, unchanged since group 49. Scope and candidate IDs checked against merged #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, this PR groups 47–49 and parallel #4054 groups 47–52. Four **new source-observed candidates**, not proven production incidents.

| Candidate | Priority | Mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYROLEPANEL-FE-001 | P1 conditional | Backend stores specialist panel role, but real Doctor frontend requests doctor and filters away the specialist-tagged delivery |
| NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001 | P2 | Incoming critical lab event gets an info-level toast because toast type ignores event severity |
| NOTIFYUNREADPARTIAL-FE-001 | P2 conditional | Partial unread-count endpoint failure suppresses successful inbox unread_count fallback |
| NOTIFYSYNCSTALECOUNT-FE-001 | P2 conditional | Old WS replay snapshot can overwrite newer live unread totals due to response-arrival order |

### NOTIFYROLEPANEL-FE-001 — specialist-tagged clinician delivery hidden by generic Doctor inbox filters

**Exact source.** [CoreMixin.normalize_department_key lines 38–42](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_core.py#L38-L42) maps a profile department such as cardiology to cardiologist using [DEPARTMENT_ROLE_ALIASES](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_base.py#L110-L126). [PolicyMixin.resolve_panel_role_for_user lines 334–347](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L334-L347) prefers that specialty over User.role doctor. [RestMixin.record_delivery_for_user lines 299–350](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L299-L350) persists this panel_role on NotificationDelivery. However, the [active DoctorDialogsLayer passes userRole="doctor"](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/pages/doctor/views/DoctorDialogsLayer.tsx#L50-L53) and [RoleNotificationCenter loads role=doctor](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/RoleNotificationCenter.tsx#L46-L58). The backend [get_inbox applies an explicit role filter](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L119-L144), and frontend [getNotificationsByRole strictly compares item.role](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L916-L932). The [GlobalNotificationCenter](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/GlobalNotificationCenter.tsx#L74-L87) and [HeaderNew badge](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/layout/HeaderNew.tsx#L224-L230) also filter using login-profile role rather than the delivery panel role.

**Impact / caveat.** A canonical Doctor with profile.department=cardiology can have correctly persisted cardiologist notifications but see an empty Doctor inbox/badge. This potentially includes lab_critical_result notifications. No real account tested, and staff whose stored User.role itself equals cardiologist may be unaffected. Distinct from group-43 NOTIFYSPECIALTY-BE-001, which concerns the *backend selecting the wrong recipients for a specialty broadcast*: this finding concerns an **already correctly owned delivery filtered out on read**. Do not relax recipient-ID ownership.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run.** Create a synthetic Doctor user and cardiology department, record a single critical lab notification for that user, assert persisted role=cardiologist. Render the real Doctor page and global header; ensure the notification is visible and the unread badge represents it. Cover dentistry, dermatology, generic doctor, a different principal and role/department scoped filters. Agree a consistent panel-role alias contract before implementing.

### NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001 — critical lab event is displayed as ordinary info toast

**Exact source.** Canonical [send_lab_event_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L253-L282) persists lab_critical_result with severity="critical" and priority="urgent"; the [lab notification service](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/lab_notification_service.py#L267-L310) invokes this producer. The [server serializer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L59-L98) includes severity/priority in the nested WS notification. But [NotificationWebSocketContext lines 269–291](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L269-L291) categorizes the toast only by substrings of notification.type: error, warning or alert, success, otherwise info. "lab_critical_result" matches none and becomes info. Its severity is ignored for the toast.

**Impact / caveat.** The realtime notice for a critical lab result looks like a routine informational toast although its inbox data may still be labeled critical. This does NOT establish a backend DND bypass failure: [the server critical breakthrough policy](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L125-L141) independently recognizes critical events. Different from group-40 NOTIFYTOAST-FE-001 (duplicate toast for same delivery) and #4054 NOTIFYMUTATIONTOAST-FE-001 (state mutation toast): here a *single legitimate new delivery* gets the wrong severity presentation.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run.** Emit one canonical synthetic lab_critical_result WS frame with severity critical and priority urgent, and verify exactly one appropriate warning/error-class toast, no info toast, correct inbox severity and same delivery ID. Cover generic event type with critical severity, info, warning and security_alert events. Do not inadvertently modify duplicate-toasting or clinical read-acknowledgment semantics.

### NOTIFYUNREADPARTIAL-FE-001 — swallowed count failure prevents recovery from valid inbox unread_count

**Exact source.** [NotificationCenterContext.refreshUnreadCounts lines 625–668](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L625-L668) catches all getUnreadCount errors and returns unreadSnapshotRef.current with a numeric total. [syncNotifications lines 688–742](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L688-L742) uses Promise.allSettled(getInbox, refreshUnreadCounts), so a rejected unread-count HTTP request appears as a fulfilled refresh result containing stale prior snapshot. The fallback in lines 731–735 uses inboxPayload.unread_count only when unreadPayload.total is undefined; since the previous snapshot always contains total, the fallback is unreachable for this normal partial-failure path.

**Impact / caveat.** The inbox endpoint can succeed with unread_count=73 while the count endpoint fails; a previous zero or older count remains in state and can undercount unread badges, especially when only 50 inbox rows are cached. No production outage demonstrated. This is distinct from prior NOTIFYZERO-FE-001 (zero OR local fallback), NOTIFYRETRY-FE-001 (15s cooldown after inbox failure), NOTIFYSCOPEFLIGHT-FE-001 (shared in-flight request scope), and NOTIFYSYNCBADGE-FE-001 (WS payload omits per-role breakdown).

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run.** Mount provider with prior total 0, mock GET inbox success with 50 synthetic deliveries and unread_count=73, and mock unread-count HTTP 503. Assert total updates from authoritative inbox response rather than staying stale; then verify 429 backoff and successful subsequent count refresh. Preserve per-role accuracy when no grouped server count exists; never infer cross-principal totals from old user state.

### NOTIFYSYNCSTALECOUNT-FE-001 — delayed WS sync response can regress newer live unread total

**Exact source.** [WS handshake requests bounded notification_sync](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L209-L230), and [notification_sync_response handler](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L234-L247) passes its unread_count unconditionally to replaceNotifications. [replaceNotifications applies snapshot replacement](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L609-L619) while [appendNotification increments total and by_role when a new unread live event arrives](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L777-L807). No request epoch/version/watermark rejects a snapshot that predates the most recently processed push.

**Conditional interleaving.** Server reads unread_count=5 for a requested replay; a new persisted delivery arrives and its WS event is processed first, making client count 6. The earlier sync response arrives afterwards with unread_count=5 and overwrites total to 5, despite a merged inbox still including the sixth item. Requires controlled response-order reproduction; no real server interleaving was measured. Different from group-47 missing by_role fields, group-43 request-scope overlap, and group-45 replay incompleteness.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run.** Emit handshake total 5, then a new synthetic delivery X, then delayed sync response with old unread_count=5. Assert X remains in inbox and total is 6 (or a newer authoritative count refresh corrects it); do not accept the old 5 as newer state. Also test the reverse arrival order, concurrent legitimate reads, and a fresh login epoch. Use server-owned version or bounded requery rather than assuming unread counts only ever increase.

## Continued source Code Review — group 51: Redis unsubscribe race, notification query-cache invalidation, obsolete socket close marker and bulk-read scaling

**Date:** 2026-11-13, Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned latest main:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2). The code blobs were read from current main and compared with merged #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, this PR groups 47–50, parallel #4054 groups 47–52, and historical DISPLAYRELAY-BE-003. **Four independent, source-observed P2 conditional candidates**; none has been reproduced against the running clinic.

| ID | Priority | Source-observed mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYRELAYUNSUB-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Pending Redis UNSUBSCRIBE for the last old socket can execute after a newer SUBSCRIBE to the same logical user channel |
| NOTIFYQUERYEPOCH-FE-001 | P2 conditional | An old GET that resolves after cache invalidation can refill the 15-second notification cache with stale values |
| NOTIFYCONNECTMARKER-FE-001 | P2 conditional | One shared pending-close marker can be overwritten, leaving an older CONNECTING socket open |
| NOTIFYMARKALLSCALE-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Mark-all-read materializes all matching unread ORM deliveries and iterates the entire result set |

### NOTIFYRELAYUNSUB-BE-001 — old Redis UNSUBSCRIBE can override a newer subscriber

**Owner and evidence.** [NotificationWebSocketManager.disconnect, lines 53–63](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L53-L63) removes the final connection and schedules async removal via [manager lines 89–119](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L89-L119). [RedisPubSubBridge.unsubscribe, lines 165–175](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L165-L175) drops the final old handler and logical channel key **before awaiting a Redis UNSUBSCRIBE**. [RedisPubSubBridge.subscribe, lines 135–163](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L135-L163) can register a new handler and SUBSCRIBE to the same channel while the old unsubscribe is still pending; there is no shared per-channel lock or generation check.

**Conditional interleaving:** socket A disconnects, Redis unsubscribe for old user channel awaits; socket B reconnects for the same authenticated user, registers a new handler, successfully subscribes; then the older unsubscribe command completes *after* B's subscription, leaving B in local handler state while Redis may no longer forward the channel. Whether redis-py command serialization permits this exact order needs a controlled deferred fake; do not claim a demonstrated relay outage. Different from group-49 NOTIFYREDISJOINRACE-BE-001 (overlapping **subscribe** operations during initial failure), group-48 head-of-line blocking and the previously repaired single-handler failed initial SUBSCRIBE.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** fake pubsub delays old UNSUBSCRIBE after logical handler removal, allow B to SUBSCRIBE and return, then release old UNSUBSCRIBE and publish one synthetic notification. Assert B receives it, A never receives it, handler count/subscription state stay synchronized, and unrelated user channels are unaffected. Include concurrent unsubscribe failure and last-handler idempotency. Future repair needs a per-channel transition lock or generation-safe subscription state, without changing cross-principal routing.

### NOTIFYQUERYEPOCH-FE-001 — cleared notification query cache can be repopulated by an obsolete GET

**Owner and evidence.** [Frontend notification API services, lines 24–85](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/api/services.ts#L24-L85) uses a 15-second module-level result Map and a pending-promise Map. clearNotificationQueryCache at lines 55–59 clears both maps, but an already-running apiRequest is neither cancelled nor invalidated. Its then handler at lines 75–79 unconditionally writes its response **after** the clear. Its finally handler at line 81 also unconditionally deletes a cache key, even if a *new* request now owns that key. Successful [notification mutations](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/api/services.ts#L207-L241) and WebSocket frames invalidate this shared cache.

**Conditional interleaving:** GET inbox A starts before a user reads or archives a notification. After the successful mutation, the cache is cleared and a newer GET B may start. The old GET A then resolves with pre-mutation data and writes it back into the cache with a new cachedAt, potentially making future loads stale for another 15 seconds. A's finally may also remove the pending entry for B, causing duplicate network queries. This is a GET cache-generation failure, **not** the provider-scope promise race in group 43 or the WS replay counter race in group 50. No production trace or patient leak established.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** defer GET A, complete a synthetic mark-read API mutation and cache clear, start GET B, resolve A with older data and assert A is *not* cached and cannot clear B's pending map entry. Resolve B with newer data and assert later GET returns B; repeat for unread-count, websocket-driven invalidation, HTTP failure and an explicit principal switch. Use generation tags and promise identity, not a timer-only workaround.

### NOTIFYCONNECTMARKER-FE-001 — rapid route changes overwrite the pending-close WebSocket marker

**Owner and evidence.** [NotificationWebSocketProvider.closeSocketSafely, lines 145–163](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L145-L163) sets a **single** closeOnOpenRef for a CONNECTING socket rather than closing it immediately. The [socket.onopen handler, lines 330–344](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L330-L344) closes only when the same socket is still stored in that singleton. The [route effect, lines 396–439](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L396-L439) transitions sockets on pathname changes, including between protected routes.

**Conditional interleaving:** A is CONNECTING on route 1; moving to route 2 marks A to close and starts B. Before A opens, moving to route 3 marks B to close and **overwrites A's marker**. When A eventually opens, the onopen check no longer matches; A remains connected even though the current provider socket is newer. Its incoming events are [ignored as stale by onmessage, lines 346–355](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L346-L355), but the server can retain an unnecessary live session. This is distinct from group-48 NOTIFYROUTECHURN-FE-001 (one gratuitous reconnect on a protected route change). No live socket leak or unauthorized delivery proved.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** fake three WebSocket instances A/B/C initially CONNECTING, navigate rapidly across three protected routes with MemoryRouter, then fire onopen for A, B and C. Assert only C can remain OPEN, A and B close once, their server-side subscriptions release, and no orphaned reconnect timer or replay persists. Also test switching to public route and switching principal while sockets connect. Avoid a singleton pending-close marker for multiple sockets.

### NOTIFYMARKALLSCALE-BE-001 — mark-all-read scales with full ORM row materialization

**Owner and evidence.** [NotificationPlatformRepository.mark_all_read, lines 344–373](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L344-L373) filters unread/non-archived deliveries by recipient, role and department, then calls query.all() and loops over every NotificationDelivery entity to set seen_at, read_at and delivery_status before flush. Neither the [service](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L482-L505) nor the [WebSocket command](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L154-L172) bounds that user-specific backlog. SQLAlchemy may batch UPDATEs on flush; **the number of hydrated Python ORM objects and per-object operations remains proportional to unread row count**.

**Conditional impact / distinction:** a user with thousands of unread notifications may incur avoidable memory use and synchronous DB work in an async WebSocket handler. No measured system latency or production backlog established. This is not group-45 NOTIFYBULKFANOUT-BE-001, which concerns missing **cross-tab push** after a successful bulk change; this one concerns the backend **write strategy** even if all clients are notified.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** seed disposable DB with 10,000 unread deliveries for one synthetic recipient and other recipients, roles and departments. Invoke REST and WS mark-all-read; assert affected count, exact user/role/department scope, preservation of existing seen timestamps, idempotent repeat and transactional rollback. Instrument ORM entity loads and SQL statements to require a bounded batch/conditional bulk update, and benchmark WS responsiveness without hardware-dependent time assumptions. Decide return-count accuracy and concurrent arrival contract before implementation.

## Continued source Code Review — group 52: critical fallback, patient deep link, multi-recipient delivery and dedup insert concurrency

**Review date:** 2026-11-13 (Asia/Tashkent). **Current main anchor:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2), still the latest verified main. Directly inspected the current clinical notification producer, canonical sender, All Free decision endpoint, per-user inbox event writer, notification repository, models and real SPA route registry. Compared with #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, #4059 groups 47–51 and parallel #4054 groups 47–52. All four below are **source-observed candidates, not production incident claims or verified running-test failures**.

| Candidate ID | Priority | Mechanism and boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYCRITFINDING-BE-001 | P1 conditional, policy decision needed | A fallback alert named critical finding is persisted as info/high and not recognized by the critical realtime breakthrough policy |
| NOTIFYALLFREEPATLINK-BE-001 | P2 | All Free approval/rejection sends patient recipients a protected registrar-only deep link |
| NOTIFYBULKUSERHOL-BE-001 | P2 conditional | One blocked WS recipient delays a separately addressed recipient in multi-user fan-out after DB commit |
| NOTIFYEVENTDEDUPRACE-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Concurrent same-key event creates race through SELECT-then-INSERT into a unique-key table; one legitimate idempotent caller may receive an integrity failure |

### NOTIFYCRITFINDING-BE-001 — fallback critical lab finding loses critical priority and breakthrough handling

**Owned chain.** The active [LabNotificationService critical-value pathway, lines 248–319](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/lab_notification_service.py#L248-L319) recognizes a threshold-critical result, tries a canonical lab_critical_result, then **if canonical_created is false** tries a fallback **lab_critical_finding** with explicitly critical Russian title, urgent attention message, and metadata is_critical=True. The canonical [send_lab_event_notification helper, lines 256–282](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L256-L282) assigns severity="critical" and priority="urgent" **only** when normalized_type is lab_critical_result; fallback lab_critical_finding gets severity="info", priority="high". The server [CRITICAL_EVENT_TYPES allowlist](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_base.py#L44-L48) omits lab_critical_finding. The [breakthrough predicate](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L125-L141) sees neither a critical event type, severity nor urgent priority, and [standard preference checks may suppress its realtime broadcast](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L163-L218).

**Conditional effect:** if the primary canonical attempt returns false but fallback succeeds, a semantically critical laboratory finding can be stored and rendered as ordinary informational importance, and can be omitted from realtime when the noncritical desktop-visibility policy is disabled. This is not proof that a missed clinic alert occurred; the failure reason, actual fallback success and policy semantics are untested. Distinct from group-50 NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001: that existing finding concerns **frontend toast styling** of a correctly persisted critical lab_critical_result; this one is **server-side severity and realtime policy** of a different fallback event type.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** with disposable DB and synthetic critical lab result, make first sender for lab_critical_result return false while second sender for lab_critical_finding succeeds; assert fallback event severity and priority conform to the approved critical-alert policy and the corresponding WS breakthrough classification is explicitly tested. Cover success-first (no duplicate fallback), both attempts failing, normal lab results and opt-out vs clinician-approved critical exceptions. Do not silently override a patient/staff preference without a product and clinical policy decision. Any source fix is clinical plus realtime scope and requires the mandatory gate.

### NOTIFYALLFREEPATLINK-BE-001 — All Free patient notification points to a staff-only registrar page

**Owned chain.** [SystemMixin.send_all_free_decision_notification, lines 153–222](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_system.py#L153-L222) gathers the original registrar *and* an attached Patient.user_id and writes all_free_approved/all_free_rejected notifications to **both** recipients using a single explicit deep_link="/registrar". The method is actively invoked from [the admin approval/denial API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_wizard/_cart.py#L1530-L1588). [NotificationInbox.resolveNotificationTarget lines 75–80](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L75-L80) prioritizes this explicit deepLink above any event-type fallback. The [real routeRegistry registrar-home entry, lines 1190–1204](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1190-L1204) permits only Admin/Registrar; the [separate patient-home route](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1349-L1360) permits Patient. This is therefore a valid patient-owned inbox notification with a destination the patient cannot open.

**Scope:** selecting the notice can navigate a patient to forbidden/role guard rather than a relevant patient page. No privilege bypass is claimed; staff route guarding works as designed. This is independent of #4054 NOTIFYCHATDEEPLINK-BE-001 (the wrong message_received chat route) and this PR NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001 (the "Прочитать" button navigates). This case is specifically the **All Free decision canonical producer assigning the same registrar-only deep link to two different authorized recipient roles**.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** seed one synthetic visit with linked patient account and original registrar. Invoke the real approved and rejected decision notification producer. Assert admin/registrar targets resolve to permitted staff routes while patient delivery resolves to an authorized, relevant patient route or intentionally has no action target until a patient decision-detail screen exists. Mount the real route guard, not an artificial unguarded test route; validate unread and deep-link behavior without broadening Patient RBAC.

### NOTIFYBULKUSERHOL-BE-001 — multi-recipient producer awaits slow user A before user B

**Owned chain.** [RestMixin.record_delivery_for_users lines 367–443](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L367-L443) persists all recipient delivery rows and commits, then loops **sequentially** over all recipient+delivery pairs and awaits _broadcast_delivery_with_policy for each. [PolicyMixin._broadcast_delivery_with_policy, lines 280–322](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L280-L322) awaits _broadcast_delivery; [RestMixin._broadcast_delivery, lines 16–56](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L16-L56) awaits the manager send_json. On the same application event loop, [NotificationWebSocketManager._send_json_local, lines 150–166](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L150-L166) awaits each WebSocket.send_json with no deadline.

**Conditional effect:** in an admin or staff fanout to users A and B, if user A's socket has an unresolved send, the producer never reaches the already-persisted notification for user B (nor its Redis relay) until A unblocks. This can delay otherwise unrelated critical staff notifications. It is **not** group-41 NOTIFYFANOUT-BE-001, which covers **one user's multiple local sockets plus pre-publish Redis ordering**; nor group-48 NOTIFYREDISHOL-BE-001 (the **shared subscriber loop** blocking other user channels). This finding is a separate **cross-recipient publisher loop** that lacks a bounded forward-progress policy. A worker loop from an external scheduler might marshal sends asynchronously, so the direct impact is topology-dependent.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** use a fake platform service with two distinct synthetic users and their own fake WS sockets. Block A's send_json; invoke record_delivery_for_users; assert B gets the persisted event and its outbound WS/Redis dispatch within a bounded deadline **without releasing A**, while database commit remains durable. Also assert no cross-user mix-up, no duplicate fanout, same-user message ordering, transaction failure semantics and bounded dispatch work. Prefer a controlled outbox/queue or capped per-user concurrent publish, not unbounded create_task or dropped errors.

### NOTIFYEVENTDEDUPRACE-BE-001 — concurrent same-key event creation throws despite intended idempotency

**Owned chain.** The canonical event writer [PolicyMixin._create_or_get_event, lines 505–567](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L505-L567) computes a deterministic dedup hash, does a SELECT for an existing event, then inserts if none is visible. [NotificationPlatformRepository.get_event_by_dedup_key and create_event, lines 23–34](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L23-L34) perform the SELECT then SQLAlchemy add, flush and refresh; there is no PostgreSQL upsert, insert-conflict retry, or transaction savepoint around the uniqueness conflict. [NotificationEvent's DB schema](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/models/notification.py#L26-L45) has a unique constraint on dedup_key, making the SELECT-before-INSERT decision non-atomic across sessions. The same pattern is also used for delivery dedup, but this finding isolates **event creation**, which fails before delivery sequence allocation.

**Conditional interleaving:** two independently authenticated/authorized producers issue the *same logical event* concurrently. Both transaction snapshots can read no matching row; one event INSERT succeeds, the other INSERT/flush encounters a uniqueness IntegrityError instead of returning the winner. Without a scoped rollback/requery or ON CONFLICT policy, the second request can surface a 500 or a failure response, even though the durable event exists. No assertion of duplicate committed rows (the unique constraint prevents that), dropped clinical content, or observed PostgreSQL incident. Different from group-41 NOTIFYSEQ-BE-001 (MAX(sequence_id)+1 on **different deliveries**), group-45 NOTIFYOCCURRENCE-BE-001 (two **independent incidents** incorrectly collapse to one under a stable hash), and parallel #4054 bulk duplicate recipient/provider calls.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT executed:** two disposable PostgreSQL sessions synchronize just after get_event_by_dedup_key reports absent for the same dedup hash; release both inserts and verify exactly one canonical event, both callers return a success/idempotent reuse result, one delivery per intended recipient and no IntegrityError escapes. Test different hashes, event transaction rollback, same request replay, actual duplicate delivery constraint, and correct advisory vs clinical event occurrence identity. An ORM SQLite-only single-session test cannot prove the concurrency contract. Future remediation must use the mandatory database/realtime agent gate, reviewed transaction boundaries, isolation tests and deterministic PostgreSQL race harness.

### Group 52 scope, ledger, and verification

- **Four new source-backed candidates** (one P1 conditional, three P2 conditional or P2), **not** executed tests or verified clinic incidents. The critical-finding severity requires a clinical/product exception policy before changes. All Free patient deep link is a concrete contract mismatch; user-facing browser behavior needs real route-guard execution. The two concurrency hazards require deferred fake or PostgreSQL-controlled interleaving.
- **Prior source-review cross-check:** no matching existing ID/root cause in #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, #4059 groups 47–51 or parallel #4054 groups 47–52. Prior NOTIFYFANOUT-BE-001, NOTIFYSEQ-BE-001, NOTIFYOCCURRENCE-BE-001, NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001 and NOTIFYCHATDEEPLINK-BE-001 are adjacent **but distinct** and not refiled. No claims that old findings were repaired.
- **Cross-report registry math:** historical merged #3961 199 + #4049 20 + #4052 8 = 227. Parallel #4054 has 24 more static candidate entries; this PR groups 47–52 now has 24 raw entries including one known duplicate with #4054 (NOTIFYPAGEFLAG-BE-001 = NOTIFYCURSORLAST-BE-001). This PR contributes **23 additional distinct source-backed candidates**, so tracked combined count becomes **274 provisional distinct observations (227+24+23)**, **not** 274 open, tested, clinically observed, or production-unresolved defects. The registry is provisional and individual issues must be de-duplicated against runtime fixes before planning merges.
- **Mode/first touch:** direct_execute for documentation-only review. Only permitted modified path: docs/audits/2026-11-13-notification-websocket-postmerge-round47.md on existing Draft PR #4059. Read-only anchors: backend/frontend/AGENTS.md/prior reports/current tests; denied edits: backend/, frontend/, ai/, .github/, tests, migrations, deployment, secrets, clinic patient data, production, and any other PR.
- **Gate:** AGENTS.md read; Vercel React skill referenced. ai/langgraph/scripts/run_agent_gate.ps1 **NOT run** because this is a docs-only append. ## Continued source Code Review — group 53: Redis initialization latch, React updater purity and notification search/filter semantics

**Review date:** 2026-11-13 (Asia/Tashkent). **Pinned current main:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2), latest confirmed main. This review read actual current bridge, backend repository, frontend notification context and inbox, plus prior audit texts in merged #4049 groups 40–44, merged #4052 groups 45–46, this Draft PR groups 47–52, and parallel Draft #4054 groups 47–52. Four **additional source-backed candidates**, of which two are P2 conditional and two lower-priority P3 interface/query semantics. All require executable tests/product decisions before remediation; there are **no verified incidents or runtime test results**.

| Candidate | Priority | Code-observed concern |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYREDISINITLATCH-BE-001 | P2 conditional | A single initialization exception sets enabled=False permanently, preventing later Redis connection recovery on the same bridge |
| NOTIFYREENTRANTCOUNT-FE-001 | P2 conditional | appendNotification schedules a second state update *inside* a functional setInbox updater, which React may invoke multiple times |
| NOTIFYSEARCHPATTERN-BE-001 | P3 | Literal percent/underscore search input is treated as SQL LIKE wildcards in both items and total queries |
| NOTIFYFILTERCONFLICT-FE-001 | P3, UX contract | Unread-only toggle combines with archived status filter, making the Archive view systematically empty until another toggle is reversed |

### NOTIFYREDISINITLATCH-BE-001 — transient init error permanently disables cross-worker bridge initialization

**Exact source:** [RedisPubSubBridge._ensure_ready lines 177–206](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L177-L206) checks if not self.enabled at the top and immediately returns false. When a first initialization attempt raises during redis.asyncio import/from_url/pubsub construction, the except block permanently assigns self.enabled=False. No runtime path sets it back to true; later [subscribe lines 135–143](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L135-L143), [publish lines 53–65](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L53-L65) and [publish_ephemeral lines 68–77](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L68-L77) all short-circuit without a retry, even if the failure is transient and Redis later becomes reachable. The [listener recovery path](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L263-L305) also calls _ensure_ready, so it cannot undo this flag.

**Boundary and distinction:** this requires an **exception during bridge initialization**, not merely a Redis outage after from_url successfully constructs its lazy client; depending on redis-py and environment, network outages may arise only at later subscribe/publish and thus not trigger this exact flag. It is a shared infrastructure issue across websocket owners, not proof of a live clinic outage. It differs from group-40 NOTIFYRELAY-BE-001 (personal manager ignores a failed subscribe result, without same-socket retry), group-49 NOTIFYREDISJOINRACE-BE-001 (two handler joiners during one failing subscribe), and group-51 NOTIFYRELAYUNSUB-BE-001 (old unsubscribe racing a new subscription). Those do not require this **permanently latched bridge-level enabled=False** failure.

**Synthetic RED test — NOT RUN:** fake redis_asyncio.from_url or PubSub constructor to raise once, then succeed. Call _ensure_ready or subscribe on the same configured bridge after recovery; require the bridge eventually returns true with bounded backoff and a live subscribed handler, without restarting the process. Cover no Redis URL (intentionally disabled, never auto-enable), persistent invalid configuration, startup connection exceptions vs lazy network exceptions, concurrent retries under _init_lock and cleanup of partially created client. Never silently suppress errors or log message payloads.

### NOTIFYREENTRANTCOUNT-FE-001 — unread total is mutated as a side effect of the inbox state updater

**Exact source:** [NotificationCenterContext.appendNotification lines 777–807](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L777-L807) wraps deduplication in a setInbox(current => ...) functional updater. On a new unread item, **inside that updater**, it calls setUnreadSnapshot(currentSnapshot => ...) to increment total and role count. React state functional updaters must be pure: they can be re-invoked in development Strict Mode and may be retried under render scheduling. Each invocation of this updater branch triggers a *new, separate unread snapshot update* even when one inbox insertion is committed. Consequently the unread total/badge can increment more than once for **one newly received distinct delivery**, while inbox identity de-duplication still shows one row.

**Boundary and distinction:** exact double-count frequency depends on React execution mode, batching and whether the updater is replayed; not demonstrated in the running app. Unlike group-40 NOTIFYTOAST-FE-001 (the same WS frame invokes duplicate *toasts*) and group-50 NOTIFYSYNCSTALECOUNT-FE-001 (late *authoritative WS snapshot* overwrites a newer local count), this defect is a **non-pure nested state setter for a single newly received notification**. It can surface with no duplicate frames at all. Existing [context test](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/__tests__/NotificationCenterContext.test.tsx) only checks the standalone applyUnreadSnapshot helper and does not mount a StrictMode append flow.

**Synthetic RED test — NOT RUN:** mount NotificationCenterProvider inside React.StrictMode with a test consumer of appendNotification/getUnreadCount; inject **one** synthetic unique unread notification with stable delivery_id, await React commit, require exactly one inbox item and total/by_role increment by exactly one. Repeat the same ID (no second increment), a second distinct ID (one more increment), a read/archived incoming item (no increment) and a concurrent server snapshot refresh. Fix by a pure single reducer/atomic state transition or a separately idempotent count derivation; do not move mutable side effects into another render updater. Confirm frontend React 19 behavior with actual Vitest, not a static assumption.

### NOTIFYSEARCHPATTERN-BE-001 — literal search characters become unintended SQL wildcard expressions

**Exact source:** [NotificationPlatformRepository.list_deliveries lines 120–142](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L120-L142) and [count_deliveries lines 200–211](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L200-L211) interpolate the user's REST/WS search text into a PostgreSQL ILIKE pattern as percent + search.strip() + percent, **without escaping literal percent, underscore or the escape character**. The input remains a SQLAlchemy bound parameter, so this is **not SQL injection**. However, search="%" creates the universal wildcard pattern "%%%" and returns virtually all permitted non-archived rows; search="A_B" matches strings with arbitrary single-character replacement between A and B, not just the literal underscore. This can mislead staff filtering notifications, with count/has_more also reflecting broad unintended matches.

**Boundary and distinction:** this is P3 search correctness, not cross-user authorization bypass, a proven performance incident or the prior group-44 NOTIFYHISTORYPAGE-FE-001 (history UI only searches locally loaded page). Both REST inbox and WS sync expose the backend search argument; active frontend NotificationInbox currently performs local substring search and may not exercise this specific server SQL pattern. Confirm the public search contract before altering behavior.

**Synthetic RED test — NOT RUN:** seed a disposable user inbox with distinct titles "Glucose 100%" and "Glucose 1000", and "A_B" versus "AcB". Query the canonical REST and WS paths for literal "%" and "_" and assert only rows containing those exact characters are returned, with matching totals and unchanged recipient restrictions. Test backslash and case-insensitivity. Use an explicit ILIKE escape policy, and test legitimate substring searches without metacharacters. Document any intentionally supported advanced wildcard mode separately.

### NOTIFYFILTERCONFLICT-FE-001 — Archive filter is silently defeated by a sticky unread-only toggle

**Exact source:** [NotificationInbox lines 158–185](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L158-L185) manages showUnreadOnly and statusFilter as independent states, applying unread-only elimination **before** status='archived' selection. Because every archived item is removed whenever showUnreadOnly=true, the intersection of "Только непрочитанные" and "Архив" is always empty by code design, even if archived entries are already locally present. Both controls remain selectable independently in [inbox filter controls lines 337–358](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L337-L358), and the result shows the generic empty-state message. The user must notice and reverse the unread toggle manually to see any archived item.

**Boundary and distinction:** whether intersecting these filters is a supported deliberate behavior or an unintended UX trap is a **product semantics decision**; the source conclusively makes the combination empty. This is distinct from group-44 NOTIFYARCHIVEFETCH-FE-001 (archived history is not fetched from server) and group-49 NOTIFYALLARCHIVE-FE-001 (archived local items persist in the All tab), which can be tested independently. It is **P3 UX**, not a clinical data-loss or permission issue.

**Synthetic RED test — NOT RUN:** render NotificationInbox with a locally cached synthetic archived delivery. Repeat Archived first then unread toggle, All and Seen, role switch and no archived items. Avoid broadening server history behavior under this purely client-side fix.
