## Continued source Code Review — group 54: lab-result dead deep link, stale canonical target, guard-cancel read state and browser-native clinical preview

**Date:** 2026-11-13, Asia/Tashkent. **Current main:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2). Verified as latest main at this review. Inspected the canonical producer, dedup hashing, real registered routes and guards, WebSocket preview, and historical #3961/#4049/#4052 audits plus #4059 groups 47–53 and concurrent #4054 groups 47–52. Four new **source-observed and not runtime-reproduced** candidate findings follow; all require synthetic regression verification and appropriate ownership decisions.

| ID | Severity | Code-observed issue |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYLABDEADROUTE-FE-001 | P1 conditional | Canonical critical lab notification links to /lab/results, an unregistered SPA path; the registered /lab is Admin/Lab-only, not a generic Doctor result view |
| NOTIFYDEEPLINKDEDUP-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Explicit deep_link is excluded from event dedup hash when caller passes a nonempty snapshot, so later changed targets reuse the old persisted target |
| NOTIFYGUARDCANCEL-FE-001 | P2 conditional / product contract | Open performs read mutation before asking the dirty-form guard to confirm navigation; cancel still leaves the notification marked read |
| NOTIFYNATIVEPREVIEW-FE-001 | P2 conditional / privacy contract | OS-native notification displays full canonical clinical title/message when the browser tab is hidden and permission granted, without a separate preview redaction policy |

### NOTIFYLABDEADROUTE-FE-001 — critical lab results action resolves to Not Found for the ordering doctor

**Exact source chain:** [send_lab_event_notification in canonical sender](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L233-L283) persists deep_link="/lab/results" even for lab_critical_result. [NotificationInbox.resolveNotificationTarget](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L75-L95) prioritizes that explicit deep link; even the event-type fallback hardcodes /lab/results and /lab/results?critical=1. But [routeRegistry lines 1304–1321](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1304-L1321) has only the exact /lab path with roles Admin/Lab. The real [lab dirty-guard e2e fixture](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/e2e/lab-dirty-guard.spec.ts) and current routeRegistry comments explicitly establish /lab/results is unregistered and wildcards to /not-found. The [actual critical laboratory producer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/lab_notification_service.py#L270-L290) sends to the ordering doctor's User. Merely replacing /lab/results with /lab is **not** a valid generic Doctor fix because RBAC denies the Doctor there.

**Conditional impact:** an authorized ordering doctor trying to open a critical lab result from the inbox can end on Not Found rather than the study. The notification can still be stored/read through other workflows; no missed clinical diagnosis or proven production incident is asserted. Distinct from #4054 NOTIFYCHATDEEPLINK-BE-001 (unmounted chat route), #4059 NOTIFYALLFREEPATLINK-BE-001 (patient navigating to registrar) and the separate already repaired route-leave dirty guard.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run:** mount true ROUTE_REGISTRY and real role-boundary using a synthetic Doctor recipient, synthetic lab_critical_result and synthetic result identity. Click the canonical Open action and require a **registered, authorized clinical result/visit destination**, not /not-found or /forbidden, preserving the intended result ID. Repeat standard lab_results, Lab/Admin staff, stale study and unsaved form guard. Choose and approve the actual Doctor-owned result route before any code change; do not broaden LabPanel RBAC as a shortcut.

### NOTIFYDEEPLINKDEDUP-BE-001 — explicit changed destination can be lost when reusing canonical dedup event

**Exact source chain:** [PolicyMixin._build_event_dedup_key lines 439–467](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L439-L467) hashes event type, title/message, scope, severity, priority, entity, correlation and payload_snapshot, **not the independent deep_link or expires_at**. [CanonicalMixin.send_canonical_notification_to_user](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L85-L132) always supplies a prebuilt nonempty payload_snapshot as well as a separate deep_link. [RestMixin.record_delivery_for_user](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L320-L344) selects that caller-supplied snapshot, so the fallback snapshot builder that would have embedded deep_link is not used. [PolicyMixin._create_or_get_event](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L524-L565) returns an existing same-hash NotificationEvent unchanged, retaining its earlier event.deep_link.

**Conditional impact:** identical canonical title/message/metadata and same recipient/event identity but different intentional links (e.g. result=A versus result=B) collapse into one old persisted event and delivery whose action still points to A. Whether link changes should create a new event or update an existing one is an SSOT contract decision. Not a claim of an actual repeated alert incident, and **different** from group-45 NOTIFYOCCURRENCE-BE-001 (identical repeated incidents ignored) and group-52 NOTIFYEVENTDEDUPRACE-BE-001 (two simultaneous same-key inserts fail).

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run:** send equal synthetic canonical notifications with deep links /doctor/cardiology?result=A then ?result=B. Require either independently persisted actionable B or a reviewed same-event update preserving the new authorized target; never silently return old A as if B was recorded. Repeat identical link replay, different expiry, different recipient and two concurrent requests. Any fix must preserve event/delivery idempotency and ownership and use the mandatory DB/realtime gate.

### NOTIFYGUARDCANCEL-FE-001 — cancellation of guarded leave does not cancel earlier mark-read writes

**Exact source:** [NotificationInbox.handleOpen lines 213–235](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L213-L235) awaits markAsSeen and markAsRead **before** calling navigateToNotificationTarget, which uses the [real LabDirtyGuardContext guarded navigator](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/laboratory/LabDirtyGuardContext.tsx). In [the existing NotificationInbox guarded-navigation test](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/__tests__/NotificationInbox.test.tsx#L210-L280), the clinician can click Open, then select Cancel and remain in the unsaved form. But by then read/seen mutations have already been issued and committed; cancelling navigation does not undo them.

**Conditional semantics:** if the product contract is "read only after actual successful navigation into linked clinical content", that workflow silently clears the critical unread reminder although the user did not open its target. Conversely, if inbox preview itself is an acknowledged read, the behavior could be deliberate; **do not label a confirmed bug without owner decision**. Distinct from group-44 NOTIFYOPENBLOCK-FE-001 (failed read prevents navigation), group-49 NOTIFYSEENREAD2X-FE-001 (two redundant HTTP mutations) and group-47 NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001 (Read button unexpectedly navigates).

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run:** mount the real guarded Router with one synthetic unread critical event and a dirty clinical form. Click the Open body; cancel route leave; check read state against explicitly approved semantics and keep unsaved form unchanged. Test Confirm/Discard, same-target path, failed REST mutation and an inaccessible destination. Avoid changing dirty guard protection or inventing clinical acknowledgement semantics.

### NOTIFYNATIVEPREVIEW-FE-001 — browser OS preview receives full sensitive clinical event text

**Exact source:** [NotificationWebSocketContext lines 269–299](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L269-L299) passes the unredacted normalized notification.title and notification.message into a native OS/browser Notification when document.hidden and Notification.permission="granted". There is no separate native preview projection or privacy filter in this call path. The [critical lab producer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/lab_notification_service.py#L270-L290) may include test name, value and patient identifier in its canonical title/message. The [backend delivery serializer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L59-L98) transports that detail to the authenticated app.

**Boundary:** on some browser/OS configurations, native previews appear outside the app and on a lock screen. A permission grant is required, and whether a particular workstation exposes native preview text is **not tested**; no actual leak or unauthorized viewing is claimed. Consent to show desktop notifications is not automatically a documented choice to show full patient-linked content on a shared/locked device. Treat as a **conditional privacy review**, not a proven privacy violation. Distinct from group-40 NOTIFYTOAST-FE-001 (duplicate toasts/natives), parallel #4054 NOTIFYMUTATIONTOAST-FE-001 (state changes cause repeat alerts) and group-50 NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001 (severity style). Here the only concern is **content projection to an OS-level notification surface**.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT run:** with mock Notification API, hidden document, granted permission and a synthetic critical lab payload containing marker PATIENT_SYNTH_41, assert native preview excludes identifiable patient marker, analyte and measured value while the authorized in-app inbox preserves full content. Repeat a non-sensitive operational event, permission denied/default and any explicitly reviewed opt-in policy. Do not suppress urgent alert awareness, log raw clinical data or use real patients. Exact redaction and consent semantics need product/privacy owner approval.

## Continued source Code Review — group 55: idempotent replay dispatch, blocking WebSocket reads, inbox unread labels and keyboard accessibility

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13 Asia/Tashkent; [main eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2), still latest observed. Compared active sources and earlier merged #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, #4059 groups 47–54 and parallel #4054 groups 47–52. **Four source-observed candidate findings, not verified runtime failures.**

| ID | Priority | Distinct issue |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYIDEMPUBLISH-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Idempotently reused delivery is nevertheless broadcast again as a new delivery |
| NOTIFYWSSYNCLOOP-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Synchronous SQLAlchemy inbox DB queries execute directly inside async WebSocket loop |
| NOTIFYINBOXCOUNT-FE-001 | P3 UX contract | Inbox header unread number changes with list search/status and counts cached page only |
| NOTIFYDIALOGFOCUS-FE-001 | P3 accessibility contract | Notification dropdown advertises dialog role without managing focus or Escape close |

### NOTIFYIDEMPUBLISH-BE-001 — repeated canonical producer call re-emits an already-persisted delivery

**Source:** [PolicyMixin._create_or_get_event, lines 538–548](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L538-L548) returns an existing event for the dedup key. [_create_or_get_delivery, lines 590–610](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L590-L610) likewise returns the existing recipient delivery when dedup matches. Nevertheless [record_delivery_for_user, lines 328–364](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L328-L364) unconditionally calls _broadcast_delivery_with_policy after commit, without new-vs-reused information. [record_delivery_for_users, lines 398–443](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L398-L443) also rebroadcasts all reused deliveries.

**Conditional consequence:** retry of one completed canonical event reuses one durable delivery_id but emits a second outbound WS notification frame for that same ID, including after the original row was read. The [current consumer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationWebSocketContext.tsx#L261-L299) presents it as a toast. Separate from group-40 NOTIFYTOAST-FE-001 (**client** repeated-frame toast dedup), group-45 NOTIFYOCCURRENCE-BE-001 (distinct incidents collapsed) and group-52 NOTIFYEVENTDEDUPRACE-BE-001 (concurrent insert uniqueness); the independent owner here is **backend duplicate post-dedup dispatch**. Do not declare every repeated producer invocation a defect without defining at-least-once replay and publish-failure retry semantics.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT executed:** with synthetic recipient/WS manager, send one canonical event twice with same stable dedup identity. Assert a single durable event and delivery, then require reviewed new-delivery dispatch semantics (normally one new-delivery frame, or explicit state-sync on replay). Mark the first delivery read before the retry and ensure the retry does not masquerade as a new unread alert. Cover multi-recipient replay, genuinely new event, policy-suppressed delivery, Redis transport loss and deliberate resend/outbox recovery. Do not sacrifice legitimate delivery retry capability.

### NOTIFYWSSYNCLOOP-BE-001 — one normal WebSocket inbox catch-up performs blocking ORM I/O on the app loop

**Source:** [notification_websocket.py, lines 174–221](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L174-L221) calls synchronous platform_service.get_inbox directly from the async receive loop; there is no to_thread/run_in_threadpool/async SQLAlchemy bridge. [RestMixin.get_inbox lines 119–165](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L119-L165) runs synchronous list_deliveries, count_deliveries and count_unread_deliveries queries, and [repository methods](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/repositories/notification_platform_repository.py#L83-L214) use ordinary SQLAlchemy Session I/O. The [connection_established path, lines 49–52](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notification_websocket.py#L49-L52) also performs synchronous get_unread_counts in the same async function.

**Conditional impact:** a delayed networked PostgreSQL read can block the entire Uvicorn worker event loop and postpone sends/heartbeats for unrelated sockets, even with **one** legitimate sync request. No live latency or worker starvation measured. Distinct from group-45 NOTIFYWSSYNCFLOOD-BE-001 (unthrottled **repetition** of sync queries), group-51 NOTIFYMARKALLSCALE-BE-001 (materializing **bulk write** rows) and group-40 NOTIFYPOOL-BE-001 (lifetime of held **DB connections**).

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT executed:** fake deterministic blocking sync repository and authorized websocket, concurrently schedule an event-loop heartbeat. Trigger one notification_sync and verify heartbeat progresses under appropriate DB offload/async architecture while exact user/role/cursor filtering remains correct. Cover errors and session ownership. Never move a single synchronous SQLAlchemy Session across threads concurrently; use a separately owned session for thread offload or properly configured async engine. Include real PostgreSQL test in isolated staging before concluding.

### NOTIFYINBOXCOUNT-FE-001 — unqualified unread header represents only the currently filtered local list

**Source:** [NotificationInbox, lines 158–211](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L158-L211) obtains getNotificationsByRole and filters the local cached items by unread-only, statusFilter and searchText. It then derives unreadCount from the **already-filtered** items and prints [role plus count plus unread in the header, lines 283–288](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L283-L288). Meanwhile [NotificationCenterContext.getUnreadCount, lines 934–946](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/contexts/NotificationCenterContext.tsx#L934-L946) exposes separate role counts and server snapshot, and the wrappers load at most 50 page items.

**Conditional UX:** selecting Seen/Archived or entering a nonmatching search yields headline 0 unread despite other unread deliveries for that role. The label lacks any indication that this is a **filtered subset count**, and backlog beyond loaded rows is also excluded. A filtered-count design may be intentional; this is P3 semantics/label clarity, not lost read state. Distinct from group-50 partial unread API failure and group-53 NOTIFYFILTERCONFLICT-FE-001 (two filters making an empty archived list).

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT executed:** seed five server-owned unread notifications but only two cached in the active list. Search with a nonmatching string or select Archived; require either stable role-scoped total 5 (with exact source of truth) or explicit filtered/local count labelling that does not claim there are zero unread globally. Test different authenticated roles, empty pages, and server zero vs stale local data.

### NOTIFYDIALOGFOCUS-FE-001 — inbox dropdown is a declared dialog but lacks focus, Escape and restore lifecycle

**Source:** [NotificationInbox lines 254–305](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L254-L305) renders a positioned role=dialog element and visible Close button but contains no focus-on-open, keyboard Escape handler, focus ref, or focus restoration. [RoleNotificationCenter lines 115–120](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/RoleNotificationCenter.tsx#L115-L120) toggles open from the bell without such logic; [GlobalNotificationCenter lines 112–119](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/GlobalNotificationCenter.tsx#L112-L119) only mounts the same dropdown. The app's accessible Modal primitive is not involved.

**Conditional accessibility:** keyboard users may stay on the opener, tab through unrelated background controls or not be able to close via Escape; no live screen-reader trial is claimed. A modal dialog and a nonmodal popover have **different** correct focus/containment policies. Do not impose a modal focus trap without deciding which interaction this fixed-position inbox actually represents. Separate from the historical project-wide Modal primitive focus-trap repair and group-53 inbox status/filter UX.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT executed:** render the bell and inbox together with keyboard interaction, check initial document.activeElement, Escape-close and restore-to-opener. Verify Tab/Shift+Tab according to approved dialog-vs-popover semantics, screen-reader name, both global and role center, and dirty-form guarded navigation. Avoid regressions in clinical draft protection.

- **Four distinct source-reviewed candidates**: two conditional P2 (server dispatch/loop starvation) and two P3 (label/keyboard UX). Total **286 provisional unique source-observed candidates** (227+24+35), NOT 286 proven open or production bugs. Independent status reconciliation may change counts.

No authorized runtime source fix.
- **Mandatory gate:** AGENTS.md and installed React best-practices reviewer consulted. ai/langgraph/scripts/run_agent_gate.ps1 NOT run for this report-only operation. Any future realtime/DB/frontend contract fix requires independently scoped mandatory gate, documented semantics and executed synthetic regression tests.

## Continued source Code Review — group 56: exhausted Redis listener recovery, suppressed-burst precursor, delivery metrics and error-severity badge

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13, Asia/Tashkent. **Current main pin:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2), still latest at lookup. Reviewed live Redis bridge, policy mixin, canonical delivery lifecycle, admin stats API/schema, notification inbox UI and existing contract tests. Compared existing audit IDs and root causes from merged #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, this Draft #4059 groups 47–55 and parallel Draft #4054 groups 47–52. Four distinct **source-backed, unexecuted candidate findings**, including explicit semantic/operational caveats.

| Candidate | Priority | Source-observed concern |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYREDISEXHAUST-BE-001 | P2 conditional | After five failed Redis listener recoveries, repeating subscribe for a still-registered handler returns success but never restarts the dead listener |
| NOTIFYBURSTPOLICYFIRST-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Queue anti-noise reads persisted predecessor even when predecessor was suppressed by realtime policy; first permissible alert can be suppressed |
| NOTIFYDELIVERYSUCCESSMETRIC-BE-001 | P3 operational-contract | Persisting an inbox row immediately stamps delivered/first_delivered_at before any realtime attempt, while admin history/stats count it successful even if broadcast was policy-suppressed |
| NOTIFYERRORBADGE-FE-001 | P3 presentation | Inbox severity style handles critical, danger, warning and success but not error, rendering error-severity badges with the default informational colors |

### NOTIFYREDISEXHAUST-BE-001 — Redis listener never restarts for existing handler after recovery exhaustion

**Code chain:** [RedisPubSubBridge._listen_loop and _recover_listener, lines 208–286](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L208-L286) catches fatal listener errors and runs **at most five** recovery attempts. On exhaustion it logs that events are unavailable until a new subscribe, but it retains the existing logical handler map and leaves the listener task completed. [subscribe, lines 117–163](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/ws_redis_pubsub.py#L117-L163) starts a listener only in the branch **was_added and len(handlers)==1** following an actual first-channel SUBSCRIBE. A **repeat subscribe with the same handler** is treated as already registered, returns True once _ensure_ready succeeds, and never executes the listener-restart branch. A different handler added to the existing populated channel also bypasses that branch. The [personal manager's _ensure_user_subscription, lines 70–87](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_websocket.py#L70-L87) keeps one cached handler per connected user and can call subscribe again.

**Conditional consequence:** if the Redis listener recovery exhausts while one or more personal sockets remain registered, subsequent same-user connection or re-subscription can appear successful yet no Redis events are actually consumed on that worker. A **new formerly empty channel** may restart the loop; this finding is specifically the **existing-handler/existing-channel after exhausted listener** path, not a blanket claim that all subscribes fail. It is different from group-40 NOTIFYRELAY-BE-001 (initial false subscribe ignored by personal manager), group-49 NOTIFYREDISJOINRACE-BE-001 (overlapping first subscriptions), group-51 NOTIFYRELAYUNSUB-BE-001 (stale unsubscribe races new subscriber), and group-53 NOTIFYREDISINITLATCH-BE-001 (a distinct init exception permanently sets enabled=False). No actual Redis crash or 5-attempt recovery timing has been reproduced.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** fake the bridge listen async iterator so it raises and the five _recover_listener attempts deterministically fail; keep handler A in _handlers. Restore fake Redis; invoke subscribe(channel, A), and also subscribe(channel, B) while A remains. Assert at least one actual active listener consumes a synthetic remote event and reaches A/B exactly once, rather than reporting successful registration while listener task remains done. Include temporary Redis absence, new empty channel and cross-user isolation. Reset or replace the listener only in a serialized generation-safe way; do not create duplicate competing listener tasks.

### NOTIFYBURSTPOLICYFIRST-BE-001 — earlier **non-broadcast** queue event can suppress next allowed realtime alert

**Code chain:** [PolicyMixin._broadcast_delivery_with_policy lines 280–322](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L280-L322) checks user policy first; a disabled desktop control, queue quiet hours or muted setting returns False without broadcasting. [RestMixin.record_delivery_for_user lines 320–364](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L320-L364) **persists and commits** the delivery before the policy decision, as intentionally tested in [test_record_delivery_for_user_persists_inbox_when_realtime_suppressed_by_event_setting](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/tests/unit/test_notification_platform_contract.py). When a later event becomes policy-allowed, [_should_suppress_queue_burst_realtime lines 221–277](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L221-L277) queries **recently persisted deliveries**, not actual successful/realtime-attempted ones. It suppresses on same entity within 45 seconds even when the predecessor was never pushed.

**Conditional interleaving:** at time T, queue_update A for visit 100 is persisted with desktop realtime off, so no WS alert. Staff re-enable desktop realtime or quiet hours end; at T+10 seconds, distinct queue_update B for same visit with changed message is persisted and policy permits push. Anti-noise sees A, treats it as a recent similar event by entity and suppresses B too. The first realtime-eligible queue change after an opt-in can therefore remain invisible until next allowed event outside the window; both rows still exist in inbox. This is distinct from parallel #4054 NOTIFYBURSTCROSSTYPE-BE-001 (suppressing **different event types** due to common entity), which need not involve the predecessor being **policy-suppressed**. It also differs from group-52 NOTIFYBULKUSERHOL-BE-001 (slow user dispatch). Whether suppressed history should count toward an anti-noise throttle is an explicit product policy choice, not an automatic contract violation.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** using synthetic user + disposable DB and controlled clock, disable queue realtime for event A and assert WS send count 0; enable it within the 45-second window and create distinct same-entity event B. With a policy that prevents duplicate *actually broadcast* events, assert B is pushed exactly once. Compare no preference change, same-type/same-message deliberately repeated, legitimately different incident, a previously actually-pushed A, and events outside window. Do not disable quiet-hours enforcement or flood protection globally.

### NOTIFYDELIVERYSUCCESSMETRIC-BE-001 — inbox persistence is counted as successful outbound delivery

**Code chain:** [PolicyMixin._create_or_get_delivery, lines 579–633](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L579-L633) creates the canonical inbox delivery with delivery_status="delivered", dispatched_at=now and first_delivered_at=now **before** the platform broadcasts to a user. [record_delivery_for_user](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L299-L364) commits this state, then checks realtime policy; policy can intentionally return False for all desktop delivery. [RestMixin.get_stats lines 216–296](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L216-L296) classifies any status "delivered" as successful and reports global successful/total_sent counts; the [admin-only /history/stats API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L781-L799) exposes the numbers. The same delivery serializer reports first_delivered_at even when no client was connected or a policy suppressed the desktop push.

**Boundary / product decision:** If this status **explicitly means 'persisted in the durable inbox'**, then delivered and successful could be semantically correct; in that case the admin label and telemetry contract should distinguish inbox persistence from client-realtime or external channel delivery. If intended to mean **sent/received by an actual client**, the metrics overstate actual alert reach. A successful Redis PUBLISH cannot itself prove user receipt either. This is P3 operational metric semantics pending product ownership, not evidence of false clinical delivery, lost messages or an error on the database commit. Parallel #4054 NOTIFYCHANNELSTATE-BE-001 concerns **failed external email/SMS/Telegram channels not reconciled to a canonical inbox row**, a separate channel outcome problem; this finding concerns **the canonical in-app status and admin aggregate labels before any attempted WebSocket push**.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** seed a synthetic user whose desktop policy disables one noncritical event; record one inbox delivery, assert persistence succeeds and zero WS sends. Define expected distinct counters such as inbox_persisted=1, realtime_attempted=0, realtime_accepted=0 rather than conflating with one 'successful sent' event; repeat with a connected socket, no socket and a synthetic Redis failure. Keep admin-only access and no message content in stats. A schema/API backward-compatibility review is mandatory before changing published metrics.

### NOTIFYERRORBADGE-FE-001 — severity error displays with ordinary informational badge colors

**Code chain:** [NotificationInbox.getSeverityStyle, lines 36–47](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L36-L47) maps "critical" and "danger" to the danger palette, warning to warning and success to success, then falls through to a blue informational default. It does **not** map the valid severity "error". The [canonical notification schema](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/schemas/notification.py#L79-L100) explicitly documents "info, warning, critical, error". [NotificationInbox's actual badge/icon rendering lines 382–458](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L382-L458) uses getSeverityStyle(item.severity) for both badge and icon, although the badge text still prints ERROR.

**Consequence and boundary:** a valid error-severity notification is *labelled* error but colored like info. This affects salience/accessibility and consistency, not the DB record or event visibility. No real error-severity producer usage was confirmed in this inspection, so this remains a small P3 UI compatibility gap until a synthetic error-level item is rendered. Distinct from group-50 NOTIFYCRITTOAST-FE-001 (transport **toast type** based on event-type substring instead of canonical **critical** severity) and group-54 NOTIFYNATIVEPREVIEW-FE-001 (native OS privacy). It specifically concerns the **inbox row's error badge palette**, not realtime transport.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** render synthetic inbox entries of severity error, critical, warning, info and success using the real component and tokens. Require error and danger/critical to use the documented alert palette while info remains informational, and include a non-color screen-reader label/role for severity. Verify dark/light contrast and that actual critical-lab entries remain appropriately emphasized. A component-level test plus visual a11y check is sufficient after the owner confirms palette semantics.

### Group 56 cumulative ledger and execution boundaries

- Four new **source-reviewed** entries: two P2 conditional (Redis listener recovery and policy-suppressed anti-noise predecessor) and two P3 (admin metrics semantics and inbox error badge). The two policy/metrics issues require an explicit operational/product contract decision before code changes. No new matching IDs/root causes were found in the audited prior groups/parallel report. All proposed tests remain **unexecuted**.

Combined **290 provisional unique source-reviewed observations** (227 + 24 + 39), **not** 290 currently open, reproducible, production-incident or unfixed bugs. Reconcile with merged repairs before planning an implementation backlog.
- **Execution mode:** direct_execute, documentation-only extension of existing Draft PR #4059. **Only allowed edited file:** docs/audits/2026-11-13-notification-websocket-postmerge-round47.md. **Read-only:** all backend/frontend/runtime/test/AGENTS.md/prior audit sources and React reviewer skill. **Denied:** other paths, backend/frontend repairs, database migrations, AI/CI/ops, credentials, patient information, staging/production, merges, deployment, or new PR. **Date:** 2026-11-13 (Asia/Tashkent). **Pinned main:** [eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2); still latest observed main. Compared historical merged #3961, #4049 groups 40–44, #4052 groups 45–46, #4059 groups 47–56 and parallel #4054 groups 47–52. **Four independent source-observed candidates, not executed failures**.

| Finding | Priority | Mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYQUEUENAV-BE-001 | P2 conditional | The canonical queue-position notification sends recipients to /queue, but that SPA path is not registered |

### NOTIFYQUEUENAV-BE-001 — canonical queue-position event points to the absent /queue SPA page

**Direct source:** [send_queue_position_event_notification, lines 353–391](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L353-L391) persists an explicit deep_link="/queue" for recipient User, including queue-position events. [NotificationInbox.resolveNotificationTarget, lines 75–112](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L75-L112) prioritizes explicit deepLink and independently defaults multiple queue types to "/queue". The real [routeRegistry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts) has **no /queue route**; it provides a public queue **join** at /queue/join and a role-scoped registrar queue screen at [registrar-queue /registrar/queue](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1225-L1250). A patient should not be sent to that staff-only route.

**Conditional effect:** an authorized recipient clicking a queue-position alert follows a nonexistent screen to Not Found rather than their queue state. A real patient may still check position through another workflow; no proven lost notification or RBAC bypass. Distinct from group-54 NOTIFYLABDEADROUTE-FE-001 (doctor critical result points to absent /lab/results), group-52 NOTIFYALLFREEPATLINK-BE-001 (patient given registrar-only link) and #4054 NOTIFYCHATDEEPLINK-BE-001 (old /messages chat route). This is a separate **queue-domain route contract**.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** create a synthetic canonical queue_position or queue_update event for an authenticated Patient and a permitted staff recipient. Open its real NotificationInbox action with the actual route registry; require a **registered recipient-authorized queue-status destination** preserving queue identity, not /not-found, /queue/join with unintended re-registration, or staff-only /registrar/queue for a Patient. Repeat different role and missing queue; do not weaken queue/RBAC/clinical ownership. Route choice requires the queue-domain owner.

### NOTIFYSTAFFNAV-BE-001 — two existing canonical staff event producers persist dead staff deep links

**Direct source:** [SystemMixin.send_all_free_request_notification, lines 64–129](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_system.py#L64-L129) delivers all_free_requested to Admin recipients with deep_link="/admin/all-free-requests". The actual role-Admin [routeRegistry entry is /admin/all-free](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L763-L783) and has no /admin/all-free-requests alias. Separately, [CanonicalSenderMixin.send_patient_registered_notification, lines 396–437](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L396-L437) sends both Admin and Registrar an explicit "/registrar/patients", absent from routeRegistry; the actual registrar-home page is [registered at /registrar](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1183-L1205), while Admin has its own authorized /admin/patients screen. [NotificationInbox's explicit link priority](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L75-L82) prevents fallback correction.

**Effect:** permitted staff clicking these notifications can land at Not Found. Unlike group-52 NOTIFYALLFREEPATLINK-BE-001 (the **decision** alert goes to an unauthorized Patient route), this is the distinct **pending All Free request** to Admin and **new-patient registration** to Registrar/Admin, both with *nonexistent* route strings. This groups two source producers under the same staff-route-registry contract rather than counting two near-duplicate stale route findings. No production browser test was run and exact target views require approval.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** create synthetic pending All Free request for Admin and patient_registered event for Registrar and Admin. Assert the links generated by each real producer exist in ROUTE_REGISTRY and are authorized for each recipient role. Confirm opening pending All Free reaches the actionable approval list, and patient_registered reaches a patient-focused staff view; preserve visit/patient identity and unsaved form guard. Do not give a Registrar Admin permissions to make a broken route seem valid.

### NOTIFYSUPERADMIN-BE-001 — superuser with non-Admin User.role is omitted from administrative notification fan-out

**Direct source:** [PolicyMixin.resolve_panel_role_for_user, lines 334–347](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L334-L347) returns "admin" for User.is_superuser, independently of its role string. Yet [resolve_users_for_role("admin"), lines 362–381](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L362-L381) includes only users whose **literal lower(User.role) == "admin"**, with no is_superuser exception. Both [send_system_alert](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_system.py#L15-L64) and [send_all_free_request_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_system.py#L64-L129) deliver via resolve_users_for_role("admin"). The [User model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/models/user.py#L24-L41) supports distinct role and is_superuser, and [core role scheme](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/backend/app/core/roles.py) recognizes a SuperAdmin string.

**Conditional scenario:** an active User(role="SuperAdmin", is_superuser=True) sees an Admin notification pane (role resolution) but receives **no targeted system/All Free request event** because the producer filters by string Admin. If product policy intentionally excludes superusers from these notifications, document that exception consistently; otherwise it is a role fan-out correctness gap. It is not the prior group-43 NOTIFYPOLICYADMIN-BE-001 (a SuperAdmin denied **settings** writes), group-50 NOTIFYROLEPANEL-FE-001 (specialist Doctor display filter), or historical queue WSROLE-BE-001 (different module's room auth guard). No actual SuperAdmin alert was measured.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** create active Admin, active SuperAdmin with User.role="SuperAdmin", is_superuser=True, ordinary non-admin superuser (if policy supports), inactive admin and ordinary Patient. Trigger a system_alert and All Free request; assert exact approved admin recipient set, no unintended Patient/disabled identities, and every targeted user receives one notification in the matching Admin panel. Explicitly decide whether is_superuser rather than string role is the SSOT before adjusting recipient predicates; no auth widening without approval.

### NOTIFYOPENPANEL-FE-001 — linked notification navigation does not close the global dropdown

**Direct source:** [NotificationInbox.handleOpen, lines 213–235](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/NotificationInbox.tsx#L213-L235) calls navigateToNotificationTarget(target) but never invokes its provided onClose callback on this normal-link path. [RoleNotificationCenter](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/eaab3b9365c0a1056b90f1afdc74c5e18924cfc2/frontend/src/components/notifications/RoleNotificationCenter.tsx#L115-L120) also controls local open state and has no mandatory close on navigation if still mounted. [The unmerged #4060 branch](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4060) closes this popup only in a special message_received→chat-overlay branch, not in the normal navigation branch.

**Conditional UX:** after a successful normal link navigation within AppShell, the dropdown remains visibly open above the destination, potentially obscuring target content. A parent route may unmount some role-specific wrappers, but the global header remains mounted and no onClose was called there. It is distinct from group-55 NOTIFYDIALOGFOCUS-FE-001 (missing keyboard focus/Escape lifecycle), group-54 NOTIFYGUARDCANCEL-FE-001 (read state before canceled guarded navigation) and group-47 NOTIFYREADBUTTON-FE-001 (separate Read action navigates). Treat P3 UI behavior; do not close prematurely when a dirty-form navigation is **canceled**.

**Proposed synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** mount AppShell's GlobalNotificationCenter on two real protected routes, open inbox, activate synthetic valid normal-link notification and allow guarded navigation; assert the panel closes on successful navigation and focus is restored appropriately. Cancel a dirty clinical leave and assert panel stays usable and unsaved form is untouched; test same-page/no-target links, chat special case from #4060 and role-specific mounted panel. Avoid bypassing the guarded router.

| NOTIFYPUSHRESULT-BE-001 | P2 conditional / queue-owner decision | send_push returns true even without attempted or successful FCM; queue manual-call flow counts that as sent and can start resend cooldown |

### NOTIFYDESKTOPPRECEDENCE-BE-001 — contradictory desktop controls silently select the enabling value

**Code chain:** [notifications.py normalization lines 152–181](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L152-L181) accepts top-level enabled/realtime_enabled/desktop booleans alongside nested channels.desktop. [PolicyMixin._extract_realtime_control_state lines 23–40](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L23-L40) prefers any top-level value to nested channels.desktop. Consequently the accepted input channel_controls: {enabled:true, channels:{desktop:false}} evaluates to true. This bypasses the explicit nested desktop disable for noncritical realtime. The same helper handles event_controls and family_controls, too.

**Conditional meaning:** if top-level enabled is explicitly authoritative over a nested channel choice, document that precedence and reject conflicting requests rather than silently accepting them. Otherwise it may defeat a specific desktop opt-out. This is distinct from #4054 NOTIFYDIRECTOPTOUT-BE-001, about external/direct channels. Critical breakthroughs intentionally bypass policy and must not be accidentally disabled.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT RUN:** with synthetic policy object enabled=true and channels.desktop=false, assert a documented precedence or 422 validation, with desktop opt-out respected when required. Test top-level desktop=true plus enabled=false, event and family overrides, ordinary noncritical event and explicitly critical breakthrough. Consider stored policy backward compatibility before modifying priority.

### NOTIFYDNDEMPTY-BE-001 — DND appears enabled but cannot suppress any realtime notification

**Code chain:** [notifications.py DND normalization lines 209–237](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L209-L237) accepts a standalone DND object containing enabled=true with neither always_on nor start/end. [PolicyMixin._evaluate_realtime_policy_overrides lines 81–97](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L81-L97) suppresses an enabled DND only when always_on is true or a start/end window is present and currently active. Therefore the accepted enabled-only DND configuration has no suppressive effect on any normal realtime event.

**Conditional policy decision:** This may represent a deliberately incomplete draft configuration, but there is no explicit incomplete state returned by the reviewed endpoint. Decide whether enabled-only means all-day DND, should fail validation, or should be reported as inactive. Do not refile the already logged NOTIFYPOLICYJSON-BE-001 (plain JSON settings dirty tracking), and preserve critical-event breakthrough design.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT RUN:** POST or PUT synthetic enabled-only DND and verify either 422, explicitly nonactive state, or actual suppression per approved contract. Include complete overnight window, always_on, disabled, partial window and critical-event boundary. Test with real UserPreferences in disposable database only after source gate approval.

### NOTIFYSMSERRLOG-BE-001 — provider-specific SMS failure details are written to logs without masking

**Code chain:** [ChannelsMixin.send_sms lines 118–138](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_channels.py#L118-L138) passes recipient phone and body to sms_manager.send_sms, then writes the raw response.error on provider failure, or raw string form of the exception if send_sms raises. Neither path uses the PII masker. In contrast, [the adjacent email exception handler lines 76–86](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_channels.py#L76-L86) uses mask_pii_text. SMS providers can include phone, text, vendor metadata or credentials in error objects, but no real production provider payload or leak has been inspected.

**Distinctness:** #4054 NOTIFYTELEHTTPLOG-BE-001 concerns Telegram HTTP exceptions that might expose a bot token. Draft #4060 sanitizes Telegram logging but leaves these separate SMS failure log branches unchanged. This is a conditional privacy boundary, not a demonstrated breach.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT RUN:** stub sms_manager.send_sms to return failure whose error has PATIENT_SYNTH_PHONE_41 and PATIENT_SYNTH_BODY_41, and separately to raise an exception with those strings. Capture actual logger output and prove none of these markers or fake credentials appear. Require error type/status or a properly sanitized provider code; verify normal success logging remains lean.

### NOTIFYPUSHRESULT-BE-001 — durable inbox success conflated with mobile push accepted, affecting queue-call resend cooldown

**Code chain:** [ChannelsMixin.send_push lines 140–259](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_channels.py#L140-L259) persists the canonical inbox delivery, then attempts FCM only if device_token, push_notifications_enabled and fcm_service.active are all true. The local push_sent may be false (no attempt or FCM failure), but the method returns True unconditionally when no exception escaped. The non-DB WebSocket branch likewise catches a send error but returns True. [QueuePositionNotificationService._send_notification_to_patient lines 452–483](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/queue_position_notifications.py#L452-L483) sets its sent field using bool(success or telegram_sent), where success is this return value. A [queue manual call cooldown](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/queue_position_notifications.py#L96-L200) is designed to suppress repeats only after successful delivery; its [existing regression contract](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_qposcall_resend_cooldown_contract.py) distinguishes sent=False from accepted work.

**Conditional impact:** a patient with no device token, no connected realtime socket and no successful Telegram signal may have a durable unread inbox row but no timely call alert; queue manual-resend is nevertheless told sent=True and may apply cooldown. This does NOT establish that an active WebSocket user would miss an alert or that inbox persistence is intrinsically unsuccessful. The required product decision is to distinguish inbox_persisted, WebSocket_attempted, FCM_accepted and Telegram_sent from a genuinely timely patient alert; do not switch the sender boolean without reviewing all callers. Unlike group-56 NOTIFYDELIVERYSUCCESSMETRIC-BE-001, this explicitly affects **the queue resend decision**, not just admin metrics. Draft #4060 retains this return behavior and the queue service is unchanged.

**Synthetic RED test, NOT RUN:** with synthetic called queue entry, no FCM token, no connected socket and Telegram false, assert a reviewed timely-alert result rather than false-positive sent. Ensure failed/no-channel notification does not poison the manual resend cooldown; repeat FCM accepted, FCM rejected, active WS, Telegram accepted, and two independent waiting-to-called cycles. Keep queue fairness, notification dedup and recipient authorization intact. No real messages or patients.

Protected source/tests/AGENTS.md/migrations/CI/ops/config/credentials/PHI, open Draft #4060, staging/production and deployment/merge remain read-only or denied. ## Continued source Code Review — group 59: cross-account HTTP cache, mobile booking notifications and dental Telegram markup

**Reviewed date:** 2026-11-13 Asia/Tashkent. **Latest main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d). This group reviews four **new and source-backed candidate root causes** in the active notification API cache, mobile patient booking producer and dental price approval producer. Read actual present main models/callers, old merged #3961/#4049/#4052 audits, existing groups 47–58 here, parallel Draft #4054 and proposed unmerged fix [#4060](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4060). No live application tests, browser sessions, SMS/Telegram or patient records were accessed.

| ID | Priority | Separate failure mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYCACHEPRINCIPAL-FE-001 | P1 conditional / privacy | Module-level notification HTTP result/in-flight cache uses URL only and is not invalidated on auth principal change; one staff account can receive the previous account's cached inbox payload |
| NOTIFYMOBILEDOCTOR-BE-001 | P1 conditional / active mobile booking | Notification sender reads nonexistent Doctor.name, catches the exception and silently returns empty result; mobile booking acknowledges success without a confirmation notification |
| NOTIFYMOBILECLOCK-BE-001 | P2 conditional / appointment time | Push confirmation formats date-only Appointment.appointment_date with hour/minute, yielding 00:00 rather than the separate stored appointment_time |
| NOTIFYDENTTELEHTML-BE-001 | P2 conditional / Telegram delivery | Dental price-override message interpolates unescaped reason/details/name into Telegram HTML, so literal angle brackets or markup can break parsing or distort content |

### NOTIFYCACHEPRINCIPAL-FE-001 — 15-second notification HTTP cache is not keyed or cleared by authenticated identity

**Exact source:** [frontend/src/api/services.ts lines 24–85](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/services.ts#L24-L85) keeps both resolved results and in-flight promises in **module-global Maps keyed only by endpoint + query string** (e.g. the same /notifications/inbox URL). There is no access token, user ID or authentication generation in the key. The module exports clearNotificationQueryCache, but it is called by notification mutations and specific WS frames, not by the authenticated principal transition. [frontend/src/stores/auth.ts setToken and clearToken](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/stores/auth.ts) do not invalidate this module cache. Normal [HeaderNew logout](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/layout/HeaderNew.tsx#L730-L752) uses the same-tab SPA auth.clearToken path, not a forced full reload. This source path is unchanged in open Draft #4060.

**Conditional reproduction mechanism:** User A opens inbox, caching its REST payload. Within 15s A logs out and User B logs in on the same tab; B asks the same URL with same filters before any WS frame invalidates the cache. fetchNotificationQuery returns A's **previously authenticated JSON without making a B-authorized request**. The backend's correct current-user SQL ownership cannot protect data that never went to the backend. This is distinct from [merged #4049 NOTIFYIDENT-FE-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4049), which covers the **notification provider's in-memory inbox state surviving SPA account switches**, and from this report's group-51 NOTIFYQUERYEPOCH-FE-001, which covers **old in-flight GET repopulating a cleared cache**. Even if both those defects were fixed independently, a resolved cache entry still leaks across principals without identity-aware keys and login/logout invalidation. No actual patient disclosure is asserted.

**Synthetic fail-first test — NOT RUN:** stub apiRequest to resolve an inbox for synthetic User A with marker A_SYNTH_ONLY, invoke notificationsService.getInbox, perform normal auth.setToken/auth.clearToken transition to User B in the same JS module lifetime without a full reload, then invoke the same getInbox URL. Require that B never receives A_SYNTH_ONLY from cached or in-flight state, and that a fresh principal-scoped REST request occurs. Test both a settled A response and a pending A request; an independent cache epoch protects late resolutions, as separately tracked under NOTIFYQUERYEPOCH-FE-001. Confirm B's cache remains effective within B session and no auth tokens are logged or included in exported cache keys.

### NOTIFYMOBILEDOCTOR-BE-001 — active mobile appointment confirmation fails before any channel dispatch because Doctor.name does not exist

**Exact source chain:** [mobile_api.py book_mobile_appointment](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/mobile_api.py#L405-L550) creates a real scheduled Appointment and calls notification_sender_service.send_appointment_confirmation(db, appointment.id). [notifications_pkg/_appointments.py lines 193–199](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_appointments.py#L193-L199) starts by evaluating appointment.doctor.name whenever a doctor exists. The mapped [Doctor model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L71-L160) has User relationship and specialty/cabinet fields but **no Doctor.name mapped field or @property**. The real mobile booking endpoint itself correctly derives doctor.user.full_name in its response; the notification helper does not. [notifications_pkg/_appointments.py lines 255–257](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_appointments.py#L255-L257) catches broad Exception and returns {} rather than sending any push, SMS, email or Telegram. The booking endpoint ignores that result. The same old expression remains in open Draft fix #4060.

**Conditional impact:** for a normal booked visit with a related Doctor row, the notification helper evaluates a nonexistent attribute and sends **zero confirmation channels** while the booking remains successful. This is source-observed; no production user notification failure measured. Distinct from #4054 NOTIFYCONFIRMPUSH2X-BE-001 (two pushes when the helper runs successfully) and group-58 NOTIFYPUSHRESULT-BE-001 (meaning of a push result after canonical persistence).

**Synthetic fail-first test — NOT RUN:** use an actual mapped synthetic Doctor linked to a synthetic User and synthetic Patient Appointment; invoke the real notification singleton from mobile booking. Verify that it does not raise/catch AttributeError before the dispatch and that a named doctor comes from User.full_name (or a reviewed fallback), without leaking recipient identity. Stub all external channels, require expected one canonical inbox/FCM attempt and policy-approved external attempts, and verify booking returns its normal response without falsely reporting delivered confirmation. Confirm zero-send behavior before repair; preserve slot reservation and patient isolation. This is a P1 candidate because it affects the **active mobile booking path**, not because a live outage was measured.

### NOTIFYMOBILECLOCK-BE-001 — push confirmation displays midnight rather than the booked slot time

**Exact source:** [Appointment model date and time columns](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L36-L52) store appointment_date as Python date and appointment_time separately as nullable HH:MM string. [notifications_pkg/_appointments.py lines 197–230](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_appointments.py#L197-L230) computes the push label visit_date_str by calling strftime with "%H:%M" on date-only appointment_date **before** it correctly combines appointment_date with appointment_time for the separate send_visit_confirmation call. A minimal standard-library mechanism check (no project test) showed Python date(2026,11,14).strftime("%d.%m.%Y в %H:%M") renders "14.11.2026 в 00:00". Therefore the push message can say appointment at midnight while the email/SMS branch receives the actual slot.

**Conditional significance:** this mechanism is independently reachable when appointment.doctor is null, or after the P1 Doctor.name attribute is repaired; it is currently masked for typical doctor-owned bookings by that earlier exception. It is **not** the same issue as #4054's duplicate push event, because incorrect time remains wrong even when one push is sent. Date-only bookings with no specified time need a truthful "time not assigned" presentation rather than an invented 00:00.

**Synthetic fail-first test — NOT RUN:** synthetic appointment_date=2026-11-14 and appointment_time="15:30", connected patient with stub push/other channels, invoke canonical appointment confirmation after bypassing/fixing Doctor.name source defect. Assert every rendered channel uses 15:30, never 00:00; repeat date-only appointment_time=None, invalid time, and local time formatting (no ungrounded timezone conversion). Keep separate canonical appointment slot values and do not modify persisted dates just to repair display text.

### NOTIFYDENTTELEHTML-BE-001 — dental price approval notification inserts unescaped user data into Telegram HTML

**Exact source chain:** [DentalApiService.send_price_override_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/dental_api_service.py#L155-L242) builds a Telegram message from doctor.user.full_name, service.name, price_override.reason and price_override.details **with direct string interpolation and no HTML escaping**, then calls notification_sender_service.send_telegram_message(..., parse_mode="HTML"). [notifications_pkg/_reminders.py send_telegram_message](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_reminders.py#L105-L134) forwards parse_mode to Telegram service/bot. A valid free-text reason such as "цена < 100000" or a literal malformed tag can be rejected as invalid entity markup or interpreted as formatting rather than clinical/staff text. The sender only sets notification_sent=true when a provider reports success; the approval itself is still persisted.

**Conditions and distinction:** if a provider sanitizes entities elsewhere, a live failure may not occur, but the active source has no explicit escape boundary before Telegram HTML. Distinct from old #3892 DENTPRICE-BE-001 (missing TelegramUser lookup and false success), this PR group-58 NOTIFYSMSERRLOG-BE-001 (SMS exception PII in logs), and #4054 NOTIFYJINJASANDBOX-BE-001 (Jinja templates). The open Draft #4060 changes the send_telegram_message contract to require db and treat user_id as an authenticated app User ID, while this dental caller still passes a linked Telegram chat ID **without db**. That is a **separate prospective blocker in unmerged #4060**, not evidence of a merged change; alert its implementing agent independently.

**Synthetic fail-first test — NOT RUN:** create a synthetic pending dental override whose reason contains literal "<", "&" and an unmatched "<i>", and whose details include nontrusted text. Stub a Telegram HTML validator that rejects malformed entities, and assert the intended literal content is safely escaped while intentional trusted formatting (if any) survives. Verify source recipient is an active registrar-owned TelegramUser link, no cross-patient data or raw PII in logs, and notification_sent changes only after provider acknowledgment. For Draft #4060 separately test the actual argument signature and chat-owner mapping so a reviewed HTML fix cannot mask a newly introduced no-db failure.

## Continued source Code Review — group 60: Telegram inline URL, reminder callbacks, false send acknowledgments and Markdown

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13 Asia/Tashkent. **Latest main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d). Inspected real aiogram bot, mounted role-protected Telegram endpoints, reminder and confirmation keyboard producers, and the existing #3892 twentieth review group including TGCONFIRM-BE-001 through BE-004. Compared prior #3961, #4049, #4052, groups 47–59 of this report, parallel #4054 and source in unmerged Draft fix #4060. **Four new source-observed candidate findings; none was runtime-tested.**

| ID | Priority | Distinct issue |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYTGPHONEURL-BE-001 | P1 conditional | All confirm-visit inline keyboards include unsupported tel: URL scheme, risking rejection of the entire Telegram message |
| NOTIFYTGREMINDCB-BE-001 | P2 | Telegram appointment-reminder action buttons emit commands the active bot dispatcher does not recognize |
| NOTIFYTGFALSEACK-BE-001 | P2 | Telegram HTTP endpoints report successful send when Bot API send failed and helper swallowed the boolean |
| NOTIFYTGMARKDOWN-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Telegram uses legacy Markdown parser but renders double-asterisk headers and raw unescaped user/service content |

### NOTIFYTGPHONEURL-BE-001 — Telegram inline keyboard contains invalid tel: target

**Evidence:** [FormattingMixin._create_telegram_keyboard](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_formatting.py#L99-L123) always adds a third inline button with a tel: link to CLINIC_PHONE. [RemindersMixin.send_confirmation_reminder](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_reminders.py#L77-L94) and [FormattingMixin._send_telegram_invitation](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_formatting.py#L140-L173) pass the whole keyboard to [ClinicTelegramBot.send_confirmation_invitation](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L45-L83). The [official Telegram Bot API InlineKeyboardButton.url specification](https://core.telegram.org/bots/api#inlinekeyboardbutton) defines HTTP or tg:// destinations; tel: is not an allowed scheme. A failing button can prevent the full confirmation/reminder from being sent.

**Scope:** conditional on configured Telegram provider and linked recipient; an actual remote Telegram rejection was not tested. The reminder worker is a reachable producer using TelegramUser link even if the initial patient Telegram invitation has a separate historical Patient.telegram_id linkage bug. Distinct from #3892 DOCINVITE-BE-001 and TGCONFIRM-BE-001..004, which address patient linkage/callback state rather than the invalid outgoing inline URL.

**Synthetic RED regression, NOT RUN:** use mocked configured Bot API validator to require allowed URL schemes for every inline button, then submit one synthetic pending-visit invitation/reminder. Assert provider accepts and the confirm/cancel buttons remain intact; render contact number as plain text or approved HTTPS target. Include missing clinic phone and expired token; no real patient send.

### NOTIFYTGREMINDCB-BE-001 — reminder produces unmatched callback commands

**Evidence:** [ClinicTelegramBot.send_appointment_reminder lines 393–419](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L393-L419) creates confirm_ followed by appointment ID and cancel_ followed by appointment ID; the [mounted Telegram reminder endpoint](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L291-L317) calls it. Yet [bot.handle_callback lines 262–293](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L262-L293) recognizes only confirm_visit: and cancel_visit: token commands, plus queue/help; for both reminder button payloads it responds with unknown command and does not change visit state.

**Distinction:** prior [TGCONFIRM-BE-003](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/3892#issuecomment-6726838798) addresses a *recognized* cancel_visit token button that only changes text rather than database state. Here **both appointment-reminder buttons never reach the confirmation handler at all**. An appointment numeric ID is not a bearer capability and must not be treated as one.

**Synthetic RED regression, NOT RUN:** construct a synthetic authorized reminder with real active endpoint, capture its keyboard, feed both button callbacks through the true bot dispatcher and assert recognized, owner-safe actions or non-actionable UI until valid signed tokens exist. Include another patient's appointment ID, expired capability, queue status and callback replay.

### NOTIFYTGFALSEACK-BE-001 — reminder/lab Telegram HTTP routes mask provider send failure

**Evidence:** [ClinicTelegramBot.send_notification lines 369–389](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L369-L389) catches sendMessage exceptions and returns False. Its [send_appointment_reminder](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L393-L419) and [send_lab_results_ready](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L421-L448) await but discard that result, implicitly return None. Their [two mounted, role-protected endpoints](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L291-L343) also discard helper result and unconditionally return success unless an uncaught exception occurs. Therefore failed Bot API sends can produce HTTP 200 with a success message.

**Distinction:** this is about two **Telegram HTTP endpoint acknowledgments**. It is not the in-app dashboard count of group-56, FCM/queue cooldown of group-58, or #4054's external provider canonical-state reconciliation. No actual delivery failure/complaint established.

**Synthetic RED regression, NOT RUN:** configure mock bot as present but raise a synthetic Telegram provider exception from send_message. Invoke both endpoints with synthetic staff authorization and assert correct failure status rather than sent. Cover one successful message and retryable failure without leaking synthetic patient content to logs.

### NOTIFYTGMARKDOWN-BE-001 — legacy Markdown rendered with incompatible markup and unescaped variable fields

**Evidence:** [FormattingMixin._format_telegram_message lines 70–98](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_formatting.py#L70-L98) builds a confirmation invitation with double-asterisk headings and raw patient, doctor and service names. [ClinicTelegramBot.send_confirmation_invitation lines 63–81](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L63-L81) sends it with parse_mode Markdown. [send_appointment_reminder lines 393–419](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L393-L419) also uses a double-asterisk heading and interpolates unescaped doctor text; [send_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L369-L389) uses the same legacy Markdown mode. [Official Telegram Bot API Markdown style](https://core.telegram.org/bots/api#markdown-style) documents single-asterisk bold and delimiter escaping, not double-asterisk bold. Raw underscore, bracket, star or backtick in a staff-entered name or service can corrupt markup or produce entity parse failure.

**Scope:** bot provider behavior is untested and clinical link reachability is conditional. Distinct from group-59 NOTIFYDENTTELEHTML-BE-001, which affects the dental Telegram HTML path, and the earlier TGCONFIRM callback defects.

**Synthetic RED regression, NOT RUN:** assemble a synthetic patient/doctor/service invitation including underscores, unmatched star and square bracket; feed through a Telegram-compatible Markdown parser/mock Bot API and require literal names plus consistent intended emphasis. Include Cyrillic and direct reminder endpoint; define consistent escaping/parser pair instead of replacing one marker blindly.
