## Continued source Code Review — group 61: patient Telegram bot queue, navigation and webhook drift

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13 Asia/Tashkent. **Current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d) (confirmed equal to prior main by exact compare). Reviewed `backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py`, `backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py`, `backend/app/services/queue_svc/__init__.py`, `backend/app/services/queue_svc/_operations.py`, `frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts` and `publicSiteRouteEntries.ts`. Checked prior group 60 and parallel #4054 for overlap. These are **four static code-review candidates**; runtime and negative tests were **not executed**.

| ID | Priority | Candidate and scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYTGBOTJOIN-BE-001 | P1, reachable when bot is configured | Patient bot queue callback calls a non-existent QueueBusinessService.join_queue method |
| NOTIFYTGBOTLINKS-BE-001 | P2, conditional on patient using the link | Patient bot WebAppInfo links do not match canonical patient routes, including a staff-only legacy alias |
| NOTIFYTGWEBHOOKROT-BE-001 | P2 conditional | Successful remote setWebhook followed by failed DB persistence leaves a secret mismatch and denies subsequent updates |
| NOTIFYTGQUEUEWINDOW-BE-001 | P2 conditional, currently masked by join failure | Hardcoded host-local 07:00–08:00 queue menu and no time/ownership check on later button callbacks |

### NOTIFYTGBOTJOIN-BE-001 — patient Telegram queue cannot invoke the canonical queue writer

**Source:** [ClinicTelegramBot._handle_queue_callback](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L297-L344) executes `await self.queue_service.join_queue(specialist_id=..., phone=..., telegram_id=..., patient_name=...)`. `self.queue_service` is [QueueBusinessService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L24-L38), composed from [CoreMixin, OperationsMixin, HelpersMixin](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/queue_svc/__init__.py#L14-L22); its actual writer in [OperationsMixin](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/queue_svc/_operations.py#L2165-L2200) is synchronous `join_queue_with_token(db, *, token_str, ...)`, not `join_queue`. The mounted HTTP `POST /queue/join` [supplies the DB and token](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/queue.py#L190-L215). The bot callback catches the `AttributeError` in a general exception handler and shows a generic failure. Merely renaming the method is unsafe: it would still lack a DB session and QR token/identity contract.

**Negative regression, NOT RUN:** configure a synthetic patient bot, press a queue specialty callback at an allowed time, assert no `AttributeError`/generic 500 and prove the exact canonical queue allocation result through the authorized token/session path. Include unlinked Telegram identity, wrong specialty, duplicate, closed QR window, and no PHI logs. Do not introduce a tokenless alternate allocator as a hotfix.

### NOTIFYTGBOTLINKS-BE-001 — patient buttons target missing or staff-only frontend routes

**Source:** `/start` builds [`FRONTEND_URL/mobile`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L111-L139); `/appointment` builds [`/appointment` and `/appointments`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L202-L224); lab-ready builds [`/lab-results`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L424-L446). The [canonical frontend registry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts) plus [public-site entries](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/routing/publicSiteRouteEntries.ts) have no `/mobile`, `/appointment` or `/lab-results` route; public booking is `/booking` or `/ru/booking`. `/appointments` is a [legacy redirect to `/clinical/appointments`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L1444-L1465), which permits Admin/Doctor/Registrar, not Patient. The bot's links therefore cannot be assumed to open a permitted patient screen.

**Distinction:** separate from group-54 NOTIFYLABDEADROUTE-FE-001, which concerned canonical notification `/lab/results`, not the patient bot's `/lab-results` WebAppInfo.

**Negative regression, NOT RUN:** extract every real bot WebAppInfo target under a test FRONTEND_URL, resolve it through canonical registry with Patient role, assert registered and authorized destinations with the expected booking/results workflow; negative cases must fail on staff-only and unknown routes. Do not silently redirect patients to staff results.

### NOTIFYTGWEBHOOKROT-BE-001 — remote webhook secret can diverge from committed local secret

**Source:** [set_telegram_webhook](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L125-L164) generates a new secret, awaits `telegram_bot.setup_webhook(url, secret_token=...)`, *then* calls `crud_telegram.update_telegram_config` or `create_telegram_config`. [Webhook ingress validator](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L70-L96) accepts only the DB-stored `webhook_secret`. If Telegram accepts the new remote secret but the following DB write fails, Telegram sends the new secret while ingress still compares against old or unset value; every legitimate webhook can be rejected until repaired.

**Conditionality:** requires failure of the DB persistence step after remote success. No evidence of such an outage, and no live Telegram call was made. This is separate from webhook request secret validation itself, which is present and fail-closed.

**Synthetic failure-injection regression, NOT RUN:** mock Telegram `set_webhook` success followed by a DB commit/constraint failure. Assert either a compensating rollback or a safely recoverable, clearly reported transition that avoids silently wedging all updates; assert no raw secret in logs. Repeat initial setup and rotation, then verify incoming header against committed secret only.

### NOTIFYTGQUEUEWINDOW-BE-001 — displayed time gate does not guard the callback and is host-local

**Source:** [handle_queue](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L158-L200) checks `datetime.now().hour` and allows a selection menu only between hardcoded 07:00 and 08:00. The already-issued Telegram button can be clicked later: [handle_callback](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L258-L288) immediately routes `queue_*` to [_handle_queue_callback](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L297-L344), with no repeat check. The canonical [join_queue_with_token](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/queue_svc/_operations.py#L2165-L2200) belongs to the clinic queue policy and token/day context, not this hardcoded bot clock. A host timezone change can also make the visible menu window diverge from clinic local policy.

**Conditionality:** actual out-of-window allocation is **not proven**: the bot currently fails earlier on its missing `join_queue` call, and a corrected canonical join should reject invalid tokens/windows. Treat this as an independent callback/UX policy gap to test during the required queue fix, not an observed fairness bypass.

**Synthetic negative regression, NOT RUN:** freeze clock at 06:59, 07:30, 08:01 in clinic timezone and a host in another timezone, create menu at 07:30 and replay stale button at 08:01. Assert the callback goes through backend-owned queue policy with valid session/token and cannot allocate when closed. Do not infer Doctor.id, QueueProfile.id or resource identity from hardcoded small integers.

Backend/frontend source, AGENTS.md, other PRs, tests, migrations, CI configuration, secrets, patient data, deploy, production, merge remain denied/read-only. `ai/langgraph/scripts/run_agent_gate.ps1` not run for this docs change; it **must** run with actual scope before any queue/Telegram/auth/routing source repair. ## Continued source Code Review — group 62: manual Telegram recipient integrity, schema-to-template drift and dual-bot token contract

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-13 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d) (exact main compare verified). **Source scope:** [Telegram API endpoints](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py), [Telegram DTOs](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py), [aiogram patient bot](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py), [admin Telegram settings](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/admin_telegram/_settings.py), [canonical credential resolver](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram_token_store.py), and [router mount](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py). Reviewed historical #3892 group 19/20, prior #4059 groups 47–61, and remediation tracker #4063 for duplicates. **Four additional source-observed candidates; no executable test performed.**

| ID | Priority | Candidate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYTGCHATSCOPE-BE-001 | P1 conditional/privacy | Staff-submitted clinical reminders/lab-ready data can be sent to an arbitrary numeric Telegram chat ID without server-side patient/result/appointment recipient ownership validation |
| NOTIFYTGDOCTORKEY-BE-001 | P2 | Typed appointment DTO exposes doctor_name while the Telegram reminder renderer reads doctor and emits the generic fallback |
| NOTIFYTGLABKEY-BE-001 | P3 conditional/UX & privacy policy decision | Typed lab DTO exposes test_type while the Telegram lab-ready renderer reads test_name and falls back to generic text |
| NOTIFYTGBOTCONFIG-BE-001 | P2 conditional/architecture | Parallel mounted aiogram bot routes are enabled only from environment TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, independent of admin-configured canonical DB patient bot credential |

### NOTIFYTGCHATSCOPE-BE-001 — manual Telegram clinical routes trust arbitrary recipient and patient text

**Exact code path:** [`POST /telegram/bot/send-appointment-reminder` and `send-lab-notification`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L288-L343) admit Doctor/Registrar/Admin or Doctor/Lab/Admin respectively and pass `reminder_data.user_id` or `lab_data.user_id`, plus caller-created appointment/lab payload, straight to the outbound bot. They require **no DB dependency or lookup to confirm** that the numeric chat is an active, permitted `TelegramUser` for the actual appointment/patient/lab result and **no doctor/specialty ownership check** on the referenced record. [The request schemas](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L107-L168) describe `user_id` as Telegram user/chat ID and allow the appointment/lab details to be supplied by the client. [Bot.send_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L367-L386) forwards that integer as `chat_id` without additional ownership checks. [Router mount](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py) makes this a live API surface.

**Risk and conditions:** a permitted staff API caller can provide a chat ID not linked to the clinical recipient and cause a Telegram message containing the supplied doctor, time, or result text to be sent there, provided that chat accepts bot messages. Source cannot prove an actual misdelivery and these staff roles are authorized to work with some notification data; the missing patient-to-chat and record-to-actor binding is the concern. This is **distinct from group-60 NOTIFYTGFALSEACK-BE-001** (false provider-success responses) and from public bearer confirmation identity attribution in old TGCONFIRM-BE-004. A generic admin free-text message route may have a different approved contract; audit and repair the *two clinical* routes first.

**Fail-first synthetic tests — NOT RUN:** with two synthetic linked patients/chats A and B, create a clinician-owned appointment/lab record for A. Send the clinical route with A's content and B's chat ID, assert deny / no Bot API send. Try unlinked/blocked/disabled TelegramUser, a caller outside patient ownership, mismatched appointment/lab ID, and legitimate A→A success. Validate permissions, consent, no PHI in error logs, and document whether these legacy manual routes should instead be removed in favor of the canonical patient-notification service.

### NOTIFYTGDOCTORKEY-BE-001 — doctor name in appointment reminder is always replaced by fallback for typed inputs

**Exact code path:** [AppointmentData](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L112-L131) accepts `doctor_name`, but not a declared `doctor` field; [`send_appointment_reminder`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L288-L305) converts this to `appointment_data = reminder_data.appointment.model_dump(exclude_none=True)`. [ClinicTelegramBot.send_appointment_reminder](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L389-L405) reads `appointment_data.get("doctor", "Врач")`. Therefore a normal typed request with `doctor_name="Дежурный врач"` still renders `Врач: Врач`, omitting the supplied doctor's name. It can work only if a caller sends an undeclared `doctor` extra accepted by `model_config={"extra":"allow"}` — the declared field does not work.

**Distinction:** separate from group-60 unrecognized reminder callback IDs, group-59 mobile appointment date/time discrepancy, and group-61 bot WebAppInfo path drift. This is body-to-template key parity.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** POST a synthetic reminder with only documented `doctor_name`, `date`, `time`, valid linked test recipient, stub Bot API, and assert rendered doctor equals the request's expected display text rather than fallback; test the legacy extra `doctor` compatibility path if retained and non-user-controlled canonical doctor projection.

### NOTIFYTGLABKEY-BE-001 — lab-ready type key mismatch, with privacy decision before revealing the test name

**Exact code path:** [LabResultsData](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L141-L168) exposes `test_type` and related identifiers but has no declared `test_name`. [The mounted API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L319-L333) dumps the typed results model; [ClinicTelegramBot.send_lab_results_ready](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L424-L434) reads `results_info.get("test_name", "Анализы")`. A normally typed `test_type="..." ` is ignored and the message always uses `Исследование: Анализы`, unless the undocumented extra `test_name` is supplied.

**Scope / privacy:** a generic "analyses ready" message may actually be **preferable**, because Telegram is a nonclinical channel and the repository strategy discourages detailed clinical disclosure. Thus this is a **P3 schema/consumer contract inconsistency requiring explicit privacy-product decision**, not an automatic instruction to place the raw lab test type in Telegram.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** submit documented `test_type` with a synthetic non-sensitive label and confirm consistent policy: either intentionally generic output with no unneeded raw client test field, or an explicitly approved privacy-safe server-derived label. Validate no clinical values/patient identifiers leak. Do not mechanically substitute `test_type` into patient chat before privacy approval.

### NOTIFYTGBOTCONFIG-BE-001 — admin-configured patient bot token does not activate the other mounted aiogram surface

**Exact code path:** [The admin settings API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/admin_telegram/_settings.py#L70-L151) stores a configured patient bot token in `telegram_configs` via `store_patient_bot_token`; [the canonical resolver](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram_token_store.py#L1-L32) defines precedence DB → legacy clinic setting → env and is used by the separate modern patient bot. However [ClinicTelegramBot.__init__](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/telegram/bot.py#L24-L38) uses only `settings.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` **once at process import** and sets `self.bot=None` if absent. [The mounted `/telegram/bot/webhook` and send routes](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/telegram_bot.py#L88-L105) check that env-only bot. They cannot be brought up by setting only the canonical token in admin UI. Conversely, changing the DB bot token does not rotate this pre-created aiogram object.

**Important distinction:** the token-store module **explicitly documents the two-bot split and env-only contract for `services/telegram/bot.py`**. This is not proof that the canonical patient bot is broken, or that its polling/webhook service lacks DB-token support. It is a *parallel legacy mounted API/operational contract gap*, conditional on expecting admin-configured bot settings to cover `/telegram/bot`. Owner must decide whether to deprecate/migrate that legacy mount or make its configuration behavior explicit; do **not** accidentally load the same Telegram bot token into competing polling/webhook consumers.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** set synthetic canonical DB token with environment unset, check canonical patient bot remains enabled through its chosen transport while the mounted legacy aiogram API reports accurately whether it is intentionally disabled; then rotate DB token and assert no stale credential is presented as current. Test the documented 2-bot split and avoid live Telegram registration/secret leakage.

### Group 62 status, dedup, scope and handoff

## Continued source Code Review — group 63: notification policy preservation and accessible settings

**Review date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d); verified identical to current main. Source checked: [NotificationPreferences.tsx](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx), [Switch.tsx](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/ui/macos/Switch.tsx), [notifications.py](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py), [notification policy evaluator](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py), predecessor audits, tracker #4063, AGENTS.md and installed React reviewer guidance. **Three source-based candidates; no executable tests or live operations.**

| ID | Severity | Source-observed mechanism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYPREFCONTROLLOSS-FE-001 | P2 conditional | Saving a visible setting may discard other server-supported event/family restrictions the UI does not display |
| NOTIFYPREFREFRESHLOSS-FE-001 | P2 | Clicking channel Refresh clears unsaved anti-noise policy edits without confirmation |
| NOTIFYPREFSWITCHA11Y-FE-001 | P2 accessibility | All notification policy and channel toggles expose the same accessible name "Toggle setting" |

### NOTIFYPREFCONTROLLOSS-FE-001 — hidden policy controls dropped from the full PUT

[normalizePolicyDraft](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L310-L376) starts with hardcoded five supported family keys and three event keys, **ignores any others** in the server policy response and reduces every control to a desktop boolean. [buildPolicyPayload](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L378-L414) serializes that reduced map. [handleSave](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L743-L789) sends it in a full policy PUT when any visible policy toggle changes. The [server policy API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L851-L879) accepts other event/family keys through [_normalize_notification_policy_payload](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/notifications.py#L184-L267) and **replaces** `security_settings["notification_policy"]`, rather than merging per key. The [runtime policy](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L99-L124) evaluates those event/family keys. A pre-existing rule for an unlisted synthetic event, e.g. `desktop:false`, can be erased by saving an unrelated visible toggle, potentially re-enabling noncritical realtime alerts.

**Caveat/dedup:** older `NOTIFYPOLICYJSON-BE-001` documents a **separate** backend plain-JSON dirty-tracking problem; this frontend payload loss matters if the PUT persists or that backend issue is fixed. This is not group-58 `NOTIFYDESKTOPPRECEDENCE-BE-001` (control precedence). No real user policy has been inspected.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** seed a synthetic user policy containing `event_controls.unlisted_event.desktop=false`, `family_controls.other.desktop=false` plus visible controls. Load UI, flip one visible control, save, read back through actual API with disposable DB, assert unlisted restrictions survive and visible change persists. Include concurrent update and a second user. Do not silently broaden notification delivery/privacy policy.

### NOTIFYPREFREFRESHLOSS-FE-001 — channel Refresh destroys unrelated policy draft

[loadSettings](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L653-L699) unconditionally executes `setPolicyLoaded(false)`, `setPolicySettings(null)`, `setPolicyDraft(null)` **before** fetching channels. The visible channel [Refresh button](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L802-L823) calls `loadSettings({force:true})` without checking `hasPolicyChanges`; it is disabled only by `saving`. A user can edit DND/mute/family controls, refresh channels, and lose the unsaved policy draft without warning. A failing channel GET still clears it before returning an error.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** load channel settings and policy, change DND and a family toggle, click channel Refresh, assert the dirty policy is preserved or a real discard-confirmation is required. Repeat with failed channel request; positive controls for explicit Reset or confirmed discard. This is distinct from policy PUT losing hidden keys and from the intentionally destructive Reset button.

### NOTIFYPREFSWITCHA11Y-FE-001 — policy switches are indistinguishable to assistive technology

[PreferenceRow](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/settings/NotificationPreferences.tsx#L594-L616) displays its label in a sibling div, but renders `<Switch checked=... onChange=... />` **without** label/aria-label/aria-labelledby. [Switch](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/ui/macos/Switch.tsx#L102-L119) sets `aria-label` to a fallback `"Toggle setting"` when neither a string label nor explicit aria-label is provided. Every channel, DND, family and event toggle using PreferenceRow consequently has this identical spoken name, and the neighboring label div is not associated with the input.

**Fail-first synthetic test — NOT RUN:** render two distinct PreferenceRows; check `getByRole("switch",{name:/specific setting/i})` for each and verify accessible name and keyboard toggle, disabled and translation behavior. Pass an accessible name or bind visible label to the input; avoid changing global Switch semantics for unrelated users. Consult React reviewer skill for accessibility and effect/state considerations.

## Continued source Code Review — group 64: active Email/SMS admin UI and backend notification contract

**Reviewed:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), verified equal to current main before edit. **Source inspected:** [EmailSMSManager.tsx](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx), [frontend API runtime](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/runtime.ts) and [shared Axios client](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/client.ts), [email_sms_enhanced.py](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py), [backend notifications schemas](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py), [router mount](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py), [CSRF middleware](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/middleware/csrf_middleware.py), [active route registry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts), existing notification audit and tracker #4063. Read installed React review guidance and repo code-security CSRF rule. **Four source-verified candidates; no browser, HTTP, provider or executable regression performed.**

| ID | Priority | Static finding |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSURL-FE-001 | P1 conditional / complete admin channel outage | Seven Email/SMS admin calls use browser-root relative /email-sms routes instead of backend /api/v1/email-sms and configured API origin |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSCSRF-FE-001 | P2, conditional on CSRF_ENABLED=1 | Six POST requests use raw fetch without X-CSRF-Token or shared interceptor, so valid URL alone would still be rejected with 403 |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSTESTDTO-FE-001 | P2 | Test-email and test-sms submit JSON body, but corresponding FastAPI endpoints require to_email/phone from query string |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSBULKDTO-FE-001 | P2 | Bulk sender passes list of strings, while backend requires list of typed RecipientData objects; email body and batch controls also mismatch |

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSURL-FE-001 — admin Email/SMS panel bypasses the canonical backend URL resolver

**Evidence:** [EmailSMSManager.tsx lines 191–312](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L191-L312) calls `fetch('/email-sms/statistics')`, `fetch('/email-sms/templates')`, `fetch('/email-sms/test-email')`, `fetch('/email-sms/test-sms')`, `fetch('/email-sms/send-bulk-email')` or `send-bulk-sms`, and `fetch('/email-sms/reset-statistics')`. Because these are origin-root-relative URLs, the browser targets the **SPA origin**, not the configured API host, and they omit `/api/v1`. The [active frontend route registry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/routing/routeRegistry.ts#L984-L1010) routes `/admin/notifications` to this actual component, not a dead legacy file. [Backend api router](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py#L469-L472) mounts the [implemented handlers](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py) below `/email-sms` on the API v1 router, so the canonical path is `/api/v1/email-sms/*`. [frontend runtime](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/runtime.ts#L100-L122) defines a configurable split-origin API and `buildApiUrl`; the shared [Axios client](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/client.ts#L115-L120) uses that resolved base.

**Consequence / qualification:** In documented split-origin deployments, all seven controls can reach the wrong host (SPA fallback, 404 or non-JSON HTML) rather than the backend. Even same-origin setups need an explicit `/email-sms` reverse-proxy exception to make these unprefixed calls work. No real site routing/rewrite configuration was tested, so **do not assert observed production failure**.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** render the active Admin route under a Vite SPA origin distinct from `VITE_API_BASE_URL`, intercept fetch/axios requests, and assert statistics/templates plus every send/reset use exact `<configured backend origin>/api/v1/email-sms/*`. Control same-origin/staging and cross-site; avoid reliance on undocumented rewrite. Do not touch real recipient destinations.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSCSRF-FE-001 — Email/SMS admin mutations do not satisfy the CSRF double-submit contract

**Evidence:** The [six POST call sites](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L222-L313) use raw `fetch` headers `Content-Type` and `Authorization` (or Authorization alone), **never** `X-CSRF-Token`; they bypass [Axios request interceptor](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/api/client.ts#L332-L401) that bootstraps and attaches the CSRF header. In [CSRFMiddleware.dispatch](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/middleware/csrf_middleware.py#L180-L248), a non-exempt POST with cookie but absent header fails `403 missing_header`; none of the [exemption prefixes/exact paths](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/middleware/csrf_middleware.py#L113-L142) applies to `/api/v1/email-sms/*`. Production enables CSRF by default in [backend main](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/main.py). Bearer credentials do **not** provide an exemption in this middleware. Unlike the URL issue, this persists after correcting the API origin/path.

**Conditionality/dedup:** If CSRF_ENABLED=0, this particular 403 does not occur. The core problem is the missing contract on mutation requests, not that the middleware is insecure. This is independent of NOTIFYEMAILSMSURL-FE-001 and analogous to previously fixed cashier raw-fetch problems, not a new count of those cashier files.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** with strict CSRF middleware enabled and a synthetic admin, seed a fake CSRF cookie, issue each UI mutation through corrected backend URL, assert `X-CSRF-Token` is attached and matches the cookie, no 403. Include missing/mismatch negative controls. For bulk recipients use synthetic provider stubs so no real message is sent; test 401 refresh and error responses via shared client.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSTESTDTO-FE-001 — test-email/test-sms request envelope mismatches FastAPI signature

**Evidence:** [frontend test-email/test-sms](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L222-L267) JSON-serialize `{to_email,subject,message}` and `{phone,message}` into the request body. [FastAPI email handler](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L644-L650) declares `to_email: str` with no `Body`, and [SMS handler](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L700-L705) declares `phone: str` similarly. FastAPI binds these scalar parameters from the query string; a JSON body does not satisfy the required `to_email`/`phone` query parameter, producing 422 before the provider. The frontend merely parses response JSON into the result object.

**Not the same as:** missing /api/v1 route or CSRF; **even with both fixed**, the documented UI payload remains unaccepted. The actual test providers/recipient addresses were not accessed.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** supply fake email and phone through frontend UI to a FastAPI TestClient route; assert exact request query binding and 200 with stubbed provider. Assert body-only yields 422 as a negative control; validate safe string handling and no real delivery. Do not silently change public endpoint parameter location without API contract/migration consideration.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSBULKDTO-FE-001 — bulk recipients and content/control fields are not backend contract-compatible

**Evidence:** [parseRecipients](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L134-L138) returns `string[]`; the [bulk state](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L166-L185) stores that type; [sendBulkNotification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L271-L290) sends `recipients: bulkForm.recipients` unchanged as JSON. Backend [SendBulkEmailRequest / SendBulkSmsRequest](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L300-L348) require **`list[RecipientData]`**, where elements are objects with `email` or `phone`. Supplying raw string elements fails Pydantic model validation (422) before dispatch. Independently, Email payload sends `message` but email schema accepts `html_content`/`text_content` (and `template_data`) instead, and both backend handlers require `batch_size` and `delay_between_batches` as **query** parameters with defaults [email](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L339-L371), [SMS](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L387-L414); the frontend puts them in the JSON body so requested operator timing settings are ignored even after the recipient shape is corrected.

**Boundary / risk:** This is one grouped **bulk UI↔API contract drift**, not separate findings for each wrong key. Until fixed, test sends fail validation; if only recipients are corrected, requested content/timing can still be ignored or empty. Avoid any real bulk emails/SMS in validation.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** use 2 synthetic email and 2 synthetic SMS contacts; run UI submit and assert serialized recipient objects carry `email` or `phone` keys, validate Pydantic model acceptances, and verify requested `batch_size` and `delay_between_batches` match FastAPI query values. For email body require approved `text_content`/template (not merely an unrecognized `message`), and prohibit sending if required content absent. Stub underlying provider with captured calls, include invalid recipient/error cases, zero real messages.

## Continued source Code Review — group 65: registrar notification delivery, acknowledgment and reporting

| ID | Priority | Condition / impact |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYREGFALSEACK-BE-001 | P1 conditional | Canonical record failure leaves `success=true`; all registrar routes calculate `sent_count=0` despite successful fan-out |
| NOTIFYREGPRICEFLAG-BE-001 | P1 conditional | DoctorPriceOverride `notification_sent` and timestamp are committed despite canonical notification failure |
| NOTIFYREGDEPTCAST-BE-001 | P2 conditional/recipient scope | Department selection is not used to filter registrars or canonical role fan-out; a department-targeted alert can reach all registrars/admins |

### NOTIFYREGFALSEACK-BE-001 — claimed delivery despite canonical failure and permanent zero sent_count

**Source:** [RegistrarNotificationService._record_registrar_canonical_event lines 55–96](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L55-L96) returns the boolean from `send_registrar_event_notification`. The canonical [sender returns false for missing eligible recipients or delivery exception](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L121-L199), as well as unsupported event types. However [notify_new_appointment lines 273–296](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L273-L296), [notify_queue_status lines 445–487](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L445-L487), [notify_system_alert lines 540–561](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L540-L561) and [send_daily_summary lines 610–625](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L610-L625) all return `success=True` even when `canonical_created=False`; all return `results=[]` with a comment that canonical delivery handles fan-out. [Registrar notifications endpoint responses](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L276-L347) compute `sent_count = len(result.get("results",[]))`, which is unconditionally zero from these service return shapes. The [active admin panel](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx#L99-L169) shows a success toast using that `sent_count` for normal/test/daily sends.

**Impact and scope:** Failed persistence may look like a successful dispatch and be excluded from retry/observability; successful canonical fan-out still displays 'sent to 0'. A `canonical_created=True` boolean alone is **not** a confirmed count of delivered WebSocket/Telegram messages; distinguishes persistent inbox creation from transport delivery and keeps counts evidence-backed. Independent of similarly named Telegram false ACK in group 60 and Email/SMS contract defects in group 64.

**Fail-first synthetic regression, NOT RUN:** mock `send_registrar_event_notification` to return false, with eligible registrars present, exercise `POST /registrar/notifications/system-alert`, `/appointment`, `/daily-summary` with synthetic fixtures, assert no success ACK and no fake nonzero delivery. Return true with 2 authorized synthetic recipients and assert accurate contract-defined `recorded_count` rather than `len([])`, or explicitly `sent_count=null` until measured; verify UI doesn't say 'sent to zero'. Distinguish canonical DB recorded from live channel outcomes.

### NOTIFYREGPRICEFLAG-BE-001 — persistent price-override notification audit flag set after failed create

**Source:** [notify_price_change lines 354–388](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L354-L388) stores boolean `canonical_created = await _record_registrar_canonical_event(...)` then **unconditionally** assigns `price_override.notification_sent=True` and `notification_sent_at=datetime.now(UTC)` and calls `self.db.commit()`. [DoctorPriceOverride model lines 88–93](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/doctor_price_override.py#L88-L93) defines durable `notification_sent` and `notification_sent_at` columns. This can mark the audit record as notified even when the canonical producer returns false (e.g. no role recipients or delivery exception). The service itself returns `success=True` regardless, but the **durable business audit state** is a separate defect from NOTIFYREGFALSEACK-BE-001.

**Impact conditions:** Actual downstream suppression based on the flag has not been proven: do not claim a specific retry job is already skipping it. Nevertheless consumers relying on `notification_sent` will see a false state. The separate dental_api_service path also sets these flags; it is not part of this finding unless verified independently.

**Fail-first synthetic regression, NOT RUN:** create pending synthetic DoctorPriceOverride with `notification_sent=False` and `notification_sent_at=None`; monkeypatch canonical notification to return false, invoke service, commit and fresh-read DB. Assert both fields remain unchanged and API reflects failure or retry state. Repeat when canonical create succeeds and assert exact persisted timestamp. Exercise transaction failure and idempotent retry, with no real patient content.

### NOTIFYREGDEPTCAST-BE-001 — department-specific alert scope ignored before canonical fan-out

**Source:** [get_registrars_by_department lines 173–192](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L173-L192) conditionally checks `department` but the branch contains a TODO and `pass`; it always returns **all active registrar users**. The [system-alert API accepts caller-provided `department`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L276-L298); [notify_system_alert](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L490-L561) calls the no-op filtered getter but then creates a canonical event with `roles=["registrar","admin"]` and puts the department **only in metadata**. The [canonical sender](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L285-L345) delegates role resolution to `send_canonical_notification_to_roles`, which [resolves recipients by role, not metadata department](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L121-L200). This sends to all eligible registrar/admin user recipients regardless of requested department; `GET /registrar/notifications/registrars?department=...` likewise enumerates all active registrars.

**Conditional policy:** Product intent for `department` is not yet established; it may be deliberately a **label only**, in which case rename/document and remove the misleading filter helper/recipient count. If meant to **target a department**, the current behavior breaks scoping and potentially overexposes text supplied by an admin. No evidence of an actual cross-department privacy incident. No cross-clinic claim.

**Fail-first synthetic regression, NOT RUN:** create registrars A/B in separate synthetic departments plus admin, send to A and inspect durable deliveries by principal; assert only policy-authorized recipients, with explicit admin exception if approved. Confirm department-less broadcast behavior and 403 permission boundary. Also GET the filtered registrar list. Do not assume arbitrary profile department implies authorization without owner-approved policy.

### NOTIFYREGSTATSZERO-BE-001 — registrar statistics screen is backed by permanent zero placeholder

**Source:** [GET /registrar/notifications/stats lines 352–377](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L352-L377) returns literal `total_sent=0`, `successful_deliveries=0`, `failed_deliveries=0`, `channels_stats` all zeros and `recent_notifications=[]` regardless of `days` or database rows. The function has a TODO for real aggregation. [RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx lines 88–95 and 365–429](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx#L88-L95) loads and displays these metrics as if current operational counts; its [send handlers](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx#L99-L169) refresh those zero stats after successful sends. The parallel canonical platform already records persistent user inbox deliveries; however an actual delivery/realtime success rate must be defined separately before exposing numerical reliability metrics.

**Consequences:** Admin could be misled into believing there was zero registrar notification activity even after recorded events. Stats should not conflate a successful persisted inbox item with SMS/Telegram acceptance or browser display. Separate from the partially placeholder daily-clinic summary metrics (not audited or counted in this group).

**Fail-first synthetic regression, NOT RUN:** seed two synthetically recorded registrar deliveries and one failed attempt if failure records exist; query `GET /registrar/notifications/stats?days=7` and confirm nonzero relevant counters with proper temporal scope and explicit unavailable/untracked channel metrics, never fabricated zeros. Confirm `days` validation and a genuinely empty period yields zero. UI should label unavailable statistics as unknown rather than data-backed zeros.

## Continued source Code Review — group 66: enhanced Email/SMS clinical / financial truth and delivery result semantics

**Date/time:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), checked identical before append. **Source actually inspected:** [email_sms_enhanced endpoint](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py), [EmailSMSEnhancedService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py), [PaymentData schema](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py), [Payment model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/payment.py), [existing financial ownership tests](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_email_sms_enhanced_payment_owner.py), [existing audit #4059, tracker #4063 and old #3892 discussions]. The installed full-story verification skill and root AGENTS.md were read; no live provider, DB or execution access was used. **Four new independent source observations; not test-proven production incidents.**

| ID | Priority | Candidate finding |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSLABPHANTOM-BE-001 | P1 conditional / clinical communication | Lab-ready Email/SMS endpoint constructs fictitious lab result metadata and never reads or binds the requested lab result |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSPAYTRUST-BE-001 | P1 conditional / financial messaging | Payment-confirmation Email/SMS can be sent without payment ID, or with unverified client amount/status even when ownership checked |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSEMPTYOK-BE-001 | P2 / no-op ACK | Three enhanced send helpers treat empty or entirely unavailable delivery-channel outcomes as successful |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSBULKACK-BE-001 | P2 / bulk operational status | Both bulk endpoints report top-level success despite every provider send failing; only nested details expose zero sends |

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSLABPHANTOM-BE-001 — lab-results-ready notification not backed by actual laboratory record

**Evidence:** The authenticated [POST /send-lab-results-enhanced handler at lines 205–268](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L205-L268) accepts `patient_id` and `lab_results_id`, looks up **only the Patient**, then explicitly comments that lab retrieval is a TODO and creates a stub `lab_data`: `id=lab_results_id`, `test_type="Общий анализ крови"`, `collection_date=now()`, `has_abnormalities=False`. It does not verify that a lab report exists, belongs to that patient, is finalized/released, or has actual flagged results. [EmailSMSEnhancedService.send_lab_results_enhanced, lines 413–474](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L413-L474) blindly uses those values in template data, assigns a patient-access link, and sends through email/SMS when configured and contact exists. The lab endpoint allows roles Admin/Lab/Doctor; role authorization is not proof of the relation `lab_results_id → patient_id`.

**Conditional consequences:** A staff user can trigger a real **"results ready"** message for a nonexistent, wrong-patient, unapproved or clinically abnormal result; where a template renders `has_abnormalities`, it receives the unsupported false value. Not claiming the actual clinical report is changed, or that a real patient has received a false notification. This finding is specifically the fake clinical data/record-binding boundary, **not** group 62's unrelated Telegram arbitrary chat ID issue.

**Fail-first test, NOT RUN:** with synthetic patient A / result owned by B and a separate unsigned/not-ready result, a stubbed provider must receive **no** email/SMS and API must return a correctly scoped failure. For released result owned by A, assert test code, collection date, abnormality flag and authorization link derive from the real lab record, not `now()`/hardcoded constants. Test Doctor/Lab authorization from the actual clinic policy; never use real PHI or send a message.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSPAYTRUST-BE-001 — financial confirmation uses unverified caller-supplied payment facts

**Evidence:** [POST /send-payment-confirmation-enhanced, lines 272–336](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L272-L336) requires Admin/Cashier and accepts `PaymentData` in the body. The [helper _payment_id_from_payload / _ensure_payment_confirmation_belongs_to_patient, lines 87–125](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L87-L125) returns without DB access when both `payment_id` and `id` are absent. When provided, it validates only Payment existence and linked Visit.patient_id; it does **not** verify amount, currency, payment status `paid`, refund/void state, or source-of-truth ownership of the rest of the body. [PaymentData schema](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L257-L270) makes payment ID and amount optional and permits extra fields. [Payment model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/payment.py#L50-L95) has authoritative amount/currency/status. [email service lines 475–537](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L475-L537) composes a **payment-confirmed** email/SMS using the supplied amount, currency and payment_method, not fetched Payment values. Existing [unit test test_send_payment_confirmation_enhanced_preserves_no_id_payload](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_email_sms_enhanced_payment_owner.py) specifically allows no-ID `PaymentData(amount=125000)`; a second test checks different-patient ownership when ID supplied. These tests do not establish payment proof or amount correctness.

**Consequence and qualifications:** An authorized staff action (including unintentional bad request) may produce a patient-facing false amount or confirmation of an unpaid payment; does not grant anonymous endpoint access or alter ledger balance. A positive client ACK only establishes email/SMS provider outcome, not actual payment. Need owner-approved policy for manually entered payment notifications versus verified receipts; do not silently ban legitimately permitted cash operations without design signoff. Distinct from existing Telegram and cashier financial review IDs, and separate from group 66's no-channel ACK.

**Fail-first test, NOT RUN:** synthetic paid Payment P of 125,000 UZS and pending Payment Q; submit no payment ID, fake amount and mismatched currency separately; enforce the approved policy, and for verified receipt require matching paid DB Payment + patient/visit and generate message from authoritative amount/status. Test forged `payment_id` of other patient and refunds; validate no send before checks. Provider must be mocked.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSEMPTYOK-BE-001 — success on zero reachable channels in three enhanced send helpers

**Evidence:** [send_appointment_reminder_enhanced lines 351–412](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L351-L412), [send_lab_results_enhanced lines 413–474](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L413-L474), and [send_payment_confirmation_enhanced lines 475–537](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L475-L537) only add results for channels where `channel == "email" and patient_data.email` or `channel == "sms" and patient_data.phone`. In all three, `results['success']=all(result.get('success',False) for result in results['channels'].values())`. With **no eligible address/phone** or unsupported/empty channel list, `results['channels']={}` and Python `all([])` evaluates True. The [three FastAPI route responses](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L127-L336) surface this successful result and "sent" wording.

**Conditionality/dedup:** Even with fully configured providers, a patient lacking both contacts yields zero attempts while `success=True`. This is distinct from the unconditional group 65 registrar `success=True` ignoring `canonical_created`, and from group 60 Telegram false success. It is one common helper-family root; do not multiply count by endpoint.

**Fail-first test, NOT RUN:** call all three helpers for synthetic patient with no email/phone, `channels=["email","sms"]`, and separately unsupported channel. Assert `attempted_count=0`, `success=False` or explicit `skipped/no_channel` contract, no transport calls, and non-misleading API wording. Positive case with one eligible channel verifies explicit selected behavior and partial failure semantics.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSBULKACK-BE-001 — top-level bulk success cannot mean that provider delivered

**Evidence:** Both [POST /send-bulk-email, lines 339–384](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L339-L384) and [POST /send-bulk-sms, lines 387–430](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L387-L430) await the entire `service.send_bulk_*()` operation, then **unconditionally** respond `{"success": True, "message": "... запущена", "details": result}`. [Service send_bulk_email and send_bulk_sms, lines 241–350](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L241-L350) explicitly count `total`, `sent`, `failed`, and `errors`, including `sent=0`, `failed=len(recipients)` when credentials/provider sends fail. Thus a fully failed batch still produces successful top-level ACK; message says 'started' even though it awaited completion. Users or automation checking `response.success` alone miss total failure; no code indicates an asynchronously queued job.

**Conditionality/dedup:** This is a **backend result semantics** defect after valid payload parsing, not group 64 frontend recipient-shape mismatch or group 65 registrar acknowledgment. Do not assume every failed provider response has been logged outside this in-memory result.

**Fail-first test, NOT RUN:** synthetic valid `RecipientData` payload (2 recipients), mocked send_email_enhanced/send_sms_enhanced returning false for both; call actual API handlers and assert no `success=True` and no 'launched' message; verify `details.sent=0,failed=2` and partial-success case `sent=1,failed=1` with an explicit status contract. Ensure no actual mail/SMS is sent. Avoid changing HTTP success codes without frontend error handling decision.

## Continued source Code Review — group 67: enhanced Email/SMS fallback, batch limits, attachment truth and statistics

**Date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), verified identical before append. **Sources actually reread:** [EmailSMSEnhancedService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py), [Email/SMS FastAPI endpoints](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py), [notification Pydantic schemas](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py), [attachment security regression tests](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_email_attachment_path_traversal.py), [active EmailSMSManager.tsx](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx), and previously filed #4059/#4063 entries and historical #3892 comments. Skills-based full-story verification guidance and current AGENTS.md read. No executable test or provider request executed.

| Distinct new ID | Priority | Source-based finding |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSFALLBACKHTML-BE-001 | P1 conditional / HTML injection | Fallback HTML interpolates unescaped patient/message data outside Jinja autoescape when template rendering fails |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSBATCHBOUND-BE-001 | P2 / API validation and resource controls | Bulk endpoint allows zero/negative/unbounded batch sizes and unbounded interbatch delays |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSATTACHACK-BE-001 | P2 conditional / document integrity | _add_attachment silently swallows missing/blocked attachments while parent reports successful Email send |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSSTATSVOLATILE-BE-001 | P2 conditional / operator visibility | API counters are stored only on an in-process singleton; restarts/workers reset/divide the statistics |

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSFALLBACKHTML-BE-001 — missing HTML escaping on template-render error path

**Evidence:** [EmailSMSEnhancedService.__init__ lines 91–94](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L91-L94) creates Jinja Environment(autoescape=True) for the normal template path. [`_render_email_template` lines 538–556](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L538-L556) catches **all template/load/render exceptions** and substitutes `self._get_basic_email_template(template_data)`; [`_get_basic_email_template` lines 612–652](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L612-L652) directly inserts `data.get('patient_name')` and `data.get('message')` inside HTML via Python f-strings. [_escape_html defined at lines 39–44](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L39-L44) is **not used here**, and Jinja autoescape cannot protect the separate f-string fallback. [Service send_email_enhanced lines 105–165](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L105-L165) attaches the result as `MIMEText(..., "html", "utf-8")` and can send normally after fallback.

**Precondition and risk:** The normal Jinja template must be missing/broken or the renderer must fail; a patient-controlled name or supplied `message` may then introduce HTML anchors, markup or remote-resource URLs into a clinical mail. No claim that modern mail clients execute script or that a patient received injected HTML. A report file documents templates but template loading on the deployment has not been validated; don't assert unconditional fallback. Distinct from preexisting `NOTIFYJINJASANDBOX-BE-001` (template expression safety) and group 64 frontend API drift.

**Synthetic RED specification — NOT RUN:** replace `template_env.get_template` with a fake raising TemplateNotFound; call `_render_email_template("appointment_reminder", {"patient_name":"<b>unsafe</b>","message":"<a href=...>x</a>"})`; assert HTML representation contains `&lt;b&gt;`/`&lt;a` and no raw markup, while plain-text rendition preserves patient text. Include user-controlled values through an actual synthetic appointment path; no outgoing SMTP. Fix should use html.escape or render fallback through an escaped Jinja environment, never disable normal autoescape; decide whether missing clinical template must instead fail closed to preserve clinical details.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSBATCHBOUND-BE-001 — unchecked request-size and delay knobs alter dispatch semantics

**Evidence:** [bulk-email endpoint lines 339–369](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L339-L369) and [bulk-SMS endpoint lines 387–415](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L387-L415) use `batch_size: int = Query(50/100)` and `delay_between_batches: float = Query(1.0/0.5)` with **no positive/lower/upper bounds**. The [service loops email lines 241–295](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L241-L295) and [SMS lines 298–348](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L298-L348) use `range(0,len(recipients),batch_size)`, `asyncio.gather(*tasks)`, then `await asyncio.sleep(delay_between_batches)`. Consequences directly follow code: with nonempty recipients and `batch_size=0`, Python range raises ValueError (caught by route's generic handler -> HTTP 500); `batch_size=-1` causes no iteration (returns `total>0,sent=0,failed=0`), yet top-level API ACK is success (cross-reference group 66, but distinct root). A very large positive batch size allows **up to the validated recipient cap (1000)** concurrent provider calls in one request, circumventing the intended batch throttle; large delay can make an awaited HTTP request take excessive time or time out after earlier batches; negative delay effectively suppresses intended pacing (without assuming a Python exception). `@limiter.limit("1/5minute")` limits requests, **not individual recipients or concurrent tasks**.

**Distinctness:** Group 64 observes frontend controls placed in JSON body instead of query; group 66 reports an unconditional bulk top-level success ACK even when providers fail. This group's root is absent server-side constraints after parameters arrive in the correct query.

**Synthetic RED specification — NOT RUN:** use synthetic validated recipient objects and stubbed service; via FastAPI test client submit `?batch_size=0`, `=-1`, huge positive, negative / huge delay and assert 422 for invalid parameters **before any provider call**; positive bounded values dispatch exactly N recipients with no more than the cap per chunk. Do not create actual mail/SMS or sleep for unbounded durations. Establish bounded configuration from service throughput policy; tests include same behavior for email and SMS.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSATTACHACK-BE-001 — attachment failure is hidden behind successful send

**Evidence:** [`send_email_enhanced` lines 148–161](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L148-L161) awaits `_add_attachment` for each provided attachment, then sends the MIME message and increments `emails_sent` if the SMTP send itself succeeds. [`_add_attachment` lines 584–611](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L584-L611) silently returns for unsupported attachment type, path with a directory component (since `safe_path=os.path.basename(path)`, `safe_path!=path`), or any file read/MIME parsing exception; it logs the exception but **does not raise or return an explicit failure**. [Schema SendCustomEmailRequest lines 343–389](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L343-L389) accepts legitimate relative paths such as `uploads/image.png` (checks neither absolute nor `..` and permitted syntax); the [existing path traversal test](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_email_attachment_path_traversal.py) explicitly accepts `uploads/image.png` at the schema layer while testing only basename-only happy path at the service layer. Thus even a schema-approved path with a directory is **dropped** by the service; an unreadable filename also gets dropped without any propagated failure.

**Conditional impact:** A user/admin may receive a `success=true` HTTP response and an actual SMTP email **without the requested attachment**. This is a message/document integrity and operator expectation defect, not a path-traversal finding; the security path restrictions must be retained. No evidence that any clinical report PDF is sent through this custom-email attachment feature in practice. Do not solve by allowing arbitrary relative filesystem traversal.

**Synthetic RED specification — NOT RUN:** construct a valid request with `attachments=[{"type":"image","filename":"scan.png","path":"uploads/scan.png"}]` then a missing/corrupt clean filename; mock file access and SMTP transport. Assert that either a validated, storage-root-confined asset is attached or the request is reported as unsuccessful **without sending the incomplete email**; check unsupported type is rejected early. Keep traversal and symlink security tests. No real local patient files or outbound emails.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSSTATSVOLATILE-BE-001 — Email/SMS dashboard uses ephemeral process-local counters

**Evidence:** [EmailSMSEnhancedService.__init__ lines 76–103](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L76-L103) initializes `self.stats` as in-memory counts, increased only in local [email and SMS send functions lines 155–166 and 220–239](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L155-L239). [get_statistics/reset_statistics lines 656–684](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L656-L684) read/reset that dict; [singleton at lines 687–694](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L687-L694) exists only in **one Python process**, not shared database/Redis. [GET /statistics lines 504–525 and POST /reset-statistics lines 529–551](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L504-L551) expose those counters as operating statistics; [EmailSMSManager.tsx](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx) displays them in an admin screen.

**Conditional impact:** Multiple Uvicorn/Gunicorn worker processes each get an independent `email_sms_enhanced_service` singleton. A request to `GET /statistics` may hit a worker with zero sends while another processed notifications. Restart resets all counters; `reset-statistics` affects only the serving worker. If stats are **intentionally per-process, since-start diagnostics**, the UI and API should disclose this and not label them as clinic-wide durable totals. This is independent from group's 65 registrar `/registrar/notifications/stats` literal-zero placeholder: this path has real but non-aggregated process data.

**Synthetic RED specification — NOT RUN:** construct two independent service instances and drive mock successful sends on only one; assert the API contract either documents per-instance count/worker identity or uses durable/shared aggregation as product-approved; verify restarts and reset across workers. Track actual attempted/accepted provider sends versus delivered inbox outcomes (not conflated). No external provider or DB migration in this docs-only PR.

## Continued source Code Review — group 68: enhanced Email/SMS appointment DTO, SMS templates, HTTP client lifecycle and optional amount

**Date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), verified unchanged before append. **Sources actually re-opened:** [enhanced Email/SMS API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py), [EmailSMSEnhancedService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py), [canonical Appointment ORM](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py), [CRUD getter](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/crud/appointment.py), [PaymentData request model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py), [actual appointment SMS template](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/sms/appointment_reminder_sms.txt), [lab SMS template](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/sms/lab_results_ready_sms.txt), [payment SMS template](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/sms/payment_confirmation_sms.txt), missing [backend/templates/email/sms/appointment_reminder_sms.txt](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/tree/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/email) verified by a 404 exact-path fetch, prior groups 47–67 and tracker #4063. | Distinct new ID | Priority | Source-based finding |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSAPPTFIELDS-BE-001 | P1 / reachable endpoint break | Appointment reminder reads non-existent ORM attributes after successful patient lookup; generic 500 precedes any send |
| NOTIFYEMAILSMSSMSLOADER-BE-001 | P1/P2 conditional / message content | SMS Jinja template lookup targets the Email loader's `sms/` child while real SMS templates live in sibling `templates/sms/`; silent fallback sends generic messages |
