### NOTIFYEMAILSMSAPPTFIELDS-BE-001 — enhanced appointment reminder incompatible with live Appointment ORM

**Source chain:** The mounted [POST /email-sms/send-appointment-reminder-enhanced handler, lines 127–203](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L127-L203) uses [crud_appointment.get_appointment lines 533–535](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/crud/appointment.py#L533-L535), which returns a **plain SQLAlchemy `Appointment` entity** from `db.query(Appointment).first()`. After finding its patient, the endpoint constructs `appointment_data` using `appointment.doctor_name`, `appointment.specialty`, `appointment.date.strftime(...)`, `appointment.time`, `appointment.cabinet` (lines 170–177). However [Appointment ORM fields lines 38–100](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L38-L100) define `doctor_id`, `doctor` relationship, `appointment_date`, `appointment_time` and `department` relationship; its only extra properties near [lines 116–133](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L116-L133) are `department_key`/`department_name`. The queried ORM does **not** expose the endpoint's five referenced attributes. The first `appointment.doctor_name` evaluation raises `AttributeError` for an ordinary ORM record. The handler catches non-HTTPException and converts to 500 before calling the Email/SMS service. This is a **different root** from group 66 lab-results placeholder and group 64 frontend API route/payload mismatches. A separate legacy Telegram reminder endpoint appears to read similar fields; do not count that as a second finding without independently adjudicating its surface.

**Consequences:** Any valid synthetic appointment with patient and allowed role reaches this erroneous mapping and cannot send an enhanced reminder; a doctor may still be correctly rejected by ownership checks earlier (e.g. existing unit test). Not claiming an observed deployed 500, and no patient message was sent during audit.

**Synthetic fail-first — NOT RUN:** create a real in-memory ORM `Appointment(id, patient_id, doctor_id, appointment_date, appointment_time,...)` and related Doctor/Patient, authorize registrar/admin and doctor-owner paths, call endpoint with mocked notification service and assert it receives actual doctor full name/specialty, formatted canonical date/time, cabinet or explicit fallback; assert 200 only when expected. Include missing doctor relation and rescheduled/cancelled record policy. Keep existing forbidden-doctor test; do not mock an invented Appointment with `doctor_name`.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSSMSLOADER-BE-001 — loader searches SMS templates in wrong directory and falls back to contentless messages

**Source chain:** [EmailSMSEnhancedService.__init__ lines 91–94](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L91-L94) sets `self.template_env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader("templates/email"), autoescape=True)`; [`_render_sms_template` lines 557–567](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L557-L567) calls `self.template_env.get_template(f"sms/{template_name}.txt")`. Relative to the configured root, that searches `templates/email/sms/<template>.txt`. Actual source files were fetched under `backend/templates/sms/appointment_reminder_sms.txt`, `backend/templates/sms/lab_results_ready_sms.txt`, `backend/templates/sms/payment_confirmation_sms.txt`. Exact `backend/templates/email/sms/appointment_reminder_sms.txt` and `backend/templates/email/sms/lab_results_ready_sms.txt` fetches yielded 404. In a working directory with `templates/email` pointing at the repo's intended email folder, this lookup cannot find those SMS templates. The catch-all then returns [`_get_basic_sms_template` lines 652–654](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L652-L654), which uses `template_data.get("message","Уведомление от клиники")`. But the three business send helpers [lines 351–537](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L351-L537) populate `doctor_name`, `appointment_time`, `test_type`, `formatted_amount`, etc., not `message`; therefore fallback says only `Programma Clinic: Уведомление от клиники`, while `send_sms_enhanced` can count provider success.

**Conditionality/dedup:** If a deployment provisions a different custom directory structure the expected path may exist; there is **no such file in the pinned repo**. The effective impact is silent loss of appointment/lab/payment details from SMS rather than total channel transport failure. Distinct from group 67's **HTML fallback escaping** (different loader and content) and group 66 clinical stub data (still wrong even if SMS loader fixed).

**Synthetic fail-first — NOT RUN:** run against cloned repository template files with service initialized in the documented backend working dir; for each SMS business template, assert rendered output contains formatted date/time, actual test_type/ready URL or formatted_amount and is not the generic fallback. Simulate missing files, require explicit failure or product-approved safe fallback with meaningful details and a detectable error. Use mocked SMS post and synthetic patient/payment/lab data; no real outbound SMS.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSCLIENTLIFE-BE-001 — HTTPX AsyncClient instances not released by SMS sender

**Evidence:** [send_sms_enhanced lines 183–239](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L183-L239) builds an outbound request and executes `await httpx.AsyncClient().post(...)` directly, then returns in multiple success/failure branches. It neither uses an `async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:` context nor calls `await client.aclose()` in a finally block. One new client is created per SMS and its lifecycle is unmanaged by the service, including exceptions and bulk sends [lines 298–350](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L298-L350) which can invoke hundreds concurrently. The response is checked for status and success, so this is not a missing HTTP response handling finding.

**Risk/condition:** Resource cleanup may rely on implementation-specific garbage collection or finalization rather than deterministic close. Under sustained/bulk load, connections and file descriptors may remain open longer than intended and exhaust resources. Static inspection cannot prove a live leak or quantify resource growth. Distinct from prior missing batch bounds group 67 and prior HTTP webhook transport mistakes.

**Synthetic fail-first — NOT RUN:** monkeypatch `httpx.AsyncClient` with an async context-tracking fake; one success, provider error and malformed JSON path must all close client once; bulk path should reuse or explicitly close clients according to supported pooling policy, with no real SMS. Validate async contexts and bounded concurrency without token/PII logs.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSAMOUNTNONE-BE-001 — optional PaymentData.amount raises 500 in presentation formatting

**Evidence:** [`PaymentData` Pydantic model, lines 255–267](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L255-L267) accepts `amount: int | float | None = Field(None, ge=0)`, including an omitted or explicit JSON null. [POST /send-payment-confirmation-enhanced lines 272–335](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L272-L335) calls `payment_data.model_dump()`, preserving `"amount": None`, and passes it into the service. [send_payment_confirmation_enhanced lines 475–503](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L475-L503) evaluates `f"{payment_data.get('amount', 0):,.0f} {payment_data.get('currency','UZS')}"`. For a dict with an explicit `amount: None`, `dict.get` returns None, not the default 0, and Python cannot apply `:,.0f` to None. Service raises `TypeError` before any channel sends, and endpoint maps it to generic HTTP 500.

**Distinctness:** Group 66 NOTIFYEMAILSMSPAYTRUST-BE-001 addresses **financial truth**, payment ID optionality and caller-controlled amount/status; this finding is a **Pydantic vs formatting type contract** with valid-but-empty payload turning into 500 rather than clean 422 or approved no-amount behavior. Product owner must choose required amount or authoritative DB-backed amount; do not invent a zero-value payment receipt to hide the crash. Existing [test_send_payment_confirmation_enhanced_preserves_no_id_payload](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_email_sms_enhanced_payment_owner.py) passes `amount=125000` and mocks the service, so it does not exercise this crash.

**Synthetic fail-first — NOT RUN:** submit `PaymentData()` and `PaymentData(amount=None)` with fake patient, mock provider, assert HTTP 422 or explicit non-send policy (not 500); test `amount=0` only if owner-approved to distinguish free services and cancellation; valid paid amount must format from actual payment record according to future canonical financial contract. No real money, patient or SMS.

## Continued source Code Review — group 69: enhanced Email/SMS recipient validation, test-mail HTML, catalogue accuracy and priority contract

| New distinct ID | Priority | Finding |

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSRECIPIENTKEY-BE-001 — optional recipient address reaches hard-key lookup

**Evidence:** [RecipientData model, lines 76–89](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L76-L89) declares both `email: EmailStr | None = None` and `phone: str | None = None`, with **no cross-field or per-channel requirement**. [SendBulkEmailRequest and SendBulkSmsRequest, lines 306–337](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L306-L337) both accept `list[RecipientData]`, capped to 1000, without checking a mandatory channel-specific contact. [Bulk handlers lines 339–417](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L339-L417) create `[r.model_dump(exclude_none=True) for r in body.recipients]`; the missing optional key stays **absent**. [send_bulk_email lines 255–276](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L255-L276) accesses `recipient['email']` while [send_bulk_sms lines 310–331](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L310-L331) accesses `recipient['phone']` before creating and awaiting the batch. An otherwise schema-valid body `{"recipients":[{"phone":"+998900000123"}],"subject":"x","text_content":"x"}` in the **Email** endpoint or `{"recipients":[{"email":"fake@example.com"}],"message":"x"}` in the **SMS** endpoint reaches `KeyError`, caught by route as generic HTTP 500. A mixed batch may already have created coroutine objects before the missing-key failure, but no proof of an actual send before `gather`; do not claim messages were delivered.

**Independence and scope:** This is backend 500 from *schema-valid RecipientData*, not group 64's frontend `list[str]` payload mismatch, group 67 invalid batch step nor group 66 top-level success on all provider failures. Only Admin is allowed by the specific bulk routes; no unauthenticated request claim.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** using FastAPI TestClient and fake EmailSMS service/provider, submit both single-channel-missing-contact bodies, mixed valid+missing recipients, and empty `RecipientData()`. Assert deterministic 422 with channel-specific field error *before any transport* or an explicit documented skip/partial-result contract, not HTTP 500. Accept fully valid Email/SMS recipient lists and verify no new calls after an invalid element, as the policy requires.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSTESTHTML-BE-001 — HTML in test-mail route bypasses template escaping

**Evidence:** [POST /test-email, lines 639–695](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L639-L695) takes `message: str` from a **query** parameter and constructs a literal HTML f-string with `<p>{message}</p>`. The same text is separately included in a plain-text alternative. It then calls [send_email_enhanced, lines 105–165](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L105-L165), which attaches given `html_content` as `MIMEText(...,'html', ...)` without escaping (reasonable for explicitly trusted HTML content, but not for this "message" text API). `_escape_html` exists at [service lines 39–44](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L39-L44) yet is not used by test-mail endpoint.

**Preconditions and security qualification:** Route is `require_roles("Admin")`, so no nonadmin injection shown; the administrator must submit or paste HTML-containing message, perhaps from an untrusted source, and an SMTP provider must be configured. Mail HTML can then contain unexpected markup, clickable phishing links or external-resource references. This does **not** imply browser XSS or reliable JavaScript execution by email clients, and none was attempted. This is a separate hard-coded route from group 67's error-only basic HTML fallback, and distinct from old Jinja sandbox policy.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** call test-email endpoint with admin-synthetic text like `<a href="https://example.invalid">x</a>`, stub outbound `send_email_enhanced`, assert HTML displays literal escaped text, while plain-text content remains unchanged; no message sent. If HTML intentionally allowed, change and validate API contract as trusted admin HTML with explicit mode, not implicitly unescaped `message`.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSTEMPLATECAT-BE-001 — fake template catalogue and inactive pagination

**Evidence:** [GET /templates handler, lines 553–635](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L553-L635) has an explicit TODO that template retrieval from DB/filesystem is not implemented, and creates a hardcoded `templates={"email":[...3], "sms":[...3]}`. Although the HTTP signature exposes `limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500)` and `offset: int = Query(0,ge=0)`, neither is applied to returned arrays. The advertised variables list for e.g. payment Email includes only `patient_name, amount, currency, payment_date`, whereas the [actual payment_confirmation.html](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/email/payment_confirmation.html) uses `formatted_amount`, `payment_method`, `transaction_id`, `receipt_link` and others not listed. Likewise [SMS templates under backend/templates/sms](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/tree/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/templates/sms) are physically distinct from the service's currently configured loader (group 68), so *catalogue advertised* is not equivalent to *renderable from that loader*. [Admin EmailSMSManager loads this catalogue](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/notifications/EmailSMSManager.tsx#L214-L231) and exposes the names to operator selections.

**Impact/qualification:** Calling `/templates?template_type=email&limit=1&offset=2` will still show all three fixed Email entries, not a page. The fixed catalogue may remain suitable as an intentional supported-template allowlist, but then API docs should state this and remove misleading pagination; **do not assert additional templates actually exist**. No claim of direct external-send failure solely because of catalogue mismatch; group 68 tracks the separate SMS loader root.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** fake isolated template registry with 3 entries and request different `limit/offset` combinations. Assert either correct pagination/metadata or deliberately static contract with no pagination controls. Compare advertised variables with real render-time metadata, and check the resolved Email/SMS template files; fail visibly if nonexistent rather than silently advertising them.

### NOTIFYEMAILSMSPRIOQUERY-BE-001 — accepted priority body field ignored by custom send routes

**Evidence:** [SendCustomEmailRequest.priority in schema lines 343–352](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L343-L352) and [SendCustomSmsRequest.priority lines 397–407](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/schemas/notifications.py#L397-L407) both explicitly define `priority: Literal["normal","high"] = "normal"`. Yet [POST /send-custom-email handler lines 434–465](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L434-L465) and [POST /send-custom-sms lines 471–495](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/email_sms_enhanced.py#L471-L495) also define independent `priority: str = Query("normal")` and pass **that** directly to service, not `body.priority`. For normal JSON client `{"to_email":"fake@example.com","subject":"x","text_content":"x","priority":"high"}`, effective priority remains default `normal` unless caller separately adds `?priority=high`. With SMS similarly ignored. The service maps `high` Email to `X-Priority: 1` [lines 123–126](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L123-L126) and forwards SMS priority in JSON [lines 204–216](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/email_sms_enhanced.py#L204-L216), so the difference is observable to providers.

**Scope/dedup:** This is *custom Email/SMS route body/query mismatch*, not prior core `NOTIFYPRIORITYDROP-BE-001` (canonical priority downgrade) or group 64's different EmailSMSManager test/bulk DTO mismatch. The active EmailSMSManager currently calls test routes and bulk routes, not these custom endpoints, so do **not** claim its controls exercise this custom body priority directly. Unbounded `?priority=anything` is another potential validation gap, but is not counted separately.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** send synthetic custom Email/SMS body with `priority="high"` and no priority query via TestClient, with mocked provider; assert transport receives `high` and Email `X-Priority:1` under documented contract; try body/query disagreement to enforce one canonical rule. Invalid body values should 422 and no send, no real provider.

## Continued source Code Review — group 70: registrar notification model contracts and daily summary truth

**Date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d) checked identical when reviewed. **Actually reopened:** [registrar notification service](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py), [registrar notification routes](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py), [Appointment model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py), [DailyQueue/OnlineQueueEntry models](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py), [Visit model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py), [patient model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/patient.py), [RegistrarNotificationManager](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx), all existing #4059 groups, issue #4063 and previous review comments. ### NOTIFYREGAPPTTIME-BE-001 — appointment_time str/None is treated as datetime.time

**Evidence:** [POST /registrar/notifications/appointment, lines 147–184](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L147-L184) builds a real Appointment or Visit via `RegistrarNotificationsApiService.get_appointment_context` and calls [RegistrarNotificationService.notify_new_appointment lines 191–290](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L191-L290). It constructs a long summary with `appointment.appointment_time.strftime("%H:%M") if hasattr(appointment, "appointment_time") else "Не указано"` at [lines 270–283](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L270-L283). [Appointment ORM declares `appointment_time: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L43-L59); this attribute **always exists** on mapped Appointment objects, but values are e.g. `"09:30"` or `None`. Both throw `AttributeError` on `.strftime`. The exception is swallowed by the [method's catch-all return success false, lines 289–296](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L289-L296); no canonical event is attempted. This is separate from group 68's enhanced Email/SMS appointment DTO nonexistent-field bug (different endpoints/services), and group 65 false-positive ACK (this branch can return `success: false`).

**Consequence/qualification:** For a valid synthetic Appointment and at least one eligible registrar, this particular code path fails before inbox creation. Other earlier branches (doctor lookup or service formatting) may also fail for certain records, but they do not rescue it. For `Visit`, `hasattr(appointment, "appointment_time")` is normally false, so this exact error belongs to the `appointment` request variant. No observed deployed HTTP response or real message claimed.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** use real ORM Appointment object with patient, date and `appointment_time="09:30"`, then same with None; stub registrars and notification_sender_service; invoke service and route; assert canonical `new_appointment` event recorded with canonical formatted time or `Не указано` and a legitimate success only after canonical creation, not an AttributeError/failure. Test `appointment_type="visit"` separately to avoid conflation; do not change the stored column type.

### NOTIFYREGQUEUEORM-BE-001 — queue status builder uses removed queue attributes

**Evidence:** [POST /registrar/notifications/queue-status, lines 238–275](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L238-L275) resolves `OnlineQueueEntry` and calls [notify_queue_status lines 390–484](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L390-L484). Once a DailyQueue row is found the method reads `daily_queue.department`, then `daily_queue.doctor_id`/`daily_queue.doctor_name`. But [DailyQueue ORM lines 133–199](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py#L133-L199) has `day`, `queue_tag`, `specialist_id`, `queue_resource_id`, `specialist` relationship, and `cabinet_number`; it has none of the three referenced legacy attributes. If no DailyQueue exists, the function still reads `queue_entry.queue_number` and `queue_entry.visit_date` in message/metadata. [OnlineQueueEntry ORM lines 266–350](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py#L266-L350) defines `number` and links via `queue_id`/queue relationship, not `queue_number` or `visit_date`. Thus both normal and dangling-queue branches fail before [canonical `queue_status_changed` event](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L459-L482) and method catches exceptions to `success: false`.

**Scope:** Real appointment/queue state may still be updated elsewhere; this finding concerns *explicit registrar notification endpoint*, not the actual queue state mutation or fairness model. No inference that every automated queue event uses this method. Distinct from group 65 department cross-recipient scope and group 62 Telegram chat authorization.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** create synthetic ORM DailyQueue(day=today, queue_resource_id=..., queue_tag="lab"), OnlineQueueEntry(queue_id, number=7, patient_id), stub human registrar and notification sender; call route with `queue_entry_id`, assert correct canonical metadata uses `entry.number`, `daily_queue.day`, server-owned doctor/resource display and receives proper date; no nonexistent attributes. Test doctor-owned and resource-owned queues and missing queue row, respecting XOR owner constraints; no real queue changes.

### NOTIFYREGDAILYORM-BE-001 — daily stats always fall into all-zero exception fallback once queue query reached

**Evidence:** [POST /registrar/notifications/daily-summary lines 311–344](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L311-L344) calls [send_daily_summary lines 560–625](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L560-L625), which calls [`_collect_daily_stats` lines 732–813](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L732-L813). After counting appointments and visits, code runs `self.db.query(DailyQueue).filter(DailyQueue.queue_date == target_date).count()` [lines 770–776](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L770-L776), but actual [DailyQueue's date is `day`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py#L146-L156), not `queue_date`; no `queue_date` mapped property exists anywhere in the pinned queue model file. Python resolves the class attribute while constructing SQL expression, so `AttributeError` happens before the query, independent of whether that day has any queue rows. The entire method catches exceptions and [returns every metric zero with `comparison_text="Ошибка сбора данных"`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L795-L813), discarding earlier nonzero appointment and visit counts. Even if `queue_date` is corrected, [lines 779–783](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L779-L783) then filter on nonexistent `OnlineQueueEntry.visit_date` (actual entry belongs to `DailyQueue.day` via `queue_id`). This is **a different ORM drift** than group70 queue-status renderer. No actual DB was queried.

**Outcome:** If active registrars exist and canonical event creation succeeds, `send_daily_summary` still sends a digest-labelled event with zeroed stats and generic “ready” message. Group 65 already tracks false success/sent_count handling when canonical creation fails; that behavior is **not re-reported** here.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** use a synthetic seeded SQLite DB with 2 appointments, 1 visit, 1 Doctor- or Resource-owned DailyQueue for target date and 2 OnlineQueueEntries; assert `_collect_daily_stats` does not enter error fallback, count of `active_queues` and `online_entries` correctly scoped via canonical day/foreign key, and appointment/visit counts preserved. Inject SQL failure separately and require an explicit invalid/error state rather than an all-zero successful digest. Do not run on patient data or staging.

### NOTIFYREGDAILYZEROS-BE-001 — uncomputed financial/clinical KPIs presented as measured daily totals

**Evidence:** [`_collect_daily_stats` initializes placeholder dict lines 735–752](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L735-L752) with `new_patients, confirmed_visits, cancelled_appointments, total_revenue, paid_amount, pending_amount, average_load, unconfirmed_visits, system_errors` all 0 and comparison_text static `"Данные обновляются"`. Its only subsequent assignments [lines 754–792](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L754-L792) are counts of `Appointment`, `Visit`, `DailyQueue`, `OnlineQueueEntry` and **current** `DoctorPriceOverride.status=="pending"`; none calculates the nine other advertised KPIs or yesterday-to-today comparison. Yet [send_daily_summary constructs the rich daily financial and clinical text, lines 572–598](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L572-L598), and [canonical event metadata includes `stats`, lines 602–612](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L602-L612). After query-field bugs are fixed, missing calculations **still** output zero financial/clinical amounts even if recorded transactions/visits are nonzero, which makes zero indistinguishable from `not implemented`.

**Independence/qualification:** This is the *daily summary statistics payload*, not [group65 NOTIFYREGSTATSZERO-BE-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4059) reporting **separate** `GET /registrar/notifications/stats` all-zero delivery-channel counters; nor is it caused solely by `queue_date` error above. A number of KPIs may not have a stable canonical source yet — in that case mark them unavailable/null until product owner defines the calculation, rather than inventing revenues. No production business totals inspected or claimed.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** with synthetic appointments, payments (including partial refund/void) and visits, assert no false numeric zero for unimplemented `total_revenue`, `paid_amount`, `pending_amount`, `confirmed_visits`, etc.; derive numbers only from policy-approved canonical tables and states or explicitly `null`/unavailable. Verify the notification renderer does not label an unknown number “0 сум”. Separate fixture tests for genuinely zero sales vs unavailable data. Do not extend this documentation-only PR to payment models or migrations.

### NOTIFYREGDOCTOROWNER-BE-001 — doctor-role caller can request an event on another doctor's record

**Source chain:** [`POST /registrar/notifications/appointment` lines 147–168](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L147-L168) admits `Doctor` alongside Admin/Registrar and accepts client-selected `appointment_id` and `appointment_type`; [`POST /registrar/notifications/price-change` lines 190–216](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L190-L216) admits Admin/Doctor and client-selected `price_override_id`. Although `current_user` is authenticated by role, neither handler **uses** its id for scope checks or passes it into [`RegistrarNotificationsApiService.get_appointment_context` and `get_price_change_context` lines 31–79](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L31-L79). The repository [lines 22–50](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/repositories/registrar_notifications_api_repository.py#L22-L50) fetches each record **only by its submitted primary key**. [Doctor model stores `user_id`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L98-L119), [Appointment stores `doctor_id`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L43-L52), [Visit stores `doctor_id`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L61-L78), and [DoctorPriceOverride stores `doctor_id`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/doctor_price_override.py#L19-L44), so an unambiguous ownership check is possible but absent in this route family. The registrar canonical event fans out to staff [`notify_new_appointment` lines 277–293, `notify_price_change` lines 358–384](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L277-L384) and may contain linked patient/visit/payment metadata and physician identity. A Doctor can therefore initiate a misleading staff alert for another Doctor's record, **provided the underlying event path succeeds**.

**Caveat:** Group 70 `NOTIFYREGAPPTTIME-BE-001` independently blocks *ordinary Appointment* messages due to `str.strftime`; choosing `appointment_type="visit"` bypasses that particular crash, and price-change has another success path given valid required model values. Do not state proven exfiltration or a completed live cross-owner send. Group 45 `NOTIFYQUEUEACTOR-BE-001` covers a **different** `/notifications/send-queue-update` endpoint on arbitrary departmental queue updates; this one covers **registrar clinical/price notification by foreign DB PK** and is not a re-count of the old queue finding.

**Synthetic RED spec — NOT RUN:** seed Doctor A and B as distinct `Doctor.user_id` profiles and real Visit/PriceOverride rows owned by B, authorize A as Doctor and call both registrar endpoints with B's IDs; assert **403 before any canonical event, database mutation or staff fanout**; A's own ID should be accepted when other business constraints permit. Verify legacy User.id doctor-id mapping only if owner-approved, and Admin/Registrar policy separately. Preserve group 70 appointment-time fixing independent of scope check; never use real clinic records.

### NOTIFYREGACTOROMIT-BE-001 — successful notifications lose initiating actor provenance

**Source:** [Registrar routes lines 147–216 and 237–342](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L147-L342) accept authenticated `current_user`, but pass only entity IDs/priority/message into [`RegistrarNotificationService`](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py). Its [`_record_registrar_canonical_event` lines 57–90](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L57-L90) builds `notification_args` without actor_user, actor_id or actor_role and calls `notification_sender_service.send_registrar_event_notification(**notification_args)`. [Canonical method lines 285–349](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L285-L349) supports `actor_user: User|None=None` and explicitly forwards `actor_id=getattr(actor_user,"id",None)`, `actor_role=getattr(actor_user,"role",None)` to `send_canonical_notification_to_roles`. [Canonical role fanout lines 154–208](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L154-L208) carries those values to [NotificationPlatformService.record_delivery_for_users lines 367–413](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L367-L413), then to the persistent event creation. Because the registrar wrapper never provides actor_user and its signatures have no such parameter, `actor_id/actor_role` on these events default to None even for a successful authenticated user-originated event. This obscures who triggered a high-priority price/security/registrar alert; it also weakens forensic review for the cross-owner notifications described above.

**Qualification:** This is missing **canonical event actor fields**, not proof that HTTP access logs or other audit systems lack the actor. Automatic/background booking requests may legitimately have system actor rather than human; do not assign a fictional user to those jobs. Distinct from prior canonical notification success/result and user inbox scope defects.

**Synthetic RED spec — NOT RUN:** mock canonical helper with synthetic `current_user.id/role`, call staff-originated appointment/price/system-alert/test/daily-summary routes; assert `actor_id/role` persist as caller fields. For post-commit scheduled/background events with no user, assert clearly identifiable system-origin policy rather than another user's identity. No PHI/logging of raw patient fields.

### NOTIFYREGLISTPAGE-BE-001 — directory ignores advertised pagination parameters

**Evidence:** [GET /registrar/notifications/registrars lines 102–136](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L102-L136) advertises `limit=Query(default=100,ge=1,le=500)` and `offset=Query(default=0,ge=0)`. The handler **never references** either value after signature. It invokes [`get_active_registrars` or `get_registrars_by_department` lines 153–188](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L153-L188), both using an unconstrained `.all()`; then iterates all rows and returns `total_count=len(registrars_data)`. E.g. for 3 synthetic staff rows, `?limit=1&offset=2` still returns all 3 rather than page size 1. Larger clinics or admin/registrar pages may retrieve more records than requested. The `department` helper's ineffective filter was already separately reported as group65 `NOTIFYREGDEPTCAST-BE-001`; this finding concerns **limit/offset enforcement and total_count semantics only**. Not a WebSocket pagination finding nor group69 Email/SMS template catalogue pagination.

**Synthetic RED spec — NOT RUN:** seed 3 synthetic registrars, request `?limit=1&offset=2`, assert one appropriate registrar and `total_count=3` (all eligible matching count) according to documented pagination contract, `?offset=99` empty items but total 3; confirm role guard still applies and stable `order_by(User.id)` for deterministic pages. Implement query-side `count()/offset().limit()`, not client-only slicing for large lists.

### NOTIFYREGPHONEPROFILE-BE-001 — staff phone is read from wrong model

**Evidence:** [directory endpoint lines 118–132](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L118-L132) gets `profile=registrar.profile` and correctly `last_login = profile.last_login if profile else None`, but serializes phone via `getattr(registrar, "phone", None)`. [User ORM lines 34–104](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user.py#L34-L104) has no mapped `phone`; instead its linked [UserProfile ORM lines 31–60](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user_profile.py#L31-L60) holds `phone: Mapped[str|None]`. [`RegistrarNotificationService.get_active_registrars` lines 153–165](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L153-L165) eager-loads this very `User.profile`. For a normal mapped User with `profile.phone="+998..."`, `getattr(User, "phone", None)` is None; the [admin RegistrarNotificationManager](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx) consumes `registrars` entries and may display incomplete contact data. The use of `getattr` prevents a 500 but silently hides the actual phone.

**Scope:** No claim the endpoint is intended to expose private numbers to all roles; Admin and Registrar currently have access. Product owner must decide whether phone should be visible to each authorized role. `telegram_id` is likewise read via `getattr(User,"telegram_id",None)` but ownership/storage of the Telegram linkage was not exhaustively verified, so that is **not counted** as another defect. Distinct from group65 departmental enumeration scope: this is a data source bug within each allowed returned record.

**Synthetic RED spec — NOT RUN:** create an active User(role=Registrar) with UserProfile(phone="synthetic") and a staff record without profile; assert authorized API response takes `profile.phone` when product policy allows and returns null only if truly absent, preserving last_login. Include selective masking if Registrar viewers should not see the full number; check no unauthorized access.

### NOTIFYREGVISITCODE-BE-001 — VisitService.service_code does not exist on the mapped visit service

**Source chain:** [POST /registrar/notifications/appointment lines 147–169](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L147-L169) passes requested appointment_type into [RegistrarNotificationsApiService.get_appointment_context lines 31–57](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L31-L57). When `appointment_type="visit"`, the service reads a real Visit, loads associated `VisitService` rows via [repository lines 33–37](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/repositories/registrar_notifications_api_repository.py#L33-L37), then calls `self.repository.get_service_by_code(visit_service.service_code)`. [VisitService ORM lines 155–191](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L155-L191) has `service_id`, `code`, `name`, `qty` and `price`, **not** `service_code` (nor a compatibility property). The first matching visit service row triggers `AttributeError` *before* calling repository.get_service_by_code. [Endpoint exception handling lines 178–187](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L178-L187) turns the unhandled error into HTTP 500; no registrar notification fan-out occurs.

**Precise conditions:** Requires a real Visit containing at least one VisitService row. An empty-service Visit will not hit this access. Group 70 `NOTIFYREGAPPTTIME-BE-001` is the *other* `appointment_type="appointment"` path and formats a string time as datetime; the present issue blocks the **Visit** path even when no appointment-time cast is attempted. The existing [unit test file](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/unit/test_registrar_notifications_api_service.py) checks the missing visit and a mock price override but does not exercise a real VisitService with mapped `code`. After fixing, map `VisitService.code` vs `Service.code` with policy and snapshot semantics; do not silently substitute `Service.service_code` (a separate, distinct catalog field) without checking its domain definition.

**Synthetic fail-first test, NOT RUN:** seed synthetic real Visit(id, patient_id) and VisitService(visit_id, service_id, code="C01", name="Synthetic", qty=2, price=...), plus catalog Service; call get_appointment_context with `appointment_type="visit"` and a mocked sender, require a valid service context and no AttributeError. Include a visit with zero services and catalog row missing (fallback should honor the persisted visit-service snapshot, not fabricate prices) as independent contracts. Assert HTTP result is not 500 for valid visit and no real patient data or notifications.

### NOTIFYREGENTITYLINK-BE-001 — default visit entity with null ID for appointment and queue events

**Source chain:** [RegistrarNotificationService._record_registrar_canonical_event lines 57–90](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L57-L90) has optional `entity_type/entity_id` parameters and forwards them **only when supplied**. [notify_new_appointment lines 274–291](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L274-L291) sends metadata with `appointment_id, appointment_kind, patient_id, doctor_id, priority`, but **no** `visit_id` and no explicit `entity_type/entity_id`. [notify_queue_status lines 465–480](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L465-L480) sends `queue_entry_id, queue_id, patient_id, queue_number, status_change`, likewise neither `visit_id` nor explicit canonical entity locator. [Canonical send_registrar_event_notification lines 285–349](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L285-L349) defaults `entity_type=entity_type or "visit"` and `entity_id=entity_id if entity_id is not None else (metadata or {}).get("visit_id")`; with these callers, `entity_type="visit"` and `entity_id=None` even for an Appointment or queue status event. [send_canonical_notification_to_roles lines 154–209](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L154-L209) and [NotificationPlatformService.record_delivery_for_users lines 367–413](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L367-L413) carry this pair into canonical event creation. The generic deep_link `"/registrar"` may still navigate to a broad panel, but structured entity association is missing for event-specific routing or correlation.

**Independence and preconditions:** Group 71 `NOTIFYREGACTOROMIT-BE-001` concerns **who initiated the event**; this finding concerns **which source entity the event represents**. Group 70 already notes separate bugs that may abort appointment/queue event construction before reaching the canonical producer, and the first group 72 finding blocks Visit-with-services context. Thus successful-delivery impact is conditional on fixing those independent bugs or a compatible synthetic/legacy caller. We do **not** claim deployed delivery with wrong entity on every request. Group 65 delivery ACK false-positive is not recounted.

**Synthetic fail-first test, NOT RUN:** invoke each typed registrar producer with synthetic Appointment, Visit and queue row, patch canonical sender to capture exact `entity_type,entity_id`; assert Appointment -> `appointment/id`, Visit -> `visit/id`, queue status -> policy-approved `queue_entry/id` or `queue/id` (explicit owner decision), and identity survives event persistence; do not infer from arbitrary numeric IDs.

### NOTIFYREGPRICEPENDING-BE-001 — stale status is represented as still awaiting approval

**Source:** [`get_price_change_context` lines 59–79](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L59-L79) fetches a DoctorPriceOverride by ID, resolves doctor/service/visit/patient, but **does not filter by status**. [POST /registrar/notifications/price-change lines 190–216](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L190-L216) sends the returned record unconditionally to [notify_price_change lines 301–384](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L301-L384). Its unused long-form `message` text includes `"🔄 Статус: Ожидает одобрения"` at [lines 343–357](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L343-L357), and its **actual canonical notification text** always says `"Цена услуги ... изменена и ожидает подтверждения."` [lines 359–363](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L359-L363), independent of status. [DoctorPriceOverride.status model lines 69–76](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/doctor_price_override.py#L69-L76) supports `pending`, `approved`, `rejected`. Thus an allowed staff call for a previously accepted/rejected override can post a **new** misleading pending-approval notice or reuse the canonical event (deduplication is implementation-dependent), even though no approval is pending. The event metadata does not carry status to help the recipient disambiguate.

**Scope:** Does not say the endpoint actually changes the price or approval status. Group 65 `NOTIFYREGPRICEFLAG-BE-001` addresses **notification_sent marked True on failed canonical creation**; here the independent problem is **wrong workflow status in text and missing status check**. If business policy requires re-announcing historical decisions, use status-aware event names/text rather than asserting pending.

**Synthetic fail-first test, NOT RUN:** seed three synthetic overrides with `pending`, `approved`, `rejected`, then mock the canonical sender; pending receives truthful awaiting-approval text; approved/rejected either return a documented conflict/no new event or produce truthful status-aware text with no actionable approval prompt. Include repeat requests, no actual price mutation or notification sent to staff.

### NOTIFYREGSUMMARYDROP-BE-001 — detailed summary string discarded before inbox write

**Source:** [`send_daily_summary` lines 560–625](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L560-L625) calls `_collect_daily_stats`, then builds a formatted multiline `message` containing appointments, new patients, confirmations, cancellations, financial sums, queues, pending changes and yesterday comparison at [lines 573–600](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L573-L600). That `message` variable is **not referenced again**. Instead, [lines 602–612](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L602-L612) call canonical notification sender with a separate one-line `message=f"Сводка за {date} готова."`, and put the `stats` dict under event metadata; the actual human-readable delivery text therefore does **not** include the detailed daily report. [NotificationPlatformService.record_delivery_for_users lines 367–413](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py#L367-L413) retains the explicit `message` and metadata snapshot. The [admin RegistrarNotificationManager calls the daily-summary endpoint](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/frontend/src/components/admin/RegistrarNotificationManager.tsx), but that action alone does not display the constructed `message` text.

**Distinctness/qualifications:** Group 70 `NOTIFYREGDAILYORM-BE-001` shows the **collector** fails on missing date columns and falls back to zero metrics; group 70 `NOTIFYREGDAILYZEROS-BE-001` finds other **uncomputed** metrics. This finding is an independent **presentation/contract omission**, even with correct stats: the rendered report is dropped. Stats in canonical metadata may still be consumed by future/other clients, so do not claim all data is irretrievably lost; the *text content* promised by the summary code is missing. Group 65 success/sent_count issues not re-reported.

**Synthetic fail-first test, NOT RUN:** supply synthetic nonzero `stats` with fake `_collect_daily_stats` and fake canonical sender, assert user-facing notification text includes target_date and selected measured KPI fields, or an approved deep link to a dedicated detailed report; metadata-only stats may remain. Verify real-zero vs unavailable metrics and prevent forwarding any unvalidated financial amounts. Ensure no real messaging. Test `canonical_created=False` separately under existing group65 finding.

## Continued source Code Review — group 73: registrar alert validation, queue claims, doctor permissions and stale appointment status

**Date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), unchanged at first touch and checked again before append. **Source independently re-opened this group:** [registrar API and DTO](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py), [context service](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py), [context repository](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/repositories/registrar_notifications_api_repository.py), [registrar event service](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py), [DailyQueue and OnlineQueueEntry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py), [Doctor ORM](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py), [Appointment](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py), [Visit](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py), [ADR-001 queue owner and same-specialty collaboration](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/docs/adr/ADR-001-queue-ownership-and-specialty-architecture.md), previous #4059 groups, merged group45 [NOTIFYQUEUEACTOR-BE-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/docs/audits/2026-11-13-notification-websocket-postmerge-round45.md) and issue #4063. Root AGENTS.md and installed verification skill read. No runtime, clinical/queue data, private messages or tests accessed.

| New distinct ID | Priority | Source-level defect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NOTIFYREGAPPTTYPE-BE-001 | P2 / request contract | appointment_type unvalidated str; all unknown values silently select Appointment repository rather than 422 |
| NOTIFYREGQUEUECLAIM-BE-001 | P1 conditional / queue truth | requester-supplied queue status_change is never checked against persisted OnlineQueueEntry.status before alert |
| NOTIFYREGQUEUEACTOR-BE-001 | P1 conditional / queue access authorization | Doctor role on registrar queue-status endpoint is not scoped to doctor/specialty eligibility for referenced DailyQueue |
| NOTIFYREGSTALEAPPT-BE-001 | P2 conditional / scheduling truth | new_appointment producer does not reject terminal Appointment/Visit statuses and may announce a canceled or closed encounter as new |

### NOTIFYREGAPPTTYPE-BE-001 — arbitrary appointment_type is routed to the Appointment table

**Source:** [AppointmentNotificationRequest, lines 40–45](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L40-L45) uses `appointment_type: str` with only a *description* mentioning `appointment` or `visit`; no `Literal`, validator or enum. [RegistrarNotificationsApiService.get_appointment_context, lines 31–57](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L31-L57) checks `if appointment_type == "visit"` and takes `get_appointment` for **every other string**, including `"visits"`, `"appointment_typo"`, empty string and uppercase `"VISIT"`. [Repository, lines 22–31](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/repositories/registrar_notifications_api_repository.py#L22-L31) fetches Visit and Appointment by unrelated PK namespaces; overlapping integer IDs can resolve a different entity and patient than client intended. A missing Appointment returns a misleading 404 rather than an input-validation 422, while one that exists can proceed on an unintended appointment path (currently may fail for a separate group70 `str.strftime` issue). This is not a claim of unauthorized patient disclosure; Doctor ownership is independently tracked in group71.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** invoke FastAPI TestClient with mocked, distinct `Visit(id=10)` and `Appointment(id=10)`, fake recipient service and authorized synthetic caller; `appointment_type="visits"` and `"bogus"` must raise 422 **without repository selection or message attempt**. Verify exact `"appointment"` and `"visit"` select correct types; if case folding is desired, explicitly document and validate it. Keep this separate from group72 VisitService.code crash and group70 Appointment.time mismatch.

### NOTIFYREGQUEUECLAIM-BE-001 — queue status announcement trusts request text over DB state

**Source:** [QueueStatusNotificationRequest lines 54–61](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L54-L61) accepts arbitrary `status_change: str`. [POST /registrar/notifications/queue-status lines 237–255](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L237-L255) fetches an entry by id but passes caller-supplied `status_change` to [notify_queue_status lines 393–480](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L393-L480) without comparing against `queue_entry.status` or a trusted transition record. It maps `"completed"` to `"Визит завершен"`, `"cancelled"` to `"Отменил визит"`, and arbitrary values to `"Статус изменен: ..."`, then provides the caller's string as canonical `queue_status_changed` text and metadata. [OnlineQueueEntry.status model lines 331–334](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py#L331-L334) persists `waiting/called/in_service/diagnostics/served/incomplete/no_show/cancelled`; string `completed` is not its direct canonical status. The route does **not itself mutate status**, so a generated alert can contradict persisted truth and can be repeated or fabricated without any real queue transition.

**Precondition:** Group70 `NOTIFYREGQUEUEORM-BE-001` currently causes this sender to abort on legacy nonexistent queue attributes. The misleading event is therefore **conditional on repair of those independent field mismatches**, or an alternative entity mock/shim exposing those fields. Not proof of an observed false delivery. Different from group45 `NOTIFYQUEUEACTOR` (different route/claimed queue-number display) and from the group73 role/queue-owner issue below (which actor may act vs which status was actually reached).

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** with synthetic queued `OnlineQueueEntry(status="waiting")`, request `status_change="completed"` as authorized operator with patched recorder; assert **no** canonical completed notice unless the backend state reflects an approved completed/served transition and business vocabulary has an explicit mapping. Test valid status mapping and idempotent repeat, a stale race (status changed between initial fetch and final producer), invalid string -> 422, and ensure no mutation outside canonical queue workflow. Both doctor-owned and resource-owned queues. Enforce claim validation server side, not in frontend.

### NOTIFYREGQUEUEACTOR-BE-001 — Doctor-role queue status alert is not authorized against the queue's owner/specialty

**Source chain:** [POST /registrar/notifications/queue-status lines 237–254](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L237-L254) permits `require_roles(["Admin","Registrar","Doctor"])`, but never reads authenticated `current_user` after FastAPI dependency evaluation. [get_queue_entry lines 81–89](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L81-L89) and [repository lines 52–56](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/repositories/registrar_notifications_api_repository.py#L52-L56) perform sole-PK lookup without joining queue/doctor or checking scoped permissions. [DailyQueue owner fields lines 133–164](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/online_queue.py#L133-L164) use XOR `specialist_id` (Doctor FK) or `queue_resource_id`, and [Doctor.user_id link](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L97-L114) identifies the staff user. [ADR-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/docs/adr/ADR-001-queue-ownership-and-specialty-architecture.md) permits **same-specialty clinician collaboration**, not access to arbitrary unrelated-specialty queues. The route does not apply these rules and could permit a Doctor account to trigger misleading registrar events for an unrelated queue if the event construction succeeds.

**Precise distinctions:** Existing [group71 NOTIFYREGDOCTOROWNER-BE-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/pull/4059) covered **appointment and price-change IDs**, not `queue-status`, while merged [group45 NOTIFYQUEUEACTOR-BE-001](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/docs/audits/2026-11-13-notification-websocket-postmerge-round45.md) covers **`/notifications/send-queue-update`** with caller-provided department and queue number. This ID covers a **third, separate registrar queue entry PK route**. The underlying policy should ideally use one central queue authority and avoid conflicting independently enforced checks. **Do not require strict `Doctor.id == specialist_id` in all cases**: same-specialty collaboration is approved; resource-owned queues need an explicit policy. Group70 ORM mismatch presently masks many real delivery paths; this is an authorization defect in reachability/policy, not evidence of live unowned-patient notifications or queue mutations.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** seed Doctor A cardiology, Doctor B dermatology, Doctor C cardiology, resource-owned lab queue and queue entries; authenticate A. For B's entry, assert 403 before event publication; for C's entry, apply ADR-001 collaborator policy and allow only when other permitted criteria hold; for A's own doctor-owned entry, allow; for resource-owned queue, require explicit queue resource grant policy. Admin access and Registrar policy tested separately. No actual clinic database or state changes.

### NOTIFYREGSTALEAPPT-BE-001 — canceled/completed source may be announced as a new appointment

**Evidence:** [`get_appointment_context` lines 31–57](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notifications_api_service.py#L31-L57) returns any found Appointment or Visit by id; no check that source is new, pending or nonterminal. [POST /registrar/notifications/appointment lines 147–169](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L147-L169) calls producer unconditionally after context retrieval. [`notify_new_appointment` lines 191–294](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L191-L294) generates event type `new_appointment`, title `"Новая запись в клинике"`, canonical body `"Создана новая запись для пациента ..."` independently of `appointment.status`. Yet [Appointment.status](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py#L60-L65) includes `"cancelled"` (confirmed by other canonical appointment code and scheduling filters) and [Visit.status](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L43-L45) includes `"open", "closed", "canceled"`. A valid already canceled/closed source can still be announced as **new** and mislead registrar workload if request reaches canonical sender.

**Condition/dedup:** `Appointment` path has earlier group70 str-time bug; `Visit` with at least one VisitService has group72 service_code bug. A **closed Visit with no VisitService rows** can pass those particular mismatches (other preconditions still apply) and reach the unconditional `new_appointment` call. Different event family from group72 `NOTIFYREGPRICEPENDING-BE-001`, which addresses stale *price override* statuses. This is also separate from group73 arbitrary appointment_type: even `"visit"` with an *exact valid type* and closed status triggers incorrect wording.

**Synthetic fail-first, NOT RUN:** synthetic canceled Appointment and closed/canceled Visit (with no VisitService for isolated test), fake registrars and canonical sender; assert 409/not-sent or status-corrected event contract and no `new_appointment` marker. Positive cases: scheduled Appointment and freshly created/open Visit, with independent code mapping/time fixes applied, emit new event only when appropriate. Include repeated delivery/idempotency under approved event policy, without real messages.

## Continued source Code Review — group 74: doctor/department information contracts supporting registrar notification context

### NOTIFYDOCDEPTID-BE-001 — numeric department ID sent to a specialty string API

**Source:** [GET /doctor-info/departments/{department_id} in doctor_info.py lines 161–186](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L161-L186) takes FastAPI `department_id: int` and directly executes `get_doctor_info_service(db).get_department_info(department_id)`. [DoctorInfoService.get_department_info lines 145–184](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L145-L184) instead expects `specialty: str`, queries `Doctor.specialty.in_(specialty_variants(specialty))` and formats department name via `specialty.title()`. [`specialty_variants` in app/core/specialties.py](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/core/specialties.py) immediately does `value.strip()` for a non-None input, so integer department_id raises `AttributeError`. The department service catches the exception and returns None; the route turns this into HTTP 404. [Actual Department ORM](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py#L19-L60) has real integer `id`, `key`, `name_ru`, `name_uz` and `active`; the route never queries that table. Therefore even a legitimately existing `Department(id=42)` cannot be resolved by this branch. FastAPI path is mounted under `/api/v1/doctor-info`, as verified from the [router include](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py).

**Distinction and boundaries:** The underlying service's `get_department_info("cardiology")` can still be used by notification formatting for a specialty string; the defect is the **ID route-service boundary**. The next finding is a separate *serialization* mismatch affecting routes even when a specialty list is successfully produced; do not conflate. No live endpoint request was executed.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** seed fake `Department(id=42,key="cardio",name_ru="Кардиология",active=True)`; with authorized Admin/Registrar/Doctor identity call `GET /api/v1/doctor-info/departments/42` and require real persisted department identity with HTTP 200; absent ID -> 404. Assert no integer is ever passed into specialty_variants. Add optional canonical specialty projection only after resolving the Department row; test doctorless department and dynamic `Department.key`. No real clinic data.

### NOTIFYDOCDEPTDTO-BE-001 — required response metadata absent from department dictionaries

**Source:** [DepartmentInfoResponse fields lines 33–42](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L33-L42) require `id: int`, `name: str`, `is_active: bool`. [DoctorInfoService.get_department_info lines 163–184](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L163-L184) constructs dict with `specialty`, `name`, `doctors_count`, `active_doctors`, `cabinets`, `description` — **no `id`, no `is_active`**. [get_all_departments lines 208–231](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L208-L231) assembles exactly these helper dicts. [GET /doctor-info/departments lines 190–214](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L190-L214) unconditionally maps each dict through `DepartmentInfoResponse(**dept)`; any non-empty list causes Pydantic `ValidationError` for missing fields, caught and returned as HTTP 500. [AppointmentDoctorInfoResponse route](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L218-L249) likewise maps `get_appointment_doctor_info(...)["department"]` to `DepartmentInfoResponse`; both normal department dict and fallback `{"name":"Неизвестно"}` lack the required fields, so this route fails even when its doctor info is otherwise valid. When doctor itself is missing, its placeholder `DoctorInfoResponse` also lacks required fields and fails; that is part of the same response-mapping contract gap, not an additional new ID.

**Boundary:** A completely empty `/departments` list returns HTTP 200 with an empty array; the error requires at least one specialty / helper-produced dict. Actual dynamic Departments in DB have `id` and `active`, but helper uses Doctor specialty aggregation, not the Department ORM; don't fabricate an ID or status from a specialty string. This is a different failure from numeric-ID route #1. No runtime verification.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** with two synthetic Doctor specialties and valid Department rows, call authenticated `GET /doctor-info/departments`; assert HTTP 200 with validated real IDs and `is_active` for both entries. For appointment-doctor info, exercise assigned Doctor, unassigned Doctor, and no Department; assert response model requirements are satisfied via explicit nullable/typed contract or documented 404, never generic 500 for a valid request. Separate missing/deleted Doctor or Department policy and ensure no fabricated resource IDs.

### NOTIFYDOCLISTEMPTY-BE-001 — unfiltered doctor directory is hardcoded empty

**Source:** [GET /doctor-info/doctors lines 95–132](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L95-L132) supports `specialization`, `department_name`, and `active_only`. If either of the first two is provided it calls service, but its `else` branch for **neither filter** explicitly sets `doctors=[]` (comment TODO `get_all_doctors`). `active_only=True` only filters this empty list and `total_count` becomes 0. Thus the ordinary default `GET /api/v1/doctor-info/doctors` returns an empty directory regardless of the [mapped Doctor rows](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L70-L145). This is static deterministic branch behavior, not a transient DB outage, provided no separate API router replaces this specific path.

**Scope:** Filtering by specialization or mapped department name can still return doctors; the defect concerns the **unfiltered** endpoint only. Not a duplicate of group71 `NOTIFYREGLISTPAGE-BE-001` (different endpoint, disregarded `limit/offset`) or any WebSocket inbox pagination. It may cause a consumer to interpret healthy configured doctor population as empty, but no UI incident proven.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** seed two synthetic active linked Doctors and one inactive Doctor, call default `GET /doctor-info/doctors` and `?active_only=false`, expect 2 and 3 correct records respectively, stable order and total_count. Verify specialized filtered calls unchanged and minimal PII. No actual clinic data.

### NOTIFYDOCUNKNOWN-BE-001 — missing doctor primary key yields synthetic success

**Source:** [DoctorInfoService.get_doctor_full_info lines 27–44](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L27-L44) queries Doctor by integer ID and, when `not doctor`, returns a filled dictionary including `"id": doctor_id`, `"full_name": f"Врач #{doctor_id}"`, `"specialization": "Неизвестно"`, `"department": "Неизвестно"`, `"is_active": False`, rather than a not-found signal. [GET /doctor-info/doctors/{doctor_id} lines 75–92](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L75-L92) immediately constructs `DoctorInfoResponse(**doctor_info)` with those values and returns HTTP 200. Unlike [GET /doctor-info/doctors/by-user/{user_id} lines 136–159](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L136-L159), which explicitly returns HTTP 404 for absent Doctor, primary-key lookup reports an apparently valid but inactive synthetic Doctor object. This makes a truly missing Doctor indistinguishable from a real inactive one for API consumers that inspect response status/resource existence.

**Risk qualifier:** `get_doctor_full_info` intentionally serves as a **lenient formatting fallback** in [RegistrarNotificationService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L208-L225). Do not globally replace fallback with raising 404 in the shared service without first checking those consumers; the fix can belong at strict ID lookup endpoint or a `strict=True` repository method. No claim this endpoint directly creates a fake appointment or affects financial transactions. Distinct from group70/72 ORM attr crashes and group74 department schema validation failure.

**Synthetic fail-first NOT RUN:** authenticated GET absent doctor id 9999 -> 404 under approved strict detail endpoint contract; existing inactive Doctor -> 200 with `is_active=false`; existing active Doctor -> 200 with correct ID. In parallel, test `RegistrarNotificationService` missing-doctor display still falls back safely without disclosing incorrect identity as a real Doctor. Avoid patient/doctor real data.

## Continued source Code Review — group 75: DoctorInfo directory specialty matching, disabled doctors, dynamic departments and catalog completeness

**Review date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), checked identical before review and write. **Reopened exact source:** [DoctorInfoService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py), [DoctorInfo API endpoints](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py), [specialty variants / canonical specialty SSOT](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/core/specialties.py), [Doctor model and active/profile fields](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py), [Department model and real name/key/active fields](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py), [integration specialty canonicalization tests](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/tests/integration/test_specialty_canonicalization.py), [API router mount](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/api.py), prior audit groups 45 and 47–74, issue #4063.
