### NOTIFYDOCSPECWILDCARD-BE-001 — free-string specialty filter is overly broad and treats wildcard characters as SQL patterns

**Source:** [`GET /api/v1/doctor-info/doctors` with `specialization`, doctor_info.py lines 95–133](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L95-L133) passes unbounded `specialization: str|None` into [DoctorInfoService.get_doctors_by_specialization lines 106–136](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L106-L136). The service constructs `Doctor.specialty.ilike(f"%{variant}%")` for each `specialty_variants(specialization)`. [`specialty_variants` SSOT](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/core/specialties.py#L68-L90) expands known dental aliases but otherwise returns the literal trimmed specialty (without escaping `%/_` and without asking for substring matching). Because SQL `ILIKE` treats percent and underscore as pattern wildcards, user query `specialization="%"` constructs `ILIKE '%%%'` and matches essentially every non-null active specialty. More subtly, `specialization="cardiology"` matches any distinct value containing that substring, e.g. synthetic `"pediatric_cardiology"`; a query for specialty prefix can include a different department. The SSOT helper/route describe specialty filtering, not free-text fuzzy search.

**Consequence/qualification:** These doctor directory records are role-authorized for Admin/Registrar/Doctor, so **do not** claim a role bypass, PHI disclosure or SQL injection. SQLAlchemy parameterizes patterns; the issue is predicate semantics, not string-concatenated SQL execution. A product decision could allow partial matching, but that must be explicit and safe; if the intended contract is exact canonical specialty family, use equality / normalized variants or explicit escaped LIKE. Existing specialty canonicalization tests confirm dental alias inclusion but do not prove rejection of unrelated substrings or wildcard inputs.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** seed active Doctor rows with `specialty="cardiology"`, `"pediatric_cardiology"`, `"dermatology"`, `"dentistry"`; query exact `specialization=cardiology` and assert only intended canonical family; query `specialization=%` and `_` assert rejection (422) or escaped literal/no match, per selected filter contract; verify dental variants (dentistry/stomatology/dental) still work, no live users. Test route serialization separately from group74 missing Department DTO fields.

### NOTIFYDOCACTIVEFILTER-BE-001 — route active_only=false is defeated before route can apply it

**Source:** [doctor_info.py `get_doctors_list` lines 95–133](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L95-L133) accepts `active_only: bool = Query(True)` and applies a **post-fetch** `if active_only: doctors = [d for d in doctors if d.get("is_active",False)]`. However [DoctorInfoService.get_doctors_by_specialization lines 116–126](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L116-L126) always filters `Doctor.active == True`, regardless of route parameter. [DoctorInfoService.get_doctors_by_department lines 293–300](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L293-L300) delegates to the same active-only specialty method. [Doctor ORM active flag](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L119-L132) is a real Boolean field. Therefore with `GET /doctor-info/doctors?specialization=cardiology&active_only=false`, a Doctor row with `Doctor.active=False` is excluded by DB predicate and cannot be recovered by the route's false flag. Linked `User.is_active=False` may produce `is_active=False` for still-active Doctor rows and is a separate axis; it does not rescue explicitly inactive Doctor rows.

**Distinction:** Group74 `NOTIFYDOCLISTEMPTY-BE-001` is the **unfiltered** route's hardcoded `doctors=[]`. This finding is observable **with a valid specialization filter** and is caused by a restrictive service SQL predicate, so it survives fixing the default list branch. Dynamic department-name filtering below is a separate lookup precondition.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** seed 2 Doctor specialty `cardiology`, one `Doctor.active=True`, one `Doctor.active=False`, with synthetic active User links; `active_only=true` => only one; `active_only=false` => both, and inactive row has `is_active=false`. Add a disabled User with active Doctor to verify user and doctor active axes. Test both specialization and valid department-name paths. Do not loosen privacy/RBAC for any role.

### NOTIFYDOCDYNDEPT-BE-001 — fixed Russian department-name mapping blocks dynamically created Departments

**Source:** [DoctorInfoService.get_department_by_name lines 189–209](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L189-L209) hardcodes only six lowercased Russian labels: `кардиология`, `дерматология`, `стоматология`, `терапия`, `неврология`, `педиатрия`. For any other name it returns `None` without reading the [Department table](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py#L19-L62), which stores dynamic `key, name_ru, name_uz, active`. [get_doctors_by_department lines 292–305](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L292-L305) returns `[]` when that mapping returns None, and [GET /doctor-info/doctors?department_name=...](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L95-L133) publishes it as `doctors=[]` and count 0. A dynamically added `Department(name_ru="Эндокринология",key="endocrinology")` with a real active linked Doctor can be a valid clinic configuration, but query by its stored name returns zero. Even the existing mapped English/localized key `"endocrinology"` is unrecognized by this route.

**Nuance:** For some standard departments the mapping still works (e.g. `Кардиология`), and the service internally chooses a Doctor.specialty rather than the canonical Department FK; an implementation must avoid mapping a department key to a different specialty accidentally and support doctor/department relationship policy. Group74 `NOTIFYDOCDEPTID-BE-001` is an **int-vs-string** bug in the **ID resource endpoint**; this is a separate **hardcoded name lookup** failing on dynamic names even when types are valid.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** create synthetic `Department(id=42,key="endocrinology",name_ru="Эндокринология",active=True)` and linked Doctor row (Doctor.department_id=42, Doctor.specialty="endocrinology", active=True); call `GET /doctor-info/doctors?department_name=Эндокринология`, assert correct Doctor and count; also test department key and Uzbek name as policy allows. Repeat with `Кардиология` baseline and a doctor assigned to a different Department to ensure no false association. Do not populate clinical records or query live DB.

### NOTIFYDOCDEPTSOURCE-BE-001 — endpoint's "all departments" data source is Doctor rows, not Department SSOT

**Source:** [get_all_departments, DoctorInfoService lines 211–233](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L211-L233) queries distinct `Doctor.specialty` **filtered by `Doctor.active == True`**, loops returned specialty strings and synthesizes department objects via `get_department_info(specialty)`. It **never** queries [`Department` ORM with `id`, `key`, `active` and localized names](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py#L19-L75). Consequently an enabled actual Department with **no active doctors** is missing from the advertised `GET /doctor-info/departments`, including a newly configured department before specialist onboarding or a temporarily empty service area. Conversely a legacy/incomplete Doctor with `specialty="general"` and `Doctor.active=True` can produce a synthetic `"Общая практика"` department even if no such Department row is configured, despite [specialty SSOT documenting `general` as incomplete/unbookable sentinel](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/core/specialties.py#L12-L31). This breaks service/department registry truth independently of response serialization.

**Distinctness and masks:** Group74 `NOTIFYDOCDEPTDTO-BE-001` finds missing `id/is_active` response fields causing Pydantic errors on **nonempty** generated rows. The present defect is the **wrong source population**, which would persist after fixing DTO field mapping: doctorless actual Departments remain invisible and phantom specialty-derived entries remain possible. With no Doctor rows, group74 DTO error is masked because the list is empty; the service still incorrectly omits actual doctorless Departments. `active_only=false` is also ineffective on this service because there is no Department query; track that as part of the same source-of-truth defect, not a fifth ID.

**Synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** seed actual Department A with no doctors and active=True, Department B with one active Doctor and active=True, Department C inactive with no doctors, and an active Doctor specialty general not linked to a real Department; assert `get_all_departments` returns actual configured A and B regardless of doctor count, not synthetic "general", and that route `active_only=false` includes real C. Bind IDs, localized names and active flags from Department records; confirm DTO validation with group74 fix, but separate schema from population assertions.

## Continued source Code Review — group 76: dormant registrar services-assigned notification contract

**Review date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), verified identical at start and immediately before document update. **Source explicitly opened:** [RegistrarNotificationService.notify_services_assigned](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L625-L729), [Patient model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/patient.py#L26-L113), [Service catalog model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/service.py#L28-L84), [VisitService snapshot model](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L155-L201), [Appointment ORM](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/appointment.py), [canonical registrar typed notification producer](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L285-L351), full #4059 groups47–75, issue #4063 and prior merged report. Root AGENTS.md and installed verification skill reread.

### NOTIFYREGASGNPATIENT-BE-001 — Patient.name nonexistent on mapped patient row

**Source:** [notify_services_assigned lines 654–663](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L654-L663) queries `Patient` by `appointment.patient_id` then sets `patient_name = patient.name if patient else ...`. [Patient ORM lines 26–45 and 77–113](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/patient.py#L26-L113) stores `last_name, first_name, middle_name` and exposes `full_name` via hybrid property. No `name` property or mapped column is defined in this model. For any real mapped Patient row, Python attempts `patient.name` and throws `AttributeError`; the outer [broad catch lines 725–728](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L725-L728) returns `{"success":False,...}`. Nothing reaches the canonical event [lines 692–705](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L692-L705).

**Impact/preconditions:** `appointment.patient_id` references a normal Patient in the Appointment model, so this is the expected normal branch *if the method is ever invoked*. Current static call-site search did not locate a caller. No claim that the current clinic already uses or fails this path. Distinct from group70/72 appointment notification ORM mismatch, which concerns different `notify_new_appointment` service and API routes.

**Proposed synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** create synthetic Appointment with Patient(last_name="Тест",first_name="Пациент"), a minimal authorized User registrar, fake canonical sender, and invoke notify_services_assigned with controlled service DTO; assert event created and name read via `patient.full_name`. Test deleted/absent Patient behavior explicitly without logging PHI. To isolate this issue, use a DTO with valid quantity and price.

### NOTIFYREGASGNQUANTITY-BE-001 — accepts list[Any] but assumes every service has quantity

**Source:** [method signature lines 627–631](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L627-L631) takes generic `services: list[Any]` with no validator or converter, and [lines 664–673](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L664-L673) displays `service.quantity` and calculates `service.price * service.quantity`. [Canonical Service model lines 28–84](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/service.py#L28-L84) has `name` and `price`, but **no quantity/qty**. [VisitService snapshot ORM lines 155–201](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L155-L201) does have `qty` and a `quantity` compatibility property. Thus passing a catalog `Service` object — a plausible but uncontracted input for a service assignment — raises `AttributeError`, even after the unrelated patient-name fix.

**Qualification:** Because the method is unreferenced from currently indexed source and `list[Any]` does not declare the intended DTO, this is **conditional on catalog objects being passed**, not proof that every supported caller is broken; `VisitService` objects are compatible with the quantity access. Do not replace visit snapshot quantities/prices with catalog defaults by accident.

**Proposed synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** after isolating patient.full_name, test input choices `Service(name="Test",price=Decimal("10000"))` and `VisitService(name="Test",qty=2,price=Decimal("10000"))` under an approved typed contract; choose whether catalog Service is legal and, if legal, supply quantity explicitly with a normalized DTO. Verify resulting count, rendered text and amount, and reject malformed inputs before fan-out. No actual staff notifications.

### NOTIFYREGASGNPRICE-BE-001 — nullable Decimal price is formatted/multiplied without policy

**Source:** [notify_services_assigned lines 664–673](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L664-L673) interpolates `{service.price:.0f}` and multiplies `service.price*service.quantity` without None handling, currency validation or explicit unavailable-price representation. Both [Service.price lines 64–66](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/service.py#L64-L66) and [VisitService.price lines 175–177](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/visit.py#L175-L177) allow SQL NULL / mapped `None`. A valid VisitService with `price=None` and `qty=1` raises `TypeError` during `{None:.0f}`; the catch returns failure and no canonical event, even if patient and quantity fields are valid.

**Distinctness/qualifications:** This is nullable money handling for the *assigned-services* producer, not group69 `PaymentData.amount=None` nor group72 `VisitService.service_code` nonexistent lookup or the `Service.quantity` missing field. A missing price may mean unavailable/not yet priced rather than free; **never silently treat None as zero UZS** without explicit business pricing policy. No live service price or billing incident verified.

**Proposed synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** synthetic VisitService(name="Synthetic",qty=1,price=None) plus populated patient; assert no server exception, explicit 'price pending/unavailable' or documented validation error, never a fabricated `0 сум`; separate price=Decimal("0") genuine zero and Decimal("10000") paid, with currency checks and qty>0 contract. Do not create charges.

### NOTIFYREGASGNDETAIL-BE-001 — detailed assignment text is constructed but dropped from canonical delivery

**Source:** [notify_services_assigned lines 664–690](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L664-L690) computes services_text with each service name, unit price, quantity, calculated total and intended registrar instruction. Its local variable `message` is **never used again**. [Canonical call lines 692–705](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L692-L705) passes only `message=f"Для записи #{appointment.id} назначены новые услуги."` and metadata `subtype,appointment_id,patient_id,doctor_id,services_count,total_price`; neither text nor metadata includes **the actual assigned service IDs/names, unit prices and quantities**. Thus even when a synthetic DTO bypasses the earlier field/nullable errors and canonical event succeeds, the registrar's notification does not contain the assignment details that the function rendered for that workflow.

**Distinctness/qualification:** This is a different producer and business handoff from group72 `NOTIFYREGSUMMARYDROP-BE-001` (daily digest message drop), although the code smell is similar. Here the loss includes individual service lines necessary to identify what to add to registrar/payment workflows; the daily report only lost a KPI summary while metadata contained the KPI values. However deep_link `"/registrar"` may lead to a separate source of assignment detail; do **not** claim all service records are irretrievably lost from the database or currently mailed. No route caller verified.

**Proposed synthetic RED, NOT RUN:** synthetic fully valid assignment with 2 VisitService snapshot rows (quantities 1/2, distinct prices/currencies), fake canonical recorder, assert user-visible event text or an explicit authorized deep link contains sufficient service ID/quantity context to act, not just counts/aggregate amount. If carrying PHI/service data in notification content is not allowed, require an authorized per-appointment detail URL and keep canonical payload minimal. Preserve patient privacy and test no raw sensitive billing data leakage to unintended roles (group65 department-recipient scope separately tracked).

**Review date:** 2026-11-14 Asia/Tashkent. **Pinned current main:** [94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/commit/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d), checked identical before review and immediately before writing. **Source reopened:** [DoctorInfoService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py), [DoctorInfo API](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py), [User](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user.py), [UserProfile](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user_profile.py), [Doctor/Department relationship](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py), [Department registry](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py), [RegistrarNotificationService](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py), [registrar API alert models](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py), [canonical registrar sender](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py), [NotificationPlatform policy](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py) and [canonical event persistence](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_rest.py), older group45, audit groups47–76 and #4063. ### NOTIFYDOCPHONEPROFILE-BE-001 — doctor contact number sourced from nonexistent User.phone

**Evidence:** [DoctorInfoService.get_doctor_full_info lines 46–57](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L46-L57) creates `user_info` with `"phone": getattr(doctor.user,"phone",None)`; [User ORM lines 34–75](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user.py#L34-L75) has no mapped `phone` attribute. [User.profile relationship](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user.py#L98-L107) connects to [UserProfile.phone, lines 31–55](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/user_profile.py#L31-L55). So even when a synthetic Doctor.user.profile.phone is populated, the returned DoctorInfoService result includes `phone=None`. [GET /doctor-info/doctors/{doctor_id}](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L75-L92) and [formatted doctor info endpoint](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L252-L273) return that field to authorized staff. Registrar event formatting consumes this same service but [format_doctor_info_for_notification](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L303-L330) does not currently print a phone; **do not claim a visible phone omission in all registrar alerts**. This is a wrong contact-data source in the shared doctor info service.

**Dedup:** Group71 `NOTIFYREGPHONEPROFILE-BE-001` covers `GET /registrar/notifications/registrars` showing **registrar's** phone from User.phone. This is an **independent DoctorInfoService code path and doctor contact API response**, so neither fixing only the registrar list nor only the doctor service fixes both. Both should use approved role visibility policy.

**Synthetic RED — NOT RUN:** create synthetic User(role=Doctor,profile=UserProfile(phone="+998900000000")), Doctor(user_id=User.id, active=True); `get_doctor_full_info(Doctor.id)["phone"]` and authorized `GET /doctor-info/doctors/{id}` must reflect approved profile source. Test absent profile returns None; verify restricted role exposure policy, no real clinician information.

### NOTIFYDOCDEPARTMENTFK-BE-001 — doctor department label ignores actual Department foreign key

**Evidence:** [Doctor model lines 114–150](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/clinic.py#L114-L150) has `department_id: FK(departments.id)` and `department: relationship(Department)`. [Department model lines 30–60](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/models/department.py#L30-L60) stores authoritative `key`, `name_ru`, `name_uz`, `active`. Yet [DoctorInfoService.get_doctor_full_info lines 63–82](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L63-L82) calls `get_department_info(doctor.specialty)` and sets returned `"department"` to that **synthesized specialty name** rather than mapped Doctor.department.name_ru; its declared [DoctorInfoResponse.department_id](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L19-L32) also remains None because the dictionary never supplies department_id. If a clinic has a configured `Department(id=40,key="special_echo",name_ru="ЭхоКГ")` and an associated Doctor.specialty="cardiology" with Doctor.department_id=40, the API reports generic `"Кардиология"` and null department_id instead of the actually linked department. [RegistrarNotificationService.notify_new_appointment and notify_price_change](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L207-L230) consume the shared doctor info for display, so source mismatches can mislabel department in rendered messages **if the relevant source event path is repaired/used**.

**Caveat/dedup:** This is the *specific doctor's mapped department identity*, not group75 `NOTIFYDOCDEPTSOURCE-BE-001` (which builds the all-departments catalog from Doctor.specialty), nor group75 `NOTIFYDOCDYNDEPT-BE-001` (which filters doctors by hardcoded department_name). A Doctor can be assigned to a specialty-aligned Department and then the computed label may be coincidentally correct, but that does not repair mismatch for distinct valid configured departments. Some workflows may prefer specialty as fallback when Doctor.department_id is NULL; preserve that explicitly rather than claiming all specialty rendering is wrong.

**Synthetic RED — NOT RUN:** seed fake Department(id=40,name_ru="ЭхоКГ"), Doctor(specialty="cardiology",department_id=40), linked User; assert `get_doctor_full_info` uses actual department name and id when assigned; test doctor with NULL department_id receives clearly flagged specialty fallback. Ensure role/locale policy and no real user data. Test registrar display only with mocked sender, without live notifications.

### NOTIFYDOCDBFAILMASK-BE-001 — failed Doctor query yields fake healthy response semantics

**Evidence:** [DoctorInfoService.get_doctor_full_info lines 28–101](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L28-L101) encloses query and relation reads in a broad `try/except Exception`. On any SQLAlchemy error such as synthetic connection failure, its catch [lines 87–101](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/doctor_info_service.py#L87-L101) logs, then returns a *schema-shaped* dict with same input id, fictitious `full_name="Врач #ID"`, `is_active=False`, and no error status; the caller cannot distinguish a DB failure from a missing real doctor based on return value. [GET /doctor-info/doctors/{doctor_id} lines 75–92](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L75-L92) does not know this is an error and constructs `DoctorInfoResponse` returning HTTP 200. [GET /doctor-info/doctors/{id}/formatted-info](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/doctor_info.py#L252-L273) similarly publishes `raw_info` as if successful. This can hide a DB outage from staff-facing health diagnostics and consumers and misrepresent an existing clinician as absent/inactive.

**Distinctness:** Group74 `NOTIFYDOCUNKNOWN-BE-001` describes the explicit **not found** branch `if not doctor` returning fake 200. This item is the separate **unexpected exception** branch where a database/ORM runtime failure masquerades as valid data. A remedy can keep a deliberately lenient display fallback for registrar messages, but strict HTTP route should distinguish infrastructure failures (e.g. sanitized 503/500) from genuinely missing records (404) and avoid raw DB exception text in client response. No actual database outage occurred during review.

**Synthetic RED — NOT RUN:** mock a Session query raising synthetic SQLAlchemy OperationalError before returning Doctor; assert strict API `GET /doctor-info/doctors/42` does not return 200 with fictitious doctor. Mock a failed lazy relation read independently, assert same policy. Test actual absent doctor separate from DB failure and validated existing inactive doctor. Ensure logs do not include patient information.

### NOTIFYREGALERTPRIO-BE-001 — admin priority input cannot drive canonical urgency

**Evidence:** [SystemAlertRequest lines 64–71](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L64-L71) accepts `priority: str` (e.g. `"normal"`/`"high"`/`"urgent"`/`"critical"` per analogous NotificationRequest), and [POST /registrar/notifications/system-alert lines 277–293](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/registrar_notifications.py#L277-L293) passes it to [notify_system_alert lines 491–549](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L491-L549). The service uses `priority` only to build a `formatted_message` local string and to write `metadata.priority`; it then discards the formatted string and calls [`_record_registrar_canonical_event`, lines 57–88](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/registrar_notification_service.py#L57-L88) which has **no priority/severity arguments**. [Canonical send_registrar_event_notification lines 285–349](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notifications_pkg/_canonical.py#L285-L349) hardcodes `severity="info", priority="high"` for every `registrar_system_alert`; only `security_alert` or `billing_alert` aliases become `severity="critical",priority="urgent"` based on **event type**, not administrator's requested priority. Thus `SystemAlertRequest(alert_type="maintenance", priority="urgent", message="synthetic")` is persisted with **high/info** in canonical event despite metadata saying urgent.

**Real policy consequence:** [NotificationPlatformService._is_critical_breakthrough_event, lines 135–161](https://github.com/drsapaev/final/blob/94d140f837a0204212e8e6b20f35ab4d3b84546d/backend/app/services/notification_platform/_policy.py#L135-L161) treats normalized priority `urgent` as a critical breakthrough condition, while `_should_broadcast_realtime` uses this condition before quiet-hour and other settings checks. Losing urgent priority for generic system alerts can change their realtime eligibility, not just badge color. Product policy may intentionally forbid admin override for some categories; if so the request schema/UI must not promise it and needs documented validation. Do not claim a security alert is downgraded: `security_alert` and billing aliases already force urgent by event type. The prior `NOTIFYPRIORITYDROP-BE-001` concerns other canonical notification pathways; this ID is for **registrar system-alert-specific request → canonical helper → policy** wiring and does not re-count unrelated routes.

**Synthetic RED — NOT RUN:** with synthetic Admin caller and mocked platform recorder, post `/registrar/notifications/system-alert` using `alert_type="maintenance"`, `priority="normal"` and `"urgent"`; assert canonical `severity/priority` match explicit allowed policy and frontend contract (or invalid request rejected 422). Include `alert_type="security_alert"`/billing and verify mandatory critical override unaffected. Test correct quiet-hours behavior only with synthetic recipients/settings, no real broadcast and do not unilaterally bypass recipient opt-outs.
